> Devotion is that bridge which connects an individual with the Cosmic Consciousness... Before crossing the bridge of devotion, one feels, "You are that Supreme Consciousness." But while crossing the bridge, one feels, "I am That."

> That very irresistible aspiration which leads humans towards their goal with tremendous speed, also builds all sorts of social structures and generates newer social consciousness. With all sincerity in thought, words and deeds you have to build that aspiration... you have to awaken the cognitive faculty latent in the innermost golden cavity of every microcosmic mind. For, you have to work to bring out the well-being of every entity... no benevolent individual can neglect anyone.
>
> — Shrii Shrii Anandamurtiji

> Oh yes, I remember one thing, Your true devotees have stolen away Your mind.
> Lord, I offer my mind to You. Please grace me by Your acceptance."

# Foreword

He is infinite. His words and His deeds are also infinite. He is a mystery. Everything He said or did was a mystery; hence the story of His life is also mystery. He came to this earth as a simple visitor, guided as a common man. Yet in the guise of a common man He accomplished an unfathomable task. As a simple government servant and family man He was able to fulfill all His worldly duties in exemplary fashion and at the same time to build up an international organization to give service and spiritual balm to the entire planet. His every action and every word was a teaching, not only for His followers but for everyone who came in His contact. How can this mystery be explained? It is a difficult task indeed!

The author, Acarya *Krpananda* Avadhuta spends twenty-seven years with Him. He was a first-hand witness to the great mystery of His life. The author is not a professional writer but what he lacks in choreography of writing he makes up with his great sincerity and direct experience. I went through His book and found that he has managed to capture all of Shrii Shrii Anandamurtijii's activities within the pages of a single book, illustrating His points with small but enjoyable and instructive stories. It is like managing to put all the water of the ocean into a small container. I advise everyone to read it and learn more about the greatness of our master, Shrii Shrii *Anandamurtijii*.

Ac. Shraddhananda Avdt
4/1/2001

-- Acarya Shraddhananda Avadhuta
Purodha Pramukh,
Ananda Marga Pracaraka Samgha

## Foreword

This long-awaited first volume from Ac Krpananda Avt offers its readers a unique and charming glimpse into the life and teachings of his master, Shrii Shrii Anandamurtijii. The author uses the stories and small episodes from the master's life as medium to convey His spiritual genius and unparalleled contributions to human society in a way that is as easy and joyful to read as it is deeply thought-provoking. Every aspect of the master's life is unveiled—His magnetic personality and miraculous powers, His profound spiritual and social philosophy, His development of the organization, His literary contributions and so on - all bound together by the disciple's unbounded love for his guru. To sum up, this book is an important and inspiring resource for anyone who wishes to know more about the foremost spiritual and social figure of the modern age.

<p align="right">-Devashish</p>

## Remembrance

To beloved Brothers/Sisters who gave their lives for the greater cause of His mission

In 1979, during the Hyderabad DMC in the southern part of India, Baba told a story on His field walk - "A temple was being built, there were many stones lying on the ground and one of them was thinking, if I placed at the top of the temple, people will see me. Another stone was thinking, 'If I placed in front of the idol, people will worship me and all would bow their head before me.' When that temple was completed, the stones had been placed according to their desires, Baba said "I love both those stones but I love more the stones which have made the foundation of the temple."

Thus Dada's, Didi's, Brothers and Sisters are the foundation of Ananda *Marga* and so, from my heart, I make an offering for the followers who sacrificed their lives for the cause to fulfill my master's mission.

On 5th of March, 1967 Ac Abhedananda Avt, Ac Saccidananda Avt, Ac Bharat Brc, Ac Prabhash Brc, and Ac Avadhesh Brc sacrificed their life at Ananda *Nagar*.

On 5th of March, 1969 Brother Ravi Sarkar at Coochbehar,

On April, 1969 Bhagawat *Panday* (Ghazipur) near Meerut was killed by communist,

On 5th April, 1973 Ac Divyananda Avt self immolated at Patna,

On April 24, 1973 Ac Dineshvarananda Avt at Delhi,

December 11, 1973 Ac *Atulananda* at Bankipur central jail, Patna Self immolated,

Feb. 4, 1974 Didi Uma Brc. (Germany),

Feb. 4, 1974,-Dada *Lokesh* (Germany),

On Feb. 17, 1977 Dada *Gagan* Deo, (Dallas, Texas),

On June 14, 1978 -Didi Asitima (Manila),
October 2, 1978-Brahmacarini Shanti Ac. (Geneva).
On 30th of April, 1982 at Calcutta in broad day light following people were killed by communist activists-
Avtk Ananda Praceta Ac, Ac Adhishivananda Avt,
Ac Bhuveshvarananda Avt, Ac Kripashivananda Avt,
Ac Artasevananda Avt, Ac Subashwarananda Avt,
Ac Venketashwarananda Avt, Ac Kamaleshananda Avt,
Ac Girishananda Avt, Ac Prashzivananda Avt,
Ac Shomnath Brc, Ac Mukul Brc,
Ac Birendra Brc, Ac Subrat Brc,
Ac Dhruvngati Brc, Ac Jaganzohan Brc,
Sri Rani Raghuvnr at Jaipur,
Ac Parasivananda Avt at Anandanagar,

April 2, 1990 Ac Ashimananda Avt at Anandanagar,
1977 Ac Tyageshvarananda Avt at Bhagalpur jail,
1977 Ac Girijananda Avt (GS) by car accident,

# Introduction

> *Asitagirisamamsya't kajjalam' sindhupa'tre;*
> *Surataruvara sha'kha' Nishitapatrnnzurvii,*
> *Likhati yadi grhiitva Sa'rada' sarvaka'lam;*
> *tava gun'a'na'miisha pa'ram'nn yati.*

"Even the goddess of wisdom, using the Himalayas for an ink tablet, the ocean for a container, a huge tree-branch as a pen, the entire surface of the earth for her paper, and writing for infinite time, cannot begin to describe Parama *Purusa*."

One day, some years ago, an intense desire came in the minds of some devotees that a biography of Baba should be written, as a precious memorial for the future generations. So they sat down and tried to write a story of His life but it was impossible. How could their tiny minds encompass the vast expanse of His existence? How could they relate and interpret all the countless miracles He performs in the lives of millions of His devotees each day, every moment? How could they express the glory of master's love for His children?

Eventually they went to Baba. "Baba, it is impossible for us to write the complete story of Your life. There is only one Entity on this earth that is capable of doing that, and that is You, Yourself. Please Baba do write Your autobiography for us."

At first He refused, protesting that He had no time for such things. But again and again they requested Him. The Lord (master) can never refuse His devotee, so after long persistence, He agreed. Those sadhakas were overjoyed, and anxiously awaited for the promised work. The next day at Darshan, (Baba's discourse) Baba suddenly said, "Well I have finished writing my story, and will you want to see it."

All were amazed. How could He have written it so fast?

Even knowing the tremendous speed at which He works they were still astonished that the autobiography was finished so soon. "Yes, yes, Baba," they said, "please give it to us!"

Then He handed over to one of them a single piece of paper. They were even more dumbfounded. How was it possible that this great work could be written on a single sheet of paper? They breathlessly saw the piece of paper written only three lines - "I was mystery, I am mystery, and I shall ever remain mystery."

"In this strange world many people are like wheelbarrows - no good unless pushed. Some are like trailers - they have to be pulled. Some are like canoes - they have to be paddled. Some are like kites - if you don't keep a string on them they fly away. Some are like footballs - you cannot tell which direction they will bounce next. Others are like balloons - full of air and ready to blow up. And yet others are like neon lights - they keep going on and off. Very few are like a good watch - open face, pure gold and quietly busy. This is how master is."

He started His organization on January 9, 1955. Until 1962, the organization was based on family margiis. In 1962, Baba started to train Monks and Nuns. At the very beginning, many trainees joined the organization; they were of different cultures, languages and different food habits. Some were more qualified than others; some were poor and some rich. Some were quite active while others were like the wheelbarrow - needing to be pushed, some were like trailers, others like canoes, kites, footballs, balloons and yet others like the neon light.

Naturally, such diversity causes problems. To help and solve all problems, Baba started a reporting system called RDS. RDS means Review, Defect and Solution. Due to this system or work pressures, workers become more active; they removed their dullness.

One day, during the reporting session, Baba related a story, saying that He was telling the story as He was feeling active because of the treatment of Dr. *Ranjan*. "Once there was a noted doctor in Berlin, Germany, in the same city was a well-to-do man. Physically, he was well but because he did not do physical work, he became dull. This dullness causes him to feel diseased in his body. He went to many doctors but all told him that they could find nothing physically wrong with him. He became hopeless. Finally, he came to Dr. Ranjan. *Dr. Ranjan* was also a psychologist. First he examined the person, and then he inquired about all his activities. The doctor found that the man was physically well but had become dull. Dr. *Ranjan* finally told the man that he can remove his disease. At the side of the dispensary, Dr. Ranjan had kept a steam room. He locked the man in this and after a short while, the room becomes very hot. Soon the man was jumping around and calling for Dr. *Ranjan* to let him out. The doctor purposely kept the room locked and the man was soon sweating all over his body. Finally, after half an hour the doctor let the man come out and he told the doctor he feels much better. The doctor said that because he did no physical activity and did not sweat, his body had become diseased."

Baba then said, "My organization is like Dr. *Ranjan's* treatment that removes the dullness and makes active."

One day during field walk, one senior margii said to Baba, "Baba, these Dada's and *Didi's* have limited energy yet you are always giving them a lot of work to do. How they will accomplish when they have the capacity to carry only 50Lbs and you give 200Lbs?"

Baba smiled and replied, 'Yes, I know these boys and girls cannot carry so much weight even though I am giving so much pressure. Do you know why I am doing that? With the pressure of different type of work, they give at least 50% output and they also keep their mind always engaged with

good thoughts."

Here is another example where master push the workers and they become responsible as well as active.

A new Dndn came for reporting. He was posted in district level and was supposed to open a school. But hard as he tried, he could not manage it. Baba was very serious and He said, "This boy is not fit for district level responsibility, he is fit for sectorial level; transfer him to sectorial level."

Everybody was surprised; if he wasn't fit for district level, how could he handle sectorial level?

In the evening, Baba said, "You know, this boy (the new *Dada*) can do better supervisory work than executive work, so I changed his posting. We saw in course of time that Dada really did successful work.

We are also like wheelbarrows, trailers or canoes due to our laziness, but my master gave such pressure that we got a lot out of nothing. 1969 was a seminar year. He gave a slogan - seminars, more and more seminars, and village level seminars.

During reporting sessions, Baba was taking reports from several workers. Turn of one Dada came, and Baba said, "When are you going to cover every village of this world with seminars?"

Dada replied, "In two months."

"What?" Baba said angrily.

In one month Baba."

Again Baba scolded him. *Dada* brought the time down to fifteen days and Baba still wasn't satisfied. Then *Dada* reduced the time to one week, then two days. Finally, Baba extracted from him that he would do the work in twelve hours. Of course, *Dada* wasn't able to do even percent of what he had promised. In next reporting session, Baba was in a different mood, smiling and very blissful. He started talking about laziness. He said there are three kinds of laziness-physical, mental, and spiritual. Physical laziness is when we want to do some thing but we put it off. Spiritual laziness is when we postpone our lessons until later. Mental laziness, Baba said, is "a lack of proper planning."

For example, some one has to catch a flight at 7 a.m. He gets up at 5 a.m., takes his bath and performs Sadhana, and leaves for the airport in a rush forgetting one of his bags. Finally, he arrives at the airport and misses his flight. My master went on, "But I will get up at 3.20 a.m., finish my spiritual practice and leave for the airport at 5 a.m.: I wouldn't miss either my bag or my flight." My master smiled at one Dada who was always missing trains or flights.

"I know it is not possible to cover all the villages in one day, or month, or even one year. It may not even be possible in twenty years. But I give pressure and scold in order to make your mind ready; for if your mind is 100% positive, then it might be possible to realize this work in less than twenty years. I scold you to remove your mental laziness."

There is one example of how Baba pushes us sometimes. During reporting time, Baba was in His presiding role - grave and silent. One day, during one session, Baba became very annoyed with one *Dada*. When Baba asked for his report, he just looked out the window and didn't answer. "What are you looking at the sky?" Baba asked. "What are you thinking?" Baba was really furious, "This boy didn't do any work for the last three months. He didn't even try." Baba said that if you take the target to stand first in your class, then at least you will make it to stand second. If your target is to be second then you will be able to stand third. If your target is third then you will be failed. You should always aim for higher than first then you will always be first. What you have to do tomorrow, try to finish it today, and what you have to do today, do it right now. Then you can accomplish a lot in a short span of your life. Baba taught us in this way and we are

following and progressing day by day.

We are like a kite. To control all the workers, to look the progress of all workers and Mnrgiis, He made a system of reporting. When the reporting system actually started we cannot say, but when I inquired of senior Mnrgiis, they told me that when they were going anywhere in beginning, they were informing Him and when they return were also telling about their progress. The organization was in full swing up to 1968. When seminar year started in 1969, Baba made a program that village workers would give reports to *panchayat*, *panchayat* will report to block, block to the district, district to diocese, diocese to region, region to sector, and sector would then report to central. In this way, no worker could escape giving progress reports. Every worker was making a tour program - one for that own office and the rest (four or five) were given to immediate authority. That tour program would go to central. So the central authority knows the movements of all workers. My master, Anandamurtii, was keeping touch with all the workers and knew their progress through these reports.

Through the reporting system, we realized the following benefits from my master - He developed a new regime for working fast. He taught the social, economic and spiritual philosophy of *Ananda Marga* and philosophies of the world as well. He created new leaders in the Organization. He taught how to work independently. When He posted any worker, in any part of the world, they were nicely instructed by Him to the social, economic and political aspects of the area and particularly about the language.

Baba always utilized His time to the maximum. Once, during the reporting session, He said "When I am taking a bath and putting water on my head, I am not talking because water will go in my mouth; therefore, I am not giving any instruction to any one. Other times, I am giving instructions or showing work. During eating time He used to explain about the food; its history, and origin. During field walk, He spoke according to the worker or Mnrgiis; when He was on tour, He would tell all the historical and economic background of the area. Once during His tour (65 days on road) He gave *Prabhat Samgiita*.

When He was in the nursing home, the doctor told Him He needed complete rest and He should not talk or take reports from the workers. Baba did not accept and He kept himself even more engaged. One man was not able to assist Him. So His P.A., General Secretary and global secretaries were always running behind Him. As He always kept Him busy, so He made all the workers to be. His life was like a golden watch - busy 24 hours a day.

In 36 years of His organization work not even most trusted monk and people perceived to be close to Him, could never predict his next move. What a mystery He was! It is surprising that in 36 years of His organizational *liila* many people mingled to be very close to Him whence was always beyond His physical touch. He was untouchable through the worldly modes of acquaintances. The only things that brought people closer to Him and maintained that bond was the love for Him and surrender at His feet.

The master planner, the cosmic conductor remained a mystery all His life and accentuated the mystery by His departure in a way no one could imagine.

He departed when He was spotless, when the mission had startled the imagination of the intelligent people of the world. He departed when the mission had established its skeleton all over the world. He departed when people had just started to inquire about the mystery man of Ananda Marga. He did not leave us in debt. He has left us with the wealth of His vision translated into Socio-economic theory, spiritual practices, dances, medicine, *Prabhat Samgiita*, secrets of

microvita and an insatiable yearning in all of us for the supreme. He found us worthy of His mission. He charged us to be with Him in this short span of 36 years. He gave His health, His sleep, His comforts, His family and His life for us to give us confidence, to give us His vision and His concern for the suffering humanity; His love to fill the vacuum in our lives.

To speak, to teach, to give advice, to make a rule, to preach to others is easy but in the practical social life, it is very difficult for anyone, except in the case of my master, to put into action what they preach. His life was a mirror; the coming generation will learn from His multicolored personality.

# Statement

At an early age in *Ananda Marga*, during a field walk, at Jamalpur, my master told us that when we had a dream at night we should write down then and there, don't wait for the morning otherwise you will forget. Therefore, before going to sleep, we should have pen and paper beside our pillow.

I was a teenager then, and I paid respect to Baba as a Father and as a guardian, not as a Guru. When He told me that I must write down my dreams, I started laughing. Baba said, "Why are you laughing?" I replied that nobody in the world wrote their dreams and I never took this matter seriously. But later, I realized that Baba never said anything without meaning. Whatever thoughts came to my mind was also a part of dream. Now, I am always keeping pen and paper and whatever thoughts or stories comes to my mind, I pen down in my diary or on paper. A day comes when these will be transformed into a book.

During reporting time, our senior Dada was always giving classes about the organization or about philosophy. Our senior learned *Acarya Vijayananda Avadhuta* was always inspiring all of us to write things about our master. So why are you not writing "With My Master"? Due to this inspiration, I wrote the book "With My Master" in my diary. In 1989, I reached Manila sector. With the help of respected, learned and devotee, Brother Ac. A. Avadhuta and Ac. Prasiidananda Avadhuta, in 1990 I completed my book "With My Master" and sent the manuscript to Cebu printing press (Philippines). One evening after meditating in the Sectorial office at Manila, we were telling the stories of Baba. Dada asked, "Why are you not writing regarding different expressions of *Anandamurtijii*? You were with Him for a long time." This thought struck in my mind and inspired me, so I began to search the matter as well as a proper name. Finally I decided I would write on "My Master - An Example."

When you write any story, it needs to have proper contents. In April 1992, I was traveling from Darasalam (Tanzania), Africa to Lusaka. This journey was for two days and two nights. In the morning of first very day, after meditation, I was looking towards the window; the sun was rising and a beautiful breeze was coming from the forest and entering inside the compartment. Sometimes I was meditating with open eyes, next time I was thinking about my book, often I was watching at beauty of the Nature (mother) and was passing my time. Beauty of the Nature and my master *Anandamurtiji*! I saw different wild animals moving freely in the forest and at times I forget that I was in the train. At such moments, the contents of the book came into my mind and in short time I had written my plan for the contents for this book. This was the one of the happiest day of my life. I had the contents of the book. Afterwards, I recorded my thoughts for each chapter. I decorated other parts that I found from the research of different books often in my life.

Cause behind writing a book on My Master - Meditation needs *Manana* (thinking about God) *Shravana* (listening to the story of the supreme) and Nididhynsana (union with the supreme). These are the things which are essential for the development in spiritual life. One day, when I was in Ananda *nager*, one of my friends asked me why I was worried about writing book on "my master". I simply replied that when I wrote anything about Him, the thinking and listening are common and as a result of these two I got union with Him. This is the way I am singing the song of my master. I completed the book in prose form. I wrote it for my individual happiness. I was 30 years with my master; I am showered with His personal love and affection. He has given cream (essence) to me in the form of devotion. My master is my personal property and that devotional cream is my personal wealth; but this book is like buttermilk. In this physical world, the physical things are Limited the more you will distribute lesser it will be. In the case of devotion, intuition or psychic wealth, before any distribution, first of all distributor falls in devotion. Whenever I tell a Baba's story, the audience gets pleasure, but before that I go in trance in devotion.

What will the reader will get from this book? - Cooperation - Actually I am not a professional writer or thinker; I am just one gardener in the garden to collect good and nice smelling flowers from different plants to make one good garland for Him. This book is like that; I am not author of a book, He is all in all. I compiled the work, on behalf of Him. So many stories from different sources and classes I have come to know, these all have come to help me.

Ac. A. is the major inspiration for this book; he pushed me to narrate the story of my master in physical form. Brother Pavitra *Kumar* (LFT) helped with typing work in Nairobi (Kenya). When I came to Jamaica, West Indies, *Mr.* Rnkesh (India) helped with some typing and he also translated some of my Hindi speeches into English and Brother Wade Chandler (*Vinaya*) of Barbados, not only helped with typing, but he also edited. For editing *Rajesh* and layout *Amod* from India has also helped me. I give lot of thanks to all of them. I also give thanks to the island of Kingston, Jamaica, where I made up my mind to complete this big task.

In first chapter. I have tried to explain the meaning of master. Whatever information I do have about the word master I want to say.

Today, most parts of the world are politically free, but economically still slave of the some powerful country. When a country is a slave, their controller (master) tortures them. If you go through the history of slavery, you will witness the torture and pain towards body and mind, and then to the race enslaved. Such people hate the word "Master." It is natural but here sense is not as exploiter, but as the master who controls the nucleus of the universe, that is the Supreme Father. He is the only master - no other entity can be.

One day at Calcutta, during reporting session, one wrong thought entered in the mind of one worker. When master touched His body the thought wave suddenly changed. After reporting the worker told me his experiences. There are thousands of stories that how Baba controlled the mental thoughts of all during His lifetime.

During the early days of *Ananda Marga* (1960's), one day a police officer came to Jamalpur *Ashram*. During personal contact, Baba told him to sit on His lap. The police officer, a big man, was thinking Baba is very short and thin, if I sit on His lap it will cause pain, therefore he sat with the support of his leg. Baba told him to sit with his full weight. When the policeman devotee did so, he felt that Baba had suddenly become very big and strong and policeman had become light like a baby.

It was a natural habit of my master to give punishment to one who had made mistake. He always kept a cane for the devotees. Once, a senior *Dada* asked Baba, "Why are you beating us?" Baba replied, "In this way I am removing the bad *Karma* from your mind and body." Sometimes He would ask the devotee whether they wanted punishment from Him or Nature. Naturally the devotees always choose Baba. Some devotees joked and gave Baba's stick a name - *Sudharam Singh* or *Dukhaharan* means that which reforms or removes the pain. One day He also told that He did not beat everybody. Only those who are simple and mine, I punish. Once, there was phase of personal contacts and so many people got it. After completion Baba went bathroom, when He returned, there was blood on His leg; P.A. inquired about the blood. Baba told him that He was wounded in the bathroom and further explained that during personal contact one boy was going to meet a dangerous accident and He took the *samsakara* (reactive momenta) of that boy on Him and saved the boy from a sever accident. "When I beat the boy I took His result of bad action on myself." There are several stories about His administration.

My Master is like a wood apple, very strong shell outside and inside very soft and compassionate.

In 1990, when I was in Bombay (India), a group of *Ananda Margiis* were saying that someone should write a book that would touch all the aspects of Baba's life so that common people should come to know *Anandamurtii* and His mission in a more casual manner. Baba's philosophy is like a vast ocean, His personality like the sky and His organization like a net. When I heard these discussions I kept mum and was thinking again and again that I could solve their problem. I don't know if this book will solve this problem or not, but in the future. If my brothers and sisters *Dadas* and *Didis* who had been with Baba for a long time are very attached and has working hard, if they will inspire, guide me and arrange more matter then I will be able to solve this purpose.

About the chapter of "Lord Shiva and Lord *Krshna*" - What is the relationship of these two great personalities Lord *Shiva* and Lord *Krshna*? According to *Ananda Marga* philosophy, *Ananda Marga* does not believe the incarnation of God. God incarnated this creation. *Ananda Marga* says, between form (*Saguna*) and formless (Nirguna) there is *Taraka Brahma*. On this earth Lord Sada Shiva came 7000 years ago as the first *Taraka Brahma* and Lord *Krshna* came 3500 years ago as the second. *Anandamurtii* came as the third *Taraka Brahma* on the earth; therefore I felt that I must say something regarding these two personalities in my book. When you read the philosophy or practice the techniques of meditation, my master always mentioned the names of these two personalities and He also wrote a book on Lord Shiva and Lord *Krshna*. I have mentioned their contribution briefly for the world so that those who read will know their contributions.

# I

## MY MYSTERIOUS MASTER

> "You infuse sweetness into the human heart its affection, its love, its great sacrifice. By the deftness of Your masterful stroke you exalt us to the status of Gods."

![My Master Shrii Shrii Anandamurtijii](image)

*My Master Shrii Shrii Anandamurtijii*

***

# MASTER (Definition)

"My master is a loving father, divine guru, reliable friend, strict guardian and a master Organizer. He came, He taught, He punished and He loved. He is always with us."

*The meaning of Master (according to the Oxford Dictionary):*
i. As a noun: - A man who has control over people or things, The captain of a merchant ship, The male, head of a household, The male owner of a dog etc, A male teacher, A school master, A holder of a university degree awarded for study beyond the level of a bachelors degree; Master of arts or science, A masters degree or its holder, A respected teacher, A person with a very great skill, a great artist, A chess player of proven ability at international level, A document or film of record etc, from which a series of copies is made, A title prefixed to the name of a boy who is not yet old enough to be called Mr.
ii. As verb: - To overcome, to bring under control, to acquire knowledge or skill in.

In the study of any subject, a proper teacher is vitally essential; otherwise the student may easily acquire bad habits and never master in his study. In the deep and subtle study of yoga, a proper teacher is a must. By the practice of yoga, we are changing every cell of our body, way of breathing, the flow of vital energy inside our body, manner of thinking, personality and our spirit.

If we do not have the proper teacher, then success in this science is impossible, a great harm may result. There are countless stories of disciples who practiced yoga, reading books or by self; they fall sick and even mentally disturbed. A true

teacher gives correct times and imparts them in such a way that it can be practiced safely, easily and joyously.

Yoga teachers are higher and greater than that. In Sanskrit He is called "Guru" which means, "One who removes the darkness from our mind". The yoga master's job is more profound and mysterious than the job of a history teacher, or a driving teacher. He does not merely train the disciple in the performance of deep science, but also invisibly and mysteriously transfers His own spiritual elevation and spiritual energy to His disciple. In this way the disciple can progress much faster, and nourished and elevated. With the help of the power of the guru they can easily attain the highest state of peace and bliss.

For thousands of years, the Indian yogis have been deeply revering their guru, their spiritual master as they know that the guru holds the key to liberation. Many books and scriptures have been written about the guru. They say "The sight of the guru is difficult to obtain in all the worlds; only by the merits of previous births it obtained, not otherwise."

"When one is ready to receive the guru's message, when one has developed enough through many life times, to be able to understand the spirit of His teachings, to live in tune with His liberated and liberating consciousness, when the seeker is thus prepared by his past and present evolution for the decisive step, he either is taken by circumstances to where the guru is, or the guru himself comes to him."

"There is no truth greater than the Guru."

"When the perfect teacher gives himself to the disciple, then the disciple gets liberation; there is no further birth for him."

"Form of the guru is the root of meditation, feet of the guru is the root of worship, every word of the guru is the root of the mantra and the grace of the guru is the root of all liberation"

"The Guru is one and that is Brahma (Supreme Consciousness), the Guru of the universe. The physical form is not the Guru, but the supreme consciousness behind the physical form is the Guru. Therefore the Supreme Consciousness alone is the Guru."

"All kind of the repetitions of mantras, fasting, austerities and other spiritual disciplines practiced for ages, or for millions of births, bear fruit the moment the Guru is pleased."

"I pay my salutations to the divine guru who by the application of the ointment of knowledge, opens the eyes of one self blinded by the darkness of ignorance."

"The Guru is not different from the self or consciousness. This is beyond doubt, truth, and the absolute truth. Hence a wise man must seek his Guru."

In Ananda Marga yoga, we also have a deep love and respect for our spiritual master (Baba). Baba is not an ordinary master but a Sadaguru. The one who has elevated his mind to the highest state of consciousness and who also can by his touch, look or merely by thought elevates others' minds to that sublime state is called Guru or Mahasambhuti. His name in Sanskrit is Shrii Shrii Anandamurtijii which means "The embodiment of bliss;" a blissful entity, filled with bliss that anyone who comes near feels waves of internal peace and ecstasy in mind and heart.

Like other teachers who learn from his master, Baba had no teacher. He was born with perfection, He was realized, and started teaching others the deep practices of yoga meditation since His childhood. We call Him Baba, which means "beloved father" because He guides us with tenderness and love like a father. At present there are millions of disciples all over the globe. We have felt lives have been transformed by His teachings, especially by His love and care. He has only one desire; that we should all progress rapidly to perfection, and inspire others around too. With joined hand march onto the path of truth, march onto the path of bliss (Ananda Marga). We can soon establish a better human society, a world family where all will live gladly and share love and joy.

In this world there are two type of knowledge, one is called

"*Parajinana*" (the knowledge of subjectivity) and the second one is called "*Apara jinana*" (knowledge of the objectivity), means knowledge of the external world and "*Para jinana*" means the knowledge of the internal world (concerning God, soul and instincts). So regarding *Aparajianana*, those who give knowledge are called teachers and those give *Parajianana*; intuitional knowledge is called Guru. There are three types of Guru in this objective world. There are three types of doctors also. One is called *Adham*. *Adham* means the lower category of doctors. They only give medicines to the patients but didn't asks the condition. The second type of doctor is called *Madhyama* (middle) doctor. They ask the patients their condition, but they don't cure. The third type of doctor (*uttam*) is the best doctor, if the patient will not take the medicine they will force to take, will take care and finally cure the disease, are the best physicians, are the best doctors. Similarly there are three categories of Guru: *Adham* (lower type of Guru) they will initiate and visit disciple's houses once, twice or thrice in a year, receive something from their disciple and go back. They never inspire him for physical or spiritual upliftment. This is lower type of guru. The second one is of medium type, (*madhyam* Guru). They will teach but they will not take care of their attainment. Last one is Uttam, the first category of Guru, will care properly, if there is no progress they will create encouraging spiritual environment and finally lead to the liberation.

He has formulated a master plan to solve all the problems of the world:

A. Socio-economic policy (PROUT), B. Environmental (Neo-Humanism), C. Individual mental problems (Yoga and Ayurvedic medicines); D. Socio-psychic (spiritual techniques and scientific spiritual philosophy), E. A formation of dynamic organization to implement His master plan.

### Complete in Himself

My Master was not merely a philosopher, but a philosophy - Philosopher originates from the Latin word "philo" which means love and "sophs" means wisdom, so philosopher means love for wisdom/knowledge. Philosophy tries to bind Paramapurusa by language. But a total knowledge of Mundane, Supramundane and Spiritual science is not capable of binding Baba. My Master is beyond the Mundane, Supramundane and institutional science. He already has all knowledge. He is knowledge. Therefore He is not a Philosopher, but rather all philosophies are within Him.

In 1964, one devotee asked Baba "Is there any philosophy except Ananda Marga?" Baba replied that in His bag there were infinite philosophies. "I can change Ananda Marga Philosophy within a minute." So, He is not a philosopher but philosophy.

Not a historian but He is history - What is History?
1. A continuous methodical records of important events.
2. Events of the Past, concerning with persons or things.
3. An interesting or eventful past
4. The study of past events especially of human affairs.

Historian means, an expert in history, a writer of history, those who are studying history—"There are some who reads the history, there are some who writes the history and there are some one who makes the history". My master did not write or had gone through it, He made history, and His each expressions of life are a part of history.

Not an organizer but He Himself is an organization - According to Oxford American dictionary, the meaning of organizer is - to arrange in an orderly systematic way, to make arrangements for, to form (people into an association for common

purpose.

What is an organization? - Organizing, being organized, an organized body of people, an organized system. Before His great departure, my master told one devotee "I am not this physical body. This physical body is not me. You are in my heart and I am in your heart. If you want to see me, do my mission because I have merged myself with my (organization) mission." Any devotee can get His physical presence. Nothing is without, every thing is within. My master is not an organizer, but an organization.

My Master is not only Yogi but the very founder of Yoga - According to yoga, the meaning of yoga is "*Samyoga yogo ityukto jiivatmah paramatmanah.*" Yoga means unification of the unit mind and the supreme. We all know that one plus one is equal to two in mundane, but supramundane science one plus one is equal to one, and in the case of intuitional science, one plus one is equal to infinity.

In the psychic world, one plus one equals one, because two friends, i.e. two bodies share one kind. In the spiritual world, one plus one equals infinity because the unit mind is finite and the Supreme Mind is infinite. Through Sadhana, unit merges with the supreme and become one. So according to mathematics, the symbol - $\infty$ represents infinity. Those who practiced meditation like *Mahavira*, *Buddha* or any great souls. They came in the world and have done lot of practice. After 10, or 20 years of practices they became the Yogi, achieved perfection. They are yogis but in the case of my master, He was born with perfection. In very childhood there is a story that a tiger takes Him to the mountain and He goes to the *sadhakas* who use to practice meditation over there and guide and bless them for their success.

Who is Yogi? - A persons who meditates applying Intuitional techniques are yogis. According to the Dhyan lesson of Ananda marga, anybody doing meditation with the help of certain mantra, he/she will get *Samadhi*.

Not a musician but He Himself is a Music - What is music? According to the oxford dictionary - the art of arranging the sounds of voice (s) or instrument (s), or both, in pleasing sequence or combination, the sound (s) or composition (s) produced, a written or printed score for this, an pleasant sound or series of sounds such as a bird's song; is called music.

The artist who learns the art of music is musicians. According to my master's autobiography, He never went to any musical college in India, but when He was a student in *Vidyasagar* College (Calcutta University), He was given a job, of teaching music and dance. Baba had not gone anywhere for training or experience in classical dance or music, yet He was teaching.

On the 14th of September 1982, Baba started composing *Prabhat Samgiita* in Deoghar (Bihar) with His first song, "Bandhu he niye calo." He taught us this song with dance and all its correct way to sing and perform. Within 8 years, He composed 5018 songs in Sanskrit, Bengali, *Urdu*, Hindi, English, *Angika*, Magahi and Maithili. Once when He was on tour He would just stop the car and start giving *Prabhat Samgiita*. Another time when He was in a nursing home, the doctor told Him that He should take some rest and do not take reporting or talk with the workers, but instead He gave more than one hundred songs within a week. When the doctor asked Baba why He is exhausting His body and mind Baba replied, "When I am giving *Prabhat Samgiita* I never feel any exhaustion; it comes naturally."

He is not only scientist but is science - What is the subject of science? - Branch of knowledge requiring systematic study, method and discoveries. This whole cosmos is a mystery.

Mystery hidden behind *this cosmos* is science (Life, death, *soul*) who *am I*? Where do I go *after death*? Where *did* I come *from*? All these are science.

There are three types of science (1) mundane science, (chemistry, physics, biology, geology, etc.); (2) Supramundane science (clairvoyance and telepathy) and (3) Intuitional science (the science for the mind, soul and God).

He who is trying to discover reality from mystery is called scientist.

One day, in *Ranchi* (Bihar), 1969 the demonstration year, Baba asked one devotee, "Where were you 100 years ago?" The devotee, who was from the Philippines, could not give a proper reply as it was beyond his capacity. Finally Baba explained saying that He was fishing in a stream of Scotland. He knew everything of the cosmos, past, present and the future.

**Not only a doctor but a complete medical science** - The year 1969 was declared demonstration year by Baba. In *Ranchi*, (Bihar) during general darshan, Baba was present with 25 disciples. Baba asked one boy who was sitting near the door "Is there any pain in your chest?" That boy replied, "Yes, Baba. For the past six months there has been pain." Thereafter, Baba called him and touched His stick. He removed the pain. Thereafter, He said that this boy was suffering from TB but by the stick, the disease is removed and the pain is no more. In this way, Baba removed so many diseases from His disciples either through His stick or His finger directly.

**Not artist alone but is a master of art** - According to the Oxford American dictionary, the meaning of an artist is - 1. A person who produces art, 2. a person who does something with exceptional skill, 3. A professional entertainer. According to the same dictionary the meaning of art is: 1. the production of something beautiful, skill or ability in such work, 2. works such as painting or sculpture produced by skills.

This whole universe is within Him and this planet earth is also an art which is within Him.

**Not merely a linguist but He Himself is language** - According to the Oxford American dictionary, the meaning of a linguist is 1. a student of linguistics, 2. person who knows foreign languages well. According to the same dictionary, the meaning of language - 1. words and their use, 2. a system of words used in one or more countries, 3 a system of signs and symbols used for conveying information or in computer symbols used for conveying information or in computer programming, 4. a particular style of wording, 5. The vocabulary of a particular group of people.

In 198_, there was DMC at *Bhuja, Gujarat*, West India. During the reporting time He expressed english pronunciation in 24 styles of different countries. In 1973, at Patna, during His trial in session court, when the judge asked, "Which language should we use to record your statement?" my master replied, "I know more than two hundred languages of the world." The judge was stunned. Then my master said He would give His statement in english, but Cambridge style. The linguist knows only two or three languages, but my master knows all the languages of the world. He composed 5018 Prabhat Samgiit but He never wrote them. He just gave the song and sang them. He did not write any manuscript or essay; He just spoke and people took note of it.

## His Different Names

When my master was born, according to local tradition, His grandmother was feeding milk from a silver cup to Him by squeezing drops from cotton into His mouth. But in the cosmic mind of the baby, Baba thought 'Why are they feeding me like this, drop by drop? I am a baby and nature does not yet permit me to express myself." Instead He reached with His tiny hands, took the cup and drank all the milk! Baba's grandmother was full of surprise and amazement. She proclaimed "He is not a new baby; He is an old man (Burha)'. So she gave the name "*Burha*" meaning "old." So the first name of my master was "*Burha*."

### Arun

After a few days, so many pundits (Intellectuals) came and

they gave Him the name Arun because my master was born at 6.07 a.m. in the morning. At the time it was sunrise - *Aruna* is the name for the colour of the dawn. In this background of darkness and frustration, a ray of hope was dawned at the eastern horizon, in the remote village town of Jamalpur in east *Bihar*.

The middle class family of Sri *Laxminarayan* Sarkar received the blessing of the almighty; He got a son, who, He did not know, was going to win the heart of every one far and near all over the world.

The rising sun of the *Vaeshakhi purnima* of 1922 was the real "*Arunodaya*" because Arun was born that day almost simultaneously with the sunrise (6.07 a.m.).

### Prabhat Ranjan Sarkar

Arun was then renamed as Prabhat Rainjan only to strike harmony with the name of his new born cousin - named Chittaranjan - because He was born the same day when the great leader of *Bengal* had died.

*Prabhat* also means dawn but if the white colour- the combination of all the seven rainbow colours- when the sun becomes stronger after having of sunrise.

His second name is *Rainjan*. In India at that time there was one famous man named *Chittaranjan* Das. Baba's father agreed also to give the name. Rainjan means radiance.

The surname of Baba is Sarkar. This is the title of those who work in the government. So my master's name became Prabhat Rainjan Sarkar.

### Buboo

One year after Baba's birth, another son was born into the family. There was a problem for the mother to control two boys. Baba's mother used to call Him "Buboo," the allies of a kind of insect. Even when He was older hitherto they use to call Him "Buboo." If someone asked her "where is Prabhat da?" she would reply, "Buboo went outside." She would never address Him as Prabhat or any other name except Buboo.

### Shrii Shrii Anandamurti

There is a story that when Baba was 15 years old, during study, the final year of Intermediate College, He went one day to a marriage party. The ceremony was in the evening. Baba, instead of remaining in the crowd, left the party and started to walk, looking for a place to be alone. He walked for more than an hour covering a distance of two to three miles within a part of the marriage village.

After some time, He thought that He had come from a very long distance, from the marriage party. With the help of His torch, He looked around and saw that He was standing in a graveyard. There were so many bones and skulls of the animals. Baba thought, "This is a good place, now I will see how brave I am." He lay down to rest there in the graveyard, in the dark lonely night, resting on a tombstone.

(Baba has explained this experience in His book "Strange Experience.") He said He was feeling there, I could explain this nice environment - how nice a place it is." Then Baba noticed that someone was coming, a shadow was coming directly towards Him. My master asked, "Who are you?" There was no reply. Then the shadow stopped. The man was singing a song 'saunga halevirvan khela bhaunglo re bhai bhaver *mela*, bhaver *manus* cal re *phire* cal." (The game of life is over. The earthly parade has ended, o' mortal humans return home).

The man came and sat near Baba. Baba asked, "What is your name? And where are you going?" The man said, "My name is Pathik (traveler) and I am living on the path. The path is my home." Baba remained silent. After a few minutes, the gentleman said, "My name is Kamalakanta Mahapatra. Then he told about his home place. Baba said, 'Kamalakanta, it is a long, long distance from this place to your house. It is better you to go your home and rest." The gentleman said, "I am Pathik. I am a traveler. I do not need to go to my house. I can live anywhere, so please don't think about me."

When he came, he was singing a song. He had such a beautiful voice. Baba told him to repeat the song and Baba enjoyed.

After telling his name, the gentleman had told Baba about one incident. "My Guru initiated me. And when my Guru was about to depart from this world he told me, oh Kamalakanta, now I am going to leave my body (from this physical world) and you will learn your second lesson from a great realized soul, who is coming to earth. Then I asked my master, 'How can I recognize Him?' My master told me 'The moment you sit near him, your inner thought would advise that you must request him to teach you second lesson.'"

"Oh great soul, when I am sitting with you, I realized that you are the perfect one so you give me the second lesson. Baba told him, "What second lesson is? What is Sadhana? I don't know. Kamalakanta, it is better you teach me because I am a very young man and I am also interested to learn all these things. So, oh Kamalakanta, you give me initiation what Guru taught you." Kamalakanta said, "No, no you don't ask me to teach. You know everything. You are the perfect entity and you bless with imparting me second lesson."

Then *Kamalakanta* continued requesting Baba, and Baba again told Kamalakanta, "No, no, I don't know anything. How can I teach you the spiritual practice?" this conversation continued between Baba and Kamalakanta. Then finally Baba said, "Okay I will teach you, be ready. I am going to teach you the Madhuvidya." Baba revised his first lesson and then taught second lesson, (Madhuvidya, Gurumantra i.e. *Brahmacarya*). When Kamalakanta heard the Madhuvidya (second lesson), he become ecstatic and was dancing. He began to dance and singing with joy that this world should become Ananda Maya (in constant Blissful stage), and he told Baba, "You are the Anandamurti." And just as he was saying this, he lay down at Baba's feet. Baba had just been observing and listening song and dance of Kamalakant. He was not actually sleeping, but was in a relaxed mood. He just noticed that someone was calling Him Anandamurti.

(Later, Baba said, "Disciple, Kamalakanta, gave my name Anandamurti.")

Ananda means internal happiness and Murti means the embodiment of bliss so is Anandamurti. So if you take the ideation of Anandamurti in your mind, you will get vibrations or Samadhi.

During Baba's childhood, in age of 5, one day He came across a column of light. Baba asked, "Who are you?" The reply was that, He is Anandamurti. That light then merged into Baba and He became Anandamurti, the embodiment of bliss.

*Anandamurti* Baba worked 26 years in Jamalpur. People of Jamalpur comprehend Him in simple, man ways. They brought problems and Baba gave them satisfactory solution, with a blissful fragrance and face, so people say you are looking Anandamurti. In this way, Anandamurti name came in worldly existence.

On November 14th 1954, my master's disciples were at a meeting at 338 *Rampur* Railway colonies. At this meeting, my master disclosed to some extent the mystery of life and death. He spoke about vital energy or Pranah and the interaction of the five internal vital airs - *Prana*, *Apana*, Samana, *Udana*, *Vyana* and the five external vital airs - *Naga*, Kurma, *Krkara*, Devadatta and Dhananjaya. He explained the function and jurisdiction of each. He asked those were present, "Do you want to see how it happens or are you afraid?" He then called a devotee ordered him to sit in front of Him and to start doing Dhyana (deep meditation). The devotee sat in meditation posture quietly. Then my master said "I, Anandamurti, *Marga* Guru order you, Prana, to push on the Samana and be one with Apana."

The Sadhaka fell back and started to shake. After some time, the Sadhaka became calm. My master, Marga Gurudeva said, "Now he's not experiencing very much pain; now he is enjoying a state: somewhat similar to sleep."

During the above demonstration, my master used the name

Anandamurti several times. It seems that He used the name on earlier occasions. Also, after that, Anandamargiis spontaneously started referring Him as *Anandamurti*.

Difference between Anandamurti and *P.R. Sarkar* - According to my master, Anandamurti does not deal any material thing. Humanity was suffering so it was necessary to propound social philosophy and give solution to the problems of the mundane world *P.R. Sarkar* is there, but Shri Shri Anandamurti is much more than that; He is the embodiment of spirituality.

### Baba

When *Ananda marga* was formed in 1954, many disciples came. One disciple asked Baba "which name should I call you?" At that time His name Prabhat rainjan was already popular, the name *Anandamurtijii* was also known, but the disciple still asked Baba Which name he should call Him. Actually the devotee wanted a related name. Baba said "Baba" this disciple become a little bit annoyed because Baba in *Bengalii* meanse father and that gentle man was of a similar age to Baba also Baba was only about five fit tall and this gentle man was of six feet so why was this smaller man telling Him Baba. Baba explained now I am your Guru when you believe me and accept me than you call me Baba. The devotee accepted Baba's argument.

### Sweetness of the word Baba

Regarding the name Baba, Baba told us a story in Bengali Sarat Chandra was a famous novelist so Baba told us that there was one daughter. One day she was calling to Sharat Chandra Baba....Baba.... Baba.... He was just listening but not giving any reply. His wife approached *Sharat Chandra* and said your daughter is calling so many times Baba but why you are not giving any reply? What type of man you are? Than Sharat Chandra told his wife "when my daughter is calling me Baba, I am just listening and enjoying.

Then Baba said "those disciples when they call me Baba, I do also listen and enjoy. Actually the word Baba came from the Samskrta word Vapra. The word Vapra gradually changed and became Baba. In this book I am going to express my masters name mostly by three names, Shri Prabhat ranjan *Sarkar*, Shrii Shrii *Ananda murtijii* and Baba.

People know that when He was going to talk about the socio economic philosophy He did so as *P.R. Sarkar* and social philosopher and social reformers will know Him as *P.R. Sarkar* and when my master gives discourses on the spiritual philosophy it is in the name of Anandamurtijii. Those who are philosopher *Yogiis* spiritual practiceners they know my masters name as Shrii *shrii Anandamurtijii*. In His college days Baba also writes article for news papers and magazines under the pen name Priyadarshi and Rangadadu. So many articles were written by Baba under these pen names.

### His Astral Body

21st October 1990, when my master left His physical body, the people thought that *P.R. Sarkar* alias Anandamurti is no more in the world. But His work started through His astral body. When He left His body, after 10 minutes, the news went throughout the world. All His devotees cried. The day before He died, He informed many devotees of the world. In North East Asia, in Philippines at Catabato City, there was one lady devotee of Baba. She saw dream that my master Shrii Shrii Anandamurti talking to her saying I'm leaving this world physically but I will be always with you. The devotee lady, mother Bhairavi, said to my master, why are you leaving physical body while 'I'm coming to see You in December to Calcutta." Baba told her, "You cannot be welcomed by my physical body as my work has been accomplished on this planet. Therefore I'm leaving this body." The next day, the devotee phoned the sectorial office and she got the

news that what she saw in the night was correct.

After the great physical departure of my master, the first batch of Acaryas passed examinations and they were posted in different places of Delhi sector (in different schools). One of those *Dadas* was thinking that if Baba is not here physically, who will work without Him. It is better to go back home. I will live with my mother and relatives; as there is no charm in Anandamarga mission without *Shrii Shrii Anandamurti*. This thought came to his mind and he took permission from his acarya. He went to the central office to express his desire to his acarya. His acarya was silent but he told, "Before leaving the mission, you do meditation; then you go." The worker went on the roof and started meditation. According to his statement, Baba came to his mind just after two minutes. Baba was laughing in his mind, the junior dada was also laughing with Baba. Then Baba asked dada, "Why are you leaving the organization? I was just testing you. I am the one who changed your mind; I'm everywhere and always with you." The junior Dada was very happy and he became more strong. After five minutes dada came to his acarya and told the entire story, how Baba came to his mind and blessed him.

After the great physical departure, Baba is still with us spiritually in different forms, guiding us and helping us. Once He told, "When I am in this physical body the nature of my expression is of atomic speed. When I will not remain in this physical form then nature of my work will be of electronic speed. Then my mission will be more transparent to the world."

He helps us - There is a nice story about His help. One day, a Didi (in Anandamarga all nuns are called Didi, respected sister, and all male workers Dada) was in the countryside in India. She came to the railway station to catch a train. At the time she entered the station the local train had already left and so she had to wait two hours for the next train. The station was in the countryside and after the departure of the train, there were no passengers left on the platform and she felt alone. Then she saw that four men were sitting on the other side of the rail track. Actually, they were not good men. They were thinking to disturb her as there was nobody else in the station. Their intention was not good. One of them followed and came near to Didi. She was afraid so then and there she started doing *Dhyan* lesson (in *Anandamarga sadhana* in *Sahaja yoga*, the sixth lesson is called *Dhyana Yoga*, this lesson helps the devotees up to *Nirvikalpa Samadhi*, a complete salvation). When the man reached near Didi, he saw a gentleman sitting in dhoti kurta, wearing glasses on eyes instead of that Didi. He was shocked and came back to his friends, but again they saw that one lady is sitting. This time all of them went to *Didi* but with good intention. They asked how you are? Do you have occult powers? Didi said, "No, I do not have any powers." Then the first man said, "When I came first time, I saw you were sitting there, but when I came near I saw you are not, a gentleman is sitting wearing dhoti *kurta* and eye glasses." There was no time for Didi to understand how the *Dhyana* lesson worked out. All four men touched the feet of Didi realizing their mistakes, and they became compassionate to Didi. (There are several stories of how my master helps devotees and workers).

### Miracles of Earlier Days

When my master was five years old, He recited the *Dhyana* mantra of Lord Shiva.

When Baba was a baby of just 4 or 5 years of age, He used to see that some strange things were entering through His one ear and coming out from another. Baba told this to His mother but she could not understand. In the dream, a great wind comes from the river side to the bed. The blow took Him and laid Baba down near

the river. One monk stood near to Baba and told Baba to speak the Shiva Mantra. Baba told him, "No, I cannot speak this." Again the blow came and returned Baba to His bed. This dream continued for many days and Baba memorized that Shiva Mantra. One day Baba was thinking, "When this time the man will tell me to speak Mantra, I will take His Trishula and hit Him." So He took the *Trishula* and hit him. Baba saw that Trishula was piercing the Shiva *Linga*; the statue of Shiva. After a few days, Baba went with His family to a temple. Baba began uttering the Dhyana *Mantra*:

> *Dhya'yennityam' rajatagirinibham'*
> *carucandravatam'sam*
> *Ratnakalpojjvalam'gam parashu*
> *mrga-varabhiitihastom' prasannam*
> *Padmasiinam' samantam'*
> *stutamamaragan'aer vyaghrakrttim vasanam*
> *Vishvadyam vishvabiijam' nikhilabhayaharam'*
> *pancavaktram' trinetram'*

> "One should constantly meditate on *Mahesvara*, who is like a silver mountain whose lovely ornament is the lovely moon, whose limbs are brightened with the splendor of jewels with axe in hand, holding animals, bestowing foods, ever blissful, seated in lotus posture, wearing a tiger skin, worshiped by all the Gods, the seed, the cause of this vast world, who removes the boundless fears of the entire universe, the one with five faces and three eyes."

When the temple priest heard Baba repeating the mantra, he was surprised, "Where from and did this small boy hear this *Dhyana mantra*? This is the *mantra* of Dhyana for Lord Shiva." The priest inquired about it from the father of the child, *Laxmi Narayan Sarkar*, who replied that they never taught Him.

**He raised *Kundalini* of a Cow** – One day Baba was walking with His friends along the street. A cow was sitting just on the way. Baba touched the spinal cord of the cow and immediately the cow began dancing. Baba explained to His friends who were with him, "The *Kulakundalini* of the cow is now in the *Sahasrara Cakra*," and after a few hours that cow died.

**Showed dead mother of the wife of Ramchandra Jha** – Baba's teacher's name was *Ramchandra Jha*. One day Ramchandra *Jha's* wife told Baba's mother, "I heard that your son is a fortune teller. I want to see my mother. My mother had died and before her death, I did not see her. So please do it for me." Baba accepted and called her. The teacher's wife entered the room. Baba said, "Look at the wall, but don't touch the wall and don't enquire about anything." The lady sat in her chair and just like a film, the image of her mother appeared on the wall before her. She could not control herself and wanted to catch her, so everything vanished then.

### Guru (Master)
#### According to the Lord Shiva (father of civilization and Tantra)

> *Shanto danto, Kuliinascha, Vinita, Shuddha Veshava'n,*
> *Suddhacarii, Supratis't'ha, Sucidiks'a Subuddhimana,*
> *Ashrami, Dhyana Nisthashca, Tantra Mantra Visharadah*
> *Nigrahanugrahe Shakto, Guru'rityabhidhiyate.*

Sadaguru should have the following qualities according to Tantra – Tranquility and purity of mind, modesty and humility, sober dress and maintaining right conduct and livelihood, leading a family life, established in meditation, possessing control over the functions of the mind, possessing control over *Kulakundalini*, loving and governing the disciple.

**Shanta (Tranquility and purity of the mind)** – All actions of the human, either of attachment or detachment, are performed through the mind. Such faculties as *Shravan* (hearing), *Manana* (thinking), and *Nididhyasana* (profound meditation concerning a particular idea) are also actions of the mind. One who has perfectly controlled one's mind through regular psycho-spiritual practices

called in Sanskrit, *Shanta*.

**Danta (balanced, within and without)** – One will have control over motor organs and nervous system by dint of regular physico-psychic, spiritual practices, so far as Baba is concerned, He variably possessed all these rare qualities. In 1967 the communist mafia, assisted by a large number of drunkards, assaulted and killed innocent ashramites. We human beings were in utter helplessness and pain but Baba was there all the while, in His characteristic calm and cool temperament. In 1971 when central bureau of investigation was conspiring against *Ananda Marga*, Baba with a smile on His face used to tell us, all that conspired was designed with, in a usual course. Rather, on those days, He used to quote profusely from the Upanishad, Tantra, Purana, Shiva, Krishna, during the emergency. When He was sentenced to a long imprisonment in a show of trial, *Nageshvaryrasad* an advocate, and *Mr. Basanta, K. Banerjii* went to convey the judgement verdict to Baba. They found later, surprise that Baba received the news with unbelievable fortitude and said, "Don't worry, very soon the tables will turn." All these incidents unmistakably point to what a great command over mind and nerves Baba possessed.

**Kuliinascha** - A Guru must be a *Kulina*, i.e. He must be well versed in the practical process of *Kulakundalini*, *Sadhana* and possess the capacity to help His disciples to attain the highest spiritual stance through the practices of Tantra. In the *sadhana* year 1969 at *Ranchi*, Baba gave practical demonstrations on how *Kundalini* will rise and how *Sadhakas* get spiritual realization. According to His autobiography from the age of six He raised the *Kulakundalini* not only of a man but of animals also. Once He told us that those who have spinal chord can have *Kulakundalini*. He had the power to raise the Kundalini. Perhaps it was 1955 there was *Dharma Maha Cakra* in Monghyr near Jamalpur, Baba demonstrated about *Kulakundalini*. One devotee was sitting in front of Baba. Baba gave the order to the *Kulakundalini*, saying I Anandamurti is ordering you to leave the center of *Muladhar Cakra*. "The *Sadhaka* felt pain in the body. Baba then said devotees body needed more spiritual practice. Then Baba ordered the *Kulakundalini* to go to *Svadhisthan Cakra*, then *Manipur Cakra*. The *Sadhaka* fell down. Baba then said, "This *Sadhaka* is getting spiritual blessing." Hundreds of devotees saw this demonstration. He gave orders to *Kulakundalini* as is His slave.

**Vinita (Modesty and humility)** - The liberated consciousness of the Guru is free from mental complexes and vanity. In His personal life, Baba was extremely modest. In 1979 He was on a world tour, when He reached the Philippines, the Philippine government deported Him. When the team of my Master came to the Bangkok airport, the airport authority asked why the Philippine government has deported Him. He replied with full modest and humility that government does not understand the monk's psychology. Thailand is the land of *Buddhist* monks. The airport authority came to my Master in full respect and allowed Him to enter the country.

**Shuddha Veshavan (Soberly dressed with clean and simple clothing)** - A Guru must be dressed in socially acceptable dress, neither too grand nor ugly. My master always weared a simple and sentient clothes. Any cloth slightly torn, He repaired Himself and used properly. One day we were sitting in the *Jamalpur Ashram*. Baba was on His cot. Baba told *Ac Dasharath*, "See my fate seven thousand years ago." *Ac Dasarath* closed His eyes and said, "You are Lord Shiva." Then Baba told Him again to close his eyes and see His fate three thousand and five hundred years ago. "Baba, I am seeing lord *Krishna*. He is playing a flute on the bank of the *Yamuna* river. Baba told Him, now see, "I'm now a simple man." I was the son of a king before. "Baba showed the cloth to us and said, see my cloth is torn, still I'm using this cloth.

**Shuddhacarii (Sentient nature)** - A Guru should be Suddhachari, i.e. of a sentient nature. When Baba was two years old, "He refused to eat non-vegetarian food." (His mother once said).

**Supratisthiita (Maintaining right conduct and livelihood)** - When my master joined the railways department in 1941 at Jamalpur, His father had died then. All responsibilities came to His shoulders. He had to maintain three brothers and three sisters. Of course He maintained them in a nice way. The family was big and He was the only earning member in the family. Even so, He worked honestly in His working place. He never accepted bribe in any form during His office life. His conduct was very strong, He ever advised His workmates not to take bribes. (See Chapter 2 for details - My master in government service)

**Subuddhimana (Pure and noble conduct)** - Baba possessed all these qualities. A Guru must command solid respect and love; He must be of pure noble conduct. He possessed all these qualities of an exceptional Guru.

**Daksha (Well versed in both theory and practice)** - Shrii Shrii Anandamurti was extremely conversant in both, the theoretical and practical side, not only in *Paravidya* (spiritual world) but also in *Aparavidya* (the knowledge of the physical world). On the one hand, He used to help His deserving devotees to attain various kinds of *Samadhis* and on the other hand, He was equally at ease in guiding the farm secretaries on the methods of rotation of crops, associate crops, sericulture, horticulture and bee keeping, cottage industry and a host of other items that demand extremely specialized knowledge.

**Subuddhiman (Exceptionally intelligent)** - A Guru must be exceptionally intelligent, i.e. it is not enough to possess ordinary intelligence merely. During DMC, Baba always gave a lecture from the platform of R.U. Club (Renaissance Universal Club). The subject of the RU speech was always exceptional and most relevant.

When the session court started in Patna (Bihar), the prominent senior criminal lawyer, Sri Nageshvar Prasad, came with a junior lawyer to the jail to meet Baba and to give Him advice on answers to give the court. When He entered the cell, Baba himself started explaining about the constitution of India and the other countries of the world. Both senior and junior lawyer listened attentively to Baba's proposals and arguments. After half an hour, both advocates came out of the jail having learnt many things from the master.

**Ashramii (Leading a family life)** - In order to set an example for family people He is not a renunciate, and maintained family as well as the spiritual responsibilities. Shrii Shrii *Anandamurti* married Smt. *Uma* Dutta in 1956 at *Chinchura* Court in West *Bengal*. They had one son. He worked till 26th years of His age as a government servant and maintained His worldly family.

**Dhyananistha (Established in Meditation)** - To teach meditation, the Guru must be Master in all the subject of meditation and mantra and should be established in every stages of realization and should make simple and easier for mankind.

**Tantra Mantra Visharada (Expert in tantra and mantra)** - According to *Shiva*, a Guru must be well versed in Tantra and mantra. According to Tantra, it has two branches: - *Vidya Maya* and *Avidya Maya*. He is a master in Vidya and Avidya *Maya* both. If the mind goes towards the supreme, in this case you attend the supreme self is Vidya Maya. If your mind goes to worldly affair i.e. towards worldly things, that is called Avidya Maya. Vidya Maya is introversial movement and Avidya Maya is extroversial one. Baba gave many demonstrations on Vidya and Avidya *Tantra*. He was authority in Tantra and Mantra.

**Nigrahanugrah Shakti (Nigraha is punishment and Anugraha is mercy)** - Guru must have quality to give punishment and love both to the disciple. If the Guru has only one quality then cannot bring discipline and morality among disciples, so the real Guru will embody kindness as well as harshness.

When our headquarters was in *Ranchi* (Bihar), Baba made *Acarya Samhita* (Conduct rules). While He was making rules, He said if anyone only shower love will spoil their child and if only punishes then child will become cruel. There should be harmony

between love and punishment. Baba also added that love and punishment is the same, as a rule (*Shashanat itii anushashanam*), means rules for betterment. Punishment - love = cruelty. The rule is that if you give punishment, it should not exceed the love. For example, if you are giving someone 50% love, then the punishment should not exceed or be more than 50%. This is rule for the betterment or rectification of a person.

From the very beginning, when Baba started this mission, people joined then He initiated the system of the personal contact (PC). During the PC, Baba used to say the misdeeds, drawbacks of a person and when they accept, Baba asked whether they want punishment or not which they always accepted, and He would give. Many *Sadhakas* got *Samadhi* during the personal contact. He gave both love and punishment to His disciples for their betterment.

During reporting, when there was no progress He became angry by abusing us using harsh words and later He would soothe us with very stories and makes all laugh, changing the mood of everyone. In 1979, there was DMC in *Bhavanagar*, Baba called all *Bhukti Pradhans* of *Gujarat* as well as all the workers. Reports were not satisfactory and in front of *Margiis* He started beating a few workers. I had never got any punishment in front of the *Margiis*. At the end of the reporting my master requested all the *Margiis* to leave and the workers to remain in the hall. We were thinking that He would give further punishment to us, but instead He said, "Do you know when a daughter-in-law makes any mistake in the home, the mother-in-law will not give her punishment, instead she will punish the daughter. In this way the daughter-in-law will fear. Like that, you people are my nearer and dearer for that reason I beat you up and out of fear and same they all will work sincerely." When we heard this we all became extremely happy.

## MASTER with all Attributes

Generally, "Ishvara" means the creator of this universe. But in spiritual philosophy, "*Ishvara*" is the one who possesses eight occult powers (Aeshvaryas). These occult powers are delineated by the yogic sages are: *Anima* (very small), *Laghima* (great), *Mahima* (to obtain or create anything), Prapti (to direct and guide any entity), *Ishitva* (to control and unite all forces), *Vashitva* (to take any form), *Prakamya* (to take any form) and *Antarayamitva* (to perceive through anything).

**Anima** (very small): (The power to become very small in size to view microscopic objects in detail) - On the journey back from Saharsa to Jamalpur in 1966, after the DMC, Baba stopped at *Manshi* for the train interchange. The scheduled train going towards Jamalpur was going to arrive two hours late. The *Margiis* knew this and so had arranged some refreshments for Baba. One devotee named Asarti *Mandal*, had the duty of washing Baba's hands. He was illiterate, Asarati had an inferiority complex. He said, "Baba every one in *Ananda Marga* is intellectual." Baba replied, "Yes, that is true except you and I", as soon as Asarti heard this, he dropped the pitcher and collapsed in *Samadhi*.

**Laghima** (The power to offset the power of gravity to make one's body lighter), *Mahima* (the power to expand in size or space viewing the whole universe) - About 1960, there was a DMC in Bengal village. It was a glorious occasion with an intense spiritual vibration. A Grhi Acarya *Sakal Dev* accompanied Baba with some friends to the village from Jamalpur. Arriving in *Bolpur*, they took a bullock cart to the village. It was a beautiful sunrise (like the meaning of the words "Prabhat Rainjan") when they crossed the river. The riverbed was sandy and the devotees had to struggle to push the bullock cart through. Upon crossing the river, the dawn

broke and the morning sun illuminated.

Entering a jungle, they encountered a number of cobras. Strangely all the cobras appeared before Baba. As He passed, one by one they raised their hoods as if like saluting Him and then slithered away.

Later, the villagers saw that Baba was coming, the men blew the conch shells and the ladies crooned. The people were pushed towards Baba. *Sakal Dev* carried Baba on His shoulders from the middle of crowd. He felt that Baba was very light. Baba asked him where He would be staying. Sakal Dev replied that He did not know. Immediately, he felt a tremendous weight pressing down on Him. Unwillingly, He had to set Baba down. Later, a house was given for Baba's accommodation and the people welcomed Him with a pompous ceremony.

After lunch two Grhi Acaryas, *Sakal* Dev and Acarya *Haladhar*, went to a nearby shop to get betel leaves. Acarya *Haladhar* (a spiritually elevated person) patted the shopkeeper, asking him for selected leaves. With the touch of His hand, the shopkeeper plunged into *Samadhi*. This happened because of the intense spiritual vibration near the DMC area and due to the special aura of Acarya Haladhar. (The people of the village were indeed blessed with the Lord's grace that day).

**Mahima** (to obtain or create anything) - Baba's habit of evening walk was from His childhood, had never been discontinued and so the disciples always gathered outside Baba's house every evening, waiting for Him to come out so they can get His glimpse. Strangely enough, that porch on the noisy and crowded street became the place for their silent blissful meditation as they waited regularly for Him. Often, they lost consciousness of the external world and He Himself had to bring them back to a normal state. They would then accompany Him to the field outside the city where Baba used to walk and then sit on a tiger's grave. Many years ago, at that place, a huge strong tiger attacked an Englishman and in a ferocious fight, both were killed. Two graves mark the spot. Baba chose the tiger's grave for their meeting place and that spot holds some of the most cherished and powerful memories for dozens of His early disciples. There, He told many wondrous things and performed many miracles and repeatedly gave His disciples many experiences of deep bliss. Many felt that on this spot they received a new life.

One day, during the rainy season, the field was very muddy. That day an extremely strong policeman of 6 feet 3 inches taller and weighing of 200 pounds was accompanying. As Baba was preparing to carefully cross the muddy field, the policeman very proudly (of his great brawn and strength) offered to carry Baba across the field.
