Baba's body was still small and thin but He smiled and said, "Will you be able to do it?" The policeman boasted, of course, why not! So Baba quietly got on the policeman's back and the huge vain man lifted him up thinking that He would run to the grave with Baba on His back. He started panting and gasping for breath. With each step he sank deeper and deeper into the mud, his back bent under the heavy load. Finally he could not bear it any longer, he sat down in the mud and cried, "Baba, please get off my back, I cannot carry you." Baba got down and they walked together to the grave through the muddy fields. From that day on, that man's vain pride was in size and strength disappeared, a new and sweet humility replaced his old arrogance.

Prapti (power to have or create whatever one desires to transport self anywhere) - Once in *Ranchi*, Baba called one dada asking him, "with what do you see?" He replied, "I see with my eyes." "Now, you see with your nose." So the boy began to see with his nose. Then Baba said, "See with your tongue." Then the power of vision came in his tongue. Then He said, "See with your ear" and he began to see with the ear. Then Baba explained, this changing the power of the organ cannot even be done by Siddhas, it is only by the power of *Paramapurusa* that it can be done. *Paramapurusa* can do anything He likes.

Ishitva (power to have control over factors or elements like

wind, rain, snow etc) -

Once Baba said, "This week, everyone will go without his or her favorite habit." There was one Margii who had a strong attachment to chewing a particular type of tobacco and did not obey his command. During the first day of the week, the Margii was walking with another Margii and chewing his tobacco. Suddenly, to his amazement, the other *Margii* stopped still and gazed into space as if under some spell. Then the *Margii* turned to him and spoke to him in the same voice as Baba saying, "I told you not to do that." The first Margii was even more amazed and shocked and so took the tobacco out at once. He did not chew any more that week. At the end of the week, Baba had put up a list of all *Margiis* who did not comply with the order. At the bottom of the list was this Margii's name, only it had a line through it.

Vashitva (to control and unite all forces) - It was 1963. Baba was sitting outside the *Jamalpur Ashram*. All of a sudden a cow entered the *Ashrama*. An *Avadhuta* asked Baba, "Is there a spine of a cow and does it have a Kundalini?" Baba then said, "Do you want to see the movement of the *Kundalini* of this cow?" They all said yes. Then Baba made a posture with His hand and directed it towards the cow. The cow began to jump and dance and seemed to be very happy. Baba said, "See, it is because of the rising Kundalini that this cow is feeling very happy and dancing. She will not live very long. She will die and after death, she will take the form of a human being."

Prakamya (To take any form) - Bhagalpur is a big city in Bihar. The first Ananda *Marga* Jagrti was established there. Due to the efforts of the *Margiis*, the message of Ananda *Marga* spread all over the state. This aroused enmity and jealousy amongst the orthodox who advocate casteism and other dogmas. They are completely conservative in their traditions, beliefs, and customs.

When I arrived, a *Patna* newspaper published a news article attacking Ananda Marga. Acarya *Kuladiip*, a senior Grhi Acarya, then filed a case against the reporter. During the court session, the defendant mockingly said to the judge that the Guru is an ordinary clerk in a railway office. The judge was amused and asked if it was so.

Acarya Kuldiipa remembered his Guru *mantra*. Then he became very serious and said, "Yes, your honor, it is so. However, history shows that Christ was a carpenter, *Mohammad* a shepherd, Shiva a bullherd and Krishna a cowherd. My Lord is a great spiritualist!" The judge was pleased by the bold words and ultimately decided the case in our favor. The defendant was penalized.

When the trial session was over, I left the courtroom happily and moved for Jagrti. After walking a little distance a man appeared and asked me where I was going. I said that I was going to the Ananda *Marga* ashram. The man said that I was going in the wrong direction and pointed out the proper way. I looked where he was pointing, but when I turned around to thank him he was gone! I reached the ashram safely by following his directions. Three days later, I went to Jamalpur and met Baba. In the course of our conversation, Baba advised me that I must memorize all the ways and direction of my destination so that I would not get lost. Then I realized that Baba had been guiding me on that day.

Antaryamitva (To perceive throughout anything) - One young family *Margii* named *Ram Kumar* from *Trimuhan*, Bihar was initiated into *Kapalika Sadhana* by Baba. Afterwards, Baba directed him to do the graveyard *Sadhana* every night (for 15 nights). Harendra *Kumar*, a senior *Margii* who also was a *Kapalika*, accompanied him every night to give him confidence.

Ram *Kumar* was fearful of snakes, which often crawled near him when he was meditating. Finally on the last night, when finished *Sadhana* at 1.30 a.m., *Ram Kumar* said to *Harendra* how relieved he was that from then on they would only have to practice the *Kapalika Sadhana* on Amavasya (the night of the new moon) only.

The next day, Baba came from Jamalpur by train, so they

both met Him and accompanied Him for part of the journey in His compartment. Baba smiled at Ram Kumar and said, "Last night at 1.30 a.m., a molecule of air came to Me from Trimuhan. It whispered to Me that it overheard you say, 'I am very happy, brother, that the troublesome duty of performing Kapalika Sadhana is finished and now I only have to do it once in a month!'"

PRATAP - The quality Pratap means to rule over and to love. The one who possesses Pratap will at one time seem very loving and affectionate, but at other times will show anger and scold when we do wrong. If one was to show love at all times or if one was to be always scolding, then we would either become spoiled or not learn to discriminate between what is good and bad, or we could not bear to be with the presence of someone who was always chastising us; that is why Baba says He loves us and at the same time He punishes us for our evil doings. It is out of love and benevolence that there are two personalities in one.

There was one Margii who was under increasing pressure to take bribes at his work place. But he knew that if he did so, all-knowing Baba would know and would scold him. Then he reasoned that even Baba must sleep. Therefore, if he would take the bribe when Baba is sleeping, He would not know. After taking the bribe late at night, he stood in front of Baba's photo smoking a cigarette and thinking how clever he was.

After sometime, he attended DMC. Just as Baba was getting up after finishing His discourses, He suddenly sat down again. He then told everyone that there was a man present who thought he could outsmart Paramapurusa by taking bribes late in the night thinking Paramapurusa is asleep. The man became very uncomfortable as he realized that Baba was talking about him. Baba asked that man concerned to stand up, but also no one would hold anything against him. Everyone agreed. The man did not like to stand up, but thought it would be worse if he did not do so, pointed by Baba. Baba said that after taking the bribe, he stood in front of him smoking a cigarette thinking how smart he was.

Finally, with a feeling of great embarrassment, he got up and everyone immediately turned and looked at him disapprovingly. Baba immediately snapped at them, "You promised not to hold anything against him," everyone quickly turned back to look at Baba. We cannot do anything secretly.

Yasha means reputation. In the Bhagavad Giita, *Bhagavan* Shrikrsna tells Arjuna "To protect the righteous and to destroy the wicked, to establish dharma firmly, I take birth age after age." So it is said in philosophy that *Taraka Brahma* comes every 3500 years to liberate His devotees, destroy the Adharma forces and establish Dharma. It is because of this that Bhagavan naturally gets both a good reputation and a bad one. The forces that are right with Him, for dharma, give Him a good reputation (*yash*) and those forces that fight against Him give Him a bad reputation (Apayash). When Bhagavan is on a planet, it gets polarized into two forces - the Dharmika and the Adharmika. There is no neutral force. However, as corruption, injustice and immorality gets nearer to their peak, it becomes impossible not to take sides. It is by this polarization alone that Bhagavan can be recognized.

"Human civilization now faces the final moment of the critical juncture. The dawn of a glorious new era is on its one side and the worn out skeleton of the past on the other. Man has to adopt either of these two. Yes, spiritual soldiers, you are the worshippers of life divine. Hence, I call on you to adorn this crimson dawn deluge with glorious light. Victory is surely yours."

This polarization took place during the time of both Bhagavan Shrikrsna and Bhagavan Sadashiva. During Shrikrsna's time, there were the Adharmika Kauravas fighting against Krishna and the *Dharmika* Pandavas. During the time of Sadashiva, there were the Adharmika Aryans fighting against Sadashiva and the Dharmika Non-Aryans of India. In both cases, the odds were in favor of Dharma (Moral) forces, because of their size and strength, but, because the Dharmika forces had Bhagavan on their side, they ultimately won. Baba's spiritual force is millions of

times more powerful than the material force and hence the spiritual moralist ultimately wins.

Some devotees were sitting with Baba in Jamalpur hills. They were happy listening to His spiritual talk when suddenly Baba stopped talking and began pointing to the stars and planets. The noble work of establishing *Dharma* has been completed in some planet of a different solar system. On some others, however, He was needed, He said. Baba sighed and said, you people are fortunate, you would do Sadhana and get salvation, but there is no peace for me. When my job is finished on one planet, I move to another. Would you like to go with me and help my mission?" They all agreed. When Baba said that Ananda Marga is not young, but old, vast and cosmic, He meant it.

"My love, affectionate blessings are always with you but those who hold the flag continuously, marching to the tune of cosmic energy, they alone will be the heroes, they alone will be able to carry on the task of the supreme, they alone will be the future vanguards and they will create such a society that love and humanity will be established.

In the fight against animosity, hypocrisy and exploitation, victory is yours. You will establish Prout, you will establish humanity. In the fight, by the way, if your throat is cut, you will remain in my cap during the fight against animality, if you die, you will get Moksha (salvation) and if you remain alive, you will establish humanity. Moksa is in your hands."

**SHRII** - The meaning of Shrii is beauty, charm and attraction. It could be said that Shrii is a characteristic of that divinity within us all. It is only because of Maya's dominance of a person that divinity cannot be seen. When someone does the Sadhana of the great, then this Shrii in everything gradually becomes apparent in the Sadhakas minds and they too feel it within themselves. This Shrii can also be dangerous if one is too egotistical. Some Sadhakas use their charm and attraction to persuade others to do things for them, which are not *Dharmika*, or according to the principles of *Yama* and *Niyama*. It is also true that one of the reasons for becoming a luminous body is because Sadhana was done with the aim of becoming handsome or beautiful.

It is said in philosophy that to the ones who do not do Bhagavan Sadhana (lessons), He looks just like an ordinary man. They cannot see anything particularly great or attractive about him. But those who do practice Bhagavans Sadhana, they alone get to realize some of his greatness, beauty and charm. I say some of his greatness because no one will completely know His greatness - it is too vast beyond "Knowing". More and more, His devotees get attracted to Him. After a while, His devotees will feel peace and bliss - even *Samadhi* (salvation) just by looking at His photo, or hearing or singing His name, or just by thinking about Him and as for being in His physical presence.... What can one say? When all anxiety, fear, shame, sadness, hate, etc are lost by thinking or singing about Him, words could not possibly describe what it is like to be in *Bhagavan's* (God's) presence.

Before Baba's release from jail, many of His devotees had never seen Him physically and yet they had sacrificed their whole life, all their personal desires and comforts for Him without a single doubt or regret. Such is the universal Shrii of Baba. Just to see Baba in a dream can cause one to wake up with tears in one's eyes and crying, Baba, Baba" and then to collapse into more tears of joy.

Even if one has not dreamt of Him, then there is no denying the wonder feeling of closeness that one gets through doing His Sadhana and service. It is no wonder that He is called Shrii Shrii *Anandamurtijii*. Even the greatest skeptic will fall in love with His personality if He or she does His *Sadhana* and work.

One evening, when Baba was on a field walk, I was walking behind Him with several other guards. After sometime, I noticed that each time Baba walked under a tree, it would come alive with the sound of birds singing. As soon as He passed under the tree,

the birds would stop singing, even though we guards were still walking under the tree.

During one *Darshan* in *Tiljala*, I looked out of the window and noticed that all the fish in the pond, below where Baba was, were facing towards Him. When Baba passes by in His car, dogs stop fighting to watch Him, and then return to fighting once He has passed. Once, Baba passed a wedding. All the guests left the bride and groom to watch Baba.

Baba's form is such a powerhouse of energy and bliss that no one can ignore His presence, even if it is just the sight of Him going by in the car. Humans, animals, plants, all are affected and vibrated by His presence. The idea that form is the point where *Parama Purusa's* love enters the universe seems apt.

If someone really loves Him, he or she is bound to say, "Ah! How nicely He speaks, how sweet His language... is how soft His hobby is, no one has ever experienced such softness. I have never seen such incomparable physical charm - how sweet is His smile - How blissful are all His expressions. It is difficult to say which expression is more charming." That is why I say there is no second entity that is so beautiful, so pleasing and soft as our *Vrajagopala*.

One very interesting phenomenon in *Ananda Marga* is that all *Margiis* seem to have a similar appearance - their eyes all look the same. The eyes reflect one's state of mind and since all these multilateral Margii minds is doing the same *Sadhana* of the great (Baba's *Sadhana*), it means that all these different minds are being gradually converted into the same cosmic mind. That is also why there is a very strong feeling of brother/sister-hood in Ananda Marga. It is this same cosmic sentiment that will create a world fraternity in the future. Human made barriers or distinctions, such as race, nationality, age, sex or profession, can never keep Brahma *Sadhakas* separate. These false distinctions have no importance in *Ananda Marga* as far as spirituality is concerned. All are looked upon as a manifestation of the Supreme Father and all are His children.

Sometimes Baba's attractiveness causes His devotees to become attached to Him physically and forget about their duties and work. There was once a Didi who was spending more time visiting Baba than doing her work. When Baba heard that she was there to see Him yet again, He said, "Why is she here again? Hasn't she got any work to do?" She got His message and left.

"When you advance in the cult of sweet devotion, you enjoy more and more sweetness. While advancing, you enjoy bliss at every step, obviously, at that stage; you don't like the tormenting harshness of knowledge and action."

Baba's form is extremely attractive, and also having great spiritual significance, it must not be forgotten that Baba is not really the body but Supreme consciousness situated in our Sahasrara Cakra. To experience the inner Baba is to experience the real Baba. By His grace, we can experience Him internally in many different ways in *Sadhana*, in dreams and in daily ideation. Actually, the inner experiences are greater because they are not limited by the restrictions of the external world (by time, place or person). Baba can spend as much time as He wants with us internally and in whatever way He wants. He can be so sweet and intimate. While He can be very sweet and intimate externally then it is a fact of spiritual life that the inner world of the devotee will be the sweetest.

Shrii also means activity and energy. "Sh" is the acoustic root of *Rajoguna*, the mutative principle or active force. "Ra' is the acoustic root of *bala* or energy. Together (Sh + Ra = *Shrii*). That means one who has much energy and utilizes it by doing much work (activity). Even physically or psychologically handicapped has energy and the capacity to use it. Many people, unfortunately, are lazy and do not use much of their energy. Even some devotees will sing and pray to the Lord for strength when they already have it but do not utilize it properly.

"I have already given you the strength to do the work." If one has used up all his strength, then immediately Baba will give

more. There is no need to ask. All that is needed is for the Sadhaka to start the work and master will provide further what is needed to complete it.

There was a doctor devotee of Chennai who was given the duty of PROUT. But he was thinking that he was not so experienced, did not know many things, and not efficient either. He wondered whether he will be able to carry out this responsibility. This tussle was going on in his mind. So he came to Jamalpur thinking that he will ask Baba to change his posting and give it to some other responsible person. But out of fear from Baba, he was not saying all these things, although it was in his mind at that time.

Baba came to Jamalpur ashram. He called one *Avadhuta* and said to sit in his lap. He said that if *Parama Purusa* so desires, He could give him all the powers to see the minds of these people. "Just see, Divyananda, just see the minds of these people." Divyananda began to see. Then Baba said, "If *Parama Purusa* desires so, He can give him the power to read others' mind." Then He said, "*Divyananda* read the thought of these people." *Divyananda* began to do it. Baba then said, "If *Parama Purusa* so desires, He can give the power to read the thoughts of a man who is in America. *Divyananda* just see in America, ananda go into his mind and read his thoughts." *Divyananda* began to do it. Baba said, "Usually people think they do the things, but it is *Parama Purusa* who does everything. Responsibility has been given and if *Parama Purusa* wants, then men can accomplish it. Man should surrender to *Parama Purusa* and He will make you success." Then Baba looked at that devotee and said, "Do you understand?" and He laughed and kept mum.

"The spirit of service comes from the spirit of serving the Supramental entity. Where the spirit of serving the supramental entity is lacking, there cannot be any service spirit. So pro spiritual psychology is essential for a happy and integrated social order."

There is someone compared to others, do not have energy but still keep them active. They keep working until the last breath of their life. This is good. Better is "working even while dying". *"Karate Karate Maro, Marate Marate Kam Karo"*

"Whatever you speak or act, forget Him never, Keeping His name in your heart, work, remembering it is for Him and, endlessly active, drift in bliss."

To be active or energetic is not the last word. There are many corrupt and immoral persons who through their hard work have caused much exploitation and cause of poverty in the society. Knowledge without action is futile, but action without proper knowledge is destructive. At least the former will affect individuals only, but the latter will affect many. All action, to be worthwhile, should be done with ideation of the supreme and to please Him alone.

If while working ideation is that Baba is doing everything and we are merely His tools then ego will not occur. I have noticed that before burnt-out occurs, the *Sadhaka* neglects his or her spiritual practices especially *Kiirtana* and *Brahmacarya*, and becomes very dry and cynical, critical, and loses balance or *Parma*. Even if 'burnt-out' does not occur, degeneration does take place, with eventual rejection of the spiritual path.

Baba is charming and beautiful, active and energetic. He also has these qualities that is merged into one, His activities are beautiful and his energies radiates charm, whatever way you look at Him, Baba is *Shrii*, the divinely attractive and dynamic one.

**Vaeragya** - It means renunciation. *Vaeragya* does not mean suppression, nor does it necessarily mean giving up worldly possessions - principally family and society. *Vaeragya* means to be in that state where the mind feels indifferent or unattached to any objects or entity. "The practice whereby detachment is developed for finite objects, that is, the discipline where by the mind is not influenced by the attraction of worldly objects is *Vaeragya Sadhana*".

*Vaeragya* means detachment or renunciation of worldly

desires. This is different from the suppression of desires, which is just as bad, if not worse, than the expression of them. While both leads to crudification, suppression leads to perversion as well. Suppression leads to obsession, which leads to perversion. *Vaeragya* does not mean neglecting family responsibilities running off into the jungles or mountains. Rather, it means to live in the world but in a detached way, having neither attraction nor aversion (both of which are caused by *Avidya Maya*) to the world. It is only when we have this detachment, along with Viveka or proper discrimination that we can advance to higher states of spirituality, including the development of true love.

*Bhagavan Shrii Shrii Anandamurti* certainly possesses *Vaeragya*. Even on the physical plane, He possesses very little, Baba's dress is always very neat and clean, but never aristocratic. In His office He was always very humble, but quite adequate for His work, never placing a burden upon others.

Mentally, his detachment can also be seen too. There was a very rich *Margii*, who came to *Ananda Marga* in its early stages. At that time, Margii was also very immoral. One day, he went to see Baba and took with him seven packs of rupees (700,000 rupees) to give Baba, thinking that Baba would be pleased with him. When he met Baba, he gave Baba the money in a bag. But Baba knew about his motives and threw the money out onto the streets. The man thought that Baba did not know what was in the bag and so he went running out onto the street to collect the money. In the mean time, Baba locked the door.

When he returned to Baba's door and knocked, Baba would not let him enter and said that because he would rather run after money than Him, he could stay out.

Once, when He was in jail, He said that He is in jail for a greater cause. Anybody can go to jail for greater cause, except my long fasting, everybody could follow my path. He sacrificed everything for His mission. He left service because He became busier in organizational work. He left family matters to teach others. He left family and followed the rules of renunciation after He made the system of monks and nuns. Nobody could blame that we left home for the greater interest of society and he is still attached. He gives up His son because He did not follow the conduct rules given by Baba. This is also a great teaching for family *Margiis* and ideal men of the society.

**Jnanana** - One day my Master was traveling in a train to go for *Dharma Maha Cakra* (DMC). A politician and a writer were sitting nearby and asked my master what His profession was. Baba smilingly replied, "I write articles". Then the man asked Baba in what language? Baba replied that He was writing in all languages of the world. The men again asked Baba which subjects He writes on and Baba answered, "on all subjects". Baba then called him by his name and asked the meaning of his name. He was surprised how Baba knew his name and said he did not know the meaning. Baba then explained to the man the meaning of his name in different language for forty-five minutes.

During His entire life my master could speak on any topic and sound like He was specialized in it. For example, when spoke on geography, about a particular mountain and its rivers, He would go into such details about the physical features or location of that area, which is not possible to explain without visiting the place. Baba would choose any language and would describe its origin and how it came into present existence. It was incredible, virtually impossible, that a single person could have such vast and absolute knowledge in the world. He must be omniscient and knowledge personified.

When Baba was in *Bankipur* central jail in *Patna*, during a court session, when the judge asked Him in which language He would give His statements He said He knew more than 200 languages of the world, you can record my statement in any language. When the judge heard this sentence He became quiet.

Baba gave 5018 *Prabhat Samgiita* in different languages and rhythms. This massive contribution is in the area of intellect and

culture, His spiritual knowledge is even deeper, but it would be absurd and even impossible to explain a little of it. It is beyond the thought and the words. Only through personal intuitional experiences one can appropriate or gain any of his spiritual knowledge.

Knowledge or *Jnanana* is of two types, one is worldly knowledge (objective knowledge or *Apara Jnanana*), and the other is spiritual knowledge (subjective knowledge or *Para Jnanana*). Worldly knowledge is not considered as real knowledge as it is relative and, therefore, it is never permanent or lasting. For an example, a person who has got degree in mathematics and if does some other work other than mathematics, later on he will forget most of the things of mathematics.

Spiritual knowledge or knowledge of self (*Atma Jnanana*), can never be lost because it is infinite and unchangeable. Realizing or knowing oneself or *Atma*, a person becomes the knower of spiritual knowledge. It does not go under metamorphosis so it is known as *Satya* or truth. Truth is *Brahma*. *Brahma* is the source of both spiritual and worldly knowledge. So those who know *Brahma* go beyond time, place and person and gets established in truth, bliss and immortality.

*Sadhana* and *Svadhyaya* bestow wisdom and spiritual knowledge. *Svadhyaya* gives spiritual knowledge. Its wisdom is not restricted with time, place and person. It is unchanging and relevant in all circumstances and in all the ages. In *Ananda Marga* only specific books are recognized for *Svadhyaya* or spiritual knowledge, they are *Ananda Sutram* and *Shubhasita Samgraha*. Also *Ananda Vacanamrtam*, *Namami Krishna Sundaram*, *Namah Shivaya Shantaya* are the sources of *Svadhyaya*.

In these *Svadhyaya* books the *Sadhaka* will find the answers of the so many questions that are needed for spiritual progress. This invaluable knowledge eliminates the obstacles and dangers from the path of the *Sadhakas*. Just as action is important for the expression of *Samskaras* and producing certain qualities, similarly, a proper understanding of spirituality is essential. For example, the more we progress, the more conspicuous is the influences of *Avidyamaya* (i.e. the pashas and ripus.)

### Taraka Brahma

*Taraka Brahma* is one who liberates all. He is the link between mundane existence and highest realization. *Taraka Brahma* is the creation of devotional sentiment.

The excellence of *Taraka Brahma* is defined as follows:

1. He takes birth at a transitional period of history, when one era is decaying. He leads the world into a golden era. (Yuga sandhi).
2. He has no guru.
3. He comes with scriptures that enlighten us with spiritual wisdom.
4. He provides the practical method to realize the content of scriptures.
5. He is a preceptor at birth.
6. He does not compromise with injustice.
7. He takes the determination (*Samkalpa*) to establish a well neat society.
8. He is a revolutionary.
9. He is a great organizer with strong moral force.
10. He roots out all immorality.
11. He possesses a multifarious personality.
12. He does not need to undergo any discipline to achieve realization.
13. He is generous.
14. His physical appearance has the subtlety of the Supramundane level (the sixth lesson of *Sahaja Yoga* is evidence of this).

15. His physical form awakens a strong vibration in the physico-psychic level of devotees.
16. He is always controversial.

All the above qualities are manifested within my master *Shrii Shrii Anandamurtijii*.

The outstanding and attractive qualities of Baba are shown as He narrates the experience of His own birth and childhood.

'I am in my mother's womb. From there itself, I am seeing my mother and I recognize her so well. I see my father, my sister and my other relations. How well do I know them! And I know their names too!

"I am born. Normally children weep on birth. I did not. I am all smiles. I am happy to be born. I want to address persons around me by their names because I know them so well. But alas! How incapacitated I am. My vocal chords do not permit my voice to come out.

"They want to feed me. They have put a piece of cotton in a cup containing milk. Drop by drop the cotton will drop milk in my mouth. How silly of these people! Am I a child to be fed in this manner? I shall drink with the cup, not the cotton. In protest, I raise my hand and hold the cup. They are taken aback at what I have done! I realize that I have done much to perplex them and I return to being a child just born.

"I grow up. To move about, I have to crawl. How painful it is my elbows and knees are full of aches! How long is this going to be? And I notice someone speaking into my ears, "Some days more, just a few days more". I don't see, Who is He? But his voice is around me whenever I object. What a consolation He showers on me by telling me that this painful affair won't be much longer! I grow up a little more. I am sleeping by my mother's side. In the dead of night, I am awakened. What I see is so unusual! My entire being, my mother's being, that cot, the room, the space beyond is all full of such sweet effulgence! I enjoy seeing it. I lost more and more in it. I wonder what would happen to my mother had she too seen this all engulfing light...."

Baba went on to explain more about His childhood experiences with this strange voice talking to Him. Finally, He asked Dada *Amitananda* what it all? Dada did not know and while Baba was away he asked another dada who replied, "It is part of Baba's being itself which is projecting now and talking to Him. Helping Him and guiding Him. It is part of His unconscious."

Baba came back and asked again if Dada knew the answer. He said, he did not, but repeated what the other dada had told him. Dada Amitananda asked if this was correct and Baba smiled and said,

> "*Naham manye* suvedti no *Na* vedeti Veda *ca*, Yo *nastadveda tadveda* no Na vedeti Veda *ca*."
>
> "Neither do I say that i know Him, nor do I say that I don't know Him. Because I know that He is beyond my knowing and not knowing".

### Devoted for the Devotees

Trimuhan is a very small village in *Bhagalpur* district, *Bihar* province in India, but this village is historically important in Ananda Marga history. The meaning of the village name comes from Tri means three and Muhan means mouth. Three rivers met in one place here therefore the village name became Trimuhan.

On January 26th, 1958, the first Renaissance Universal Club was established in Trimuhan, *Bhagalpur*, Bihar (India.)

In presence of Baba, the youth of Trimuhan played drama from the plateform of Rawa. One of the devotee of the village was called Narsingh. He was a good devotee of Baba. He was a landlord and a retired agriculturist. He spent most of his time helping to spread the philosophy of Ananda *Marga*. He was not very qualified, but he always talks on devotion, and through his talk many people took initiation. Once, my master said to

Narsingh "Narsingh never left D.M.C (Dharma *Maha Cakra*, collective spiritual gathering, Where Baba gave spiritual discourse and *Varabhaya Mudra*)" Once, there was D.M.C. at Bangalore, (an important city of south India). One egoistical intellectual Margii said to Narsingh at Bangalore, How you are going to understand English? "Narsingh replied, "you know, Dada *jii* even when Baba speaks in my area in *Hindi*, I cannot understand His discourses. I am going to *Bangalore* just to get His glimpses". The Dada was mum.

Once, in 1966, when the mangoes ripe in his orchard, *Narsingh* would select the best ones and bring them to Baba's house. In those days the organization was new, but there was a rule that for any one who will go to Baba's house, they would have to obtain permission from General Secretary. One day, Narsingh came to Baba's house with some mangoes. When Baba saw Narsingh, He scolded him saying, why have you come here without the permission of G.S.? Narasingh replied, "Do you know, Baba, the milkman, sweeper or gardener does not take permission. I am the gardener of your garden. I have brought mangoes from your garden to your house; I don't need to take permission? Why do you scold me?" Baba's mood was changed. He entertained Narsingh with some refreshment. Later Baba said to G.S. "Narsingh doesn't require permission to enter my house. There are no rules for a devotee in the field of his devotion."

My master is a devotee of :

> Yogi Jati Tapasvi Anisiddha Nirmtara Tahi Dhyana *Lagawe*,
> Tahi Ahiran Ki Chokarian,
> Chachian Bhara Chacha Par Nach Nacawe.

Some yogis who lack devotion they practice severe penance to obtain His grace, but to no avail. But the devotees for simple minded people devotion makes God very loyal to them. He comes whenever the devotee calls. When He was asked for His autobiography, He replied, "I was a mystery, I am a mystery, and I shall remain a mystery."

His philosophy encompasses the trifarious existence, (physical, mental and spiritual), individual and collective interests, and presents a complete adjustment amongst them.

If one thinks deeply about the Master, one becomes Him. Like the salt doll that, according to one story, goes into the sea to measure it, but in so doing, dissolves and merges with the sea.

> *Lali Mere Lal kii, Jita Dekhu Tita lal,*
> *Lali Dekhan Mae Gaya, Mae Bhi Ho Gaya Lal.*

The glory of my Lord shines everywhere, where ever He is. I had a glimpse of Him, I find myself as Him.

### A Brief Autobiography of My Master

In the 15th century, in the time of *Shershah Surii*, *Sri Jaicand Asthana*, the generous intellectual in *Arabic* and Farsi language, had come from *Etawah (Uttar Pradesh)* to *Dhaka* in *Bengal (Bangladesh)*. At the request of *Nabab* of *Dhaka*, *Jaichand Asthana* established himself in *Dhaka*. His son was *Sri Harsha Vardhana Asthana*. *Sri Harsha Vardhana Asthana's* son was *Srii Krshnanda Deo*, and his son *Ramanand Deo*. *Ramanand's* son was *Sri Gorhari Sarkar* and *Gorhari'* son was *Sri Kunj Bihari Sarkar*. *Sri Kunj Bihri* had two sons, *Sri Laxminarayana Sarkar* and *Sri Nirmal Chand Sarkar*. *Sri Laxminarayana sarkar* had four sons and three daughters; my master is the second child and the eldest son of the family. His mother's name was *Abhadevi*.

My master was born in the day of full moon, of 21st may, 1921, at *Jamalpur*, *Bihar*, India. He completed class 10th from *Jamalpur Railway High school*. During His high school education, His father died. He completed two years college education from *Vidya Sagar College*, *Calcutta University*. During that time, He gave initiation to *Kalikananda Avadhuta*. In 1941,

He joined the railway department as an accountant. He worked 26 years in *Jamalpur*. Meanwhile, He started Ananda Marga mission in 1954. He left *Jamalpur* on 30th December, 1966 for Ananda Nagar, the headquarters of the mission.

After 65 days, (March 5th, 1967) He also left Ananda *Nagar* when the communists killed five monks. *Ranchi* was then headquarters. From June 20th, 1970, through seminar years He was in *Ranchi*, He educated the *Margiis* in all the philosophies of *Ananda Marga* - social as well as spiritual.

It was in 1968 that the first worker left India, to propagate the philosophy of Ananda Marga in Kenya (Africa). Many workers went to different parts of the world. He also left India for the first time in 1968 for Philippines.

The fourth headquarters became Patna, the state capital of *Bihar*.

From June 20, 1970 till September 1979, the organization and He faced much struggle. He endured them one by one. He was arrested on false charges of murder. The government through the help of jail doctor on February 12, 1973, poisoned to kill Him. On 1st April 1973, (as per marga diary) He started fasting to have His demands met; this fasting lasted until the 2nd of August 1978 (5 years, 6 months and 2 days).

He shifted headquarters from Patna to Calcutta in 1979.

He visited many countries of the world. For social service projects, He divided the world into nine sectors. He conducted more than 300 spiritual gatherings. He wrote more than 100 books. He has more than three thousand trained monks and nuns. He worked day and night to give service to the suffering humanity. My master left His body on October 21st, 1990, in *Tiljala* (Calcutta).

# II

## MY MASTER AN EXAMPLE

> "Set an example of individual conduct before asking anyone to do the same."

> [Two sunflowers]

### Basic Quality Of the People

*Prarabhyati na khalu vighna bhayena niicaih,*
*vighnavihata viramanti madhyah*
*Vighnairmuhurapi pratihanyamanah mahajanah,*
*Prarabhya karmani na parityajanti.*

"The most ordinary people never begin any good work, as they are afraid of the obstacles. The people of mediocre merits, when faced obstacles, leave their work unfinished, but the best ones, though confronted by obstacles time and again, yet never leave their work unfinished."

*Nindantu niti nipunah yadiva stuvantu,*
*Laksmii samavisatu graham gacchatuva yathestam*
*Adyaeva va maranmastu yugantare va,*
*Nyayat patha pravicalanta padamna dhiirah.*

"The so-called experts of moral principals may shower praises and abuses. The mythological goddess of wealth may come in or go away, death may threaten the existence or lure, the life of ages may persist, but under all these circumstances, the extraordinary persons never digress and go away single step from the righteous path."

During field walk in 1966, one evening at *Jamalpur*, my master was telling how to initiate and finish the work? He gave a formula - RCP. R means right time, C means continuous effort, and the meaning of P is proper finishing.

He started the mission at the right time - when the world was in full of crisis economically, politically, and socially too. From childhood, He had started work with planning and He never stopped work although there were many difficulties.

For example: He was arrested on false charges. His mission was banned from July 4, 1971 to March 2, 1977. His personal assistant and His wife became antagonistic towards the organization. The government was also against Him due to His social and political philosophy - PROUT.

When *Ananda Marga* was banned, He applied to the government to stop the case against Ananda Marga as almost all the workers were in jail or had gone underground. The government refused. He demanded the return of the car and valuable things for sale to raise funds to fight His case. The government also refused this demand. He was poisoned in jail.

Throughout the entire situations of struggle, my master stood for *Dharma* and His own rights. He never stopped the work and through His continuous effort the ban was lifted and we won the case from the *Patna* High Court.

One day, during a reporting session, He told a funny story. "Once upon a time there was a dog who decided to reach Delhi from *Calcutta* within fifteen days. When he started the journey from *Calcutta*, many dogs started barking at him and his speed doubled and so he reached Delhi in ten days. The company of dogs helped him to reach *Delhi* very fast."

Baba said that in 1976 Ananda Marga was banned and at that time we existed in only five countries of the world. The ban gave much publicity to *Ananda* Marga and they came to know *Ananda Marga* as a right path and so it became very popular among the public.

When you press a spring, if it is weak it will be destroyed. But if the spring is strong then it will jump many times this is what happened with Ananda Marga- my master never stopped the work.

He was always bold in every respect. One day in 1968 He was resting in Neterhat (the eastern part of Bihar province). When He came back from His evening walk He entered the room but came back out and quoted the Hindi film line - "*Jalne wale jala kare Kismat mere sath hai.*" - Those that have jealousy let him jealous; my luck is always with me.

He accepted the challenge of difficulties as a friend and He embraced it. He was never scared of difficulties. During reporting sessions He advised "If no one helps you then you march alone". He quoted the poem from *Ravindranath Tagore* - "*Ekala chalo...* If nobody is helping you, you move alone, people will follow you. And you follow the path of Rigveda, move together."

In 1973, one senior Dada went to the jail in Patna to see Baba. One jail authority said to *Dada* that *Ananda* Marga philosophy was new and original. When *Dada* entered Baba's cell, Baba asked if there was any good news. Then the dada told "Baba, the senior Jail authority said that *Ananda Marga* philosophy and its Ideology is new and original". Then Baba said, do you know when people's thoughts, speech, and actions are the same then originality will come".

Later Baba explained the following three points:

1. There are three categories of human beings. The first category is that whose thoughts, words and deeds are the same, which means whatever they think they speak, and whatever they speak, they do. Such people are first-class people.
2. The second category comprises those whose thoughts and deeds are different, but whatever they speak, they do, which means they think one and speak another but they do whatever they say. Those are the second-class group.
3. The last category comprises those whose thoughts, words and deeds are different. What they think they do not say, what they say do not do. Their thoughts, words and deeds are never the same, thus they are the third class people.

My master followed the first rule for the whole of His life.

## An Obedient Son

"The only way to do service to one's mother after her death is to help every female member of the universe to progress on the path of highest development". The only way to do service to one's father after his death is to help every male member of the universe to progress on the path of highest development."

Devotion to His mother:

1. Baba had an extraordinary personality even so; He gave proper respect to everyone. When His mother was alive, if she became sick, Baba Himself massaged her legs. He always was ready to cooperate in her domestic work. When the younger brother was not able to bring things from the shop in Jamalpur, Baba Himself went shopping for everything in the house.

2. When He began service in Railway department (Jamalpur), my master, *Shri Prabhat Ranjan Sarkar*, gave His entire salary to His mother. After receiving the entire sum of money mother *Abharani* gave Rs. 5 to Baba for His pocket expenditure. With that money He used to buy fruits and sweets on the day of fasting for His mother. Like that, Baba served His mother. Baba never ate in hotels or restaurant and never even went to see films.

3. When my master formed *Ananda Marga* mission and started *Dharma Maha Cakra* program throughout India, He have to leave *Jamalpur* periodically. Like a most obedient and gentle boy, Baba asked His mother, "Mother, there is a program of *Dharma Maha Cakra* and the General Secretary of the organization has been requesting me to go out of *Jamalpur* for this purpose. May I go?" My master was a great philosopher and had millions of devotees, but He followed the rules as a simple organized person.

4. When He left *Jamalpur*, His mother objected that He should resign from the service first. First of all Baba used to take leave from the office and also the payment, Then Baba took leave with half payment, and then took leave without payment, and then lastly He resigned from the service.

5. *Thakur Ma* (Baba's mother) told Baba that He should not leave *Jamalpur* as His elder sister's daughter's marriage had not been yet arranged and that was His responsibility also. Baba followed His mother's order and did not leave *Jamalpur* until the marriage was arranged.

6. When He was in the *Patna* jail as under-trial prisoner under the false charge noted above, He got the news of His mother's death. My master gave application to the jail authority for leave on parole to perform the last rites of His mother, but the jail authorities did not give permission and finally, the application was rejected. Baba could not perform the last rites of His mother. (It is custom of India, that the eldest son performs the last rites for their parent.)

## Genius student

"One will not be able to know anything unless one develops the psychology of "I know not". It is the fundamental spirit of a true aspirant".

When my master was four years old His name, *Arun* was changed to *Prabhat Ranjan Sarkar*, and His father enrolled Him in the *Keshopur* Colony in the lower primary school, *Jamalpur*. My master's father was secretary of that school. He was very brilliant in education. What the teacher taught was enthusiastically understood. He took an interest in reading everything. He was silent, brave, and disciplined in the school, the teacher was very happy with His behavior.

When Baba was to get admission in high school He was too young to be admitted, so in His certificate, the year of His birth was reduced from 21st May 1921 to 1922. We don't have much information about His primary age. But when He came into high school, He stood first in the class and went to Calcutta. While

He was in high school, He had one local teacher who was giving private tuition to Him. His name was *Ram Chandra Jha*. Sometimes Baba would ask many questions regarding Sanskrit and English. His teacher was sometimes surprised at the depth of His understanding. Once he said, "*Prabhat* is a master of the English and Sanskrit languages". When Baba joined Vidyasagar College in Calcutta, He was engaged in three works. First, His studies; second, in a part-time working as sub-editor of "*Amrita Bazar Patrika*" (newspaper), He was writing articles and submitting to the paper. Thirdly, in spare time He was teaching classical Indian dance.

Once Baba said that whatever amount He earned from His part-time jobs, that money was given to help the poor students.

This is a practical teaching for all students. Everyone can utilize some spare time to earn money and that money can help the poor. This is a teaching of my master by His own action. Baba said He started Prabhat *Samgiit*, when I was seven years old, the song; "I love this tiny Green Island" was made. Once when visitors came to Baba's family house, His mother told them, "My son is very bright." Mother called Baba and asked Him to write a poem. Baba told a lengthy poem without any hesitation.

One day, I was on a field walk with Baba, at that time, Baba told two stories of His student life. He said, "When I was a student of *Vidyasagar* College (Calcutta), I went my home, *Jamalpur*. It was wintertime. I was sitting on a chair in the front of my house (in the sun). I saw two women coming with some sweets. They came and put the sweets in front of me. The women had come to see me, so I called them but they could not come near to me. When I insisted, one woman came near me and told, 'Oh, boy, I am the lower-caste lady, I cannot come near you.' When she said that, I became so worried. Then I took the hands of both the ladies and called them to sit down next to me. Then I ate all the sweets they had brought for me. Baba then asked them many questions regarding their family, their children etc. Baba told them, 'If you require any money to educate your children, I have money. I can give you.' But the ladies refused. After that, both the lady left the house. My mother became very angry because I had eaten and talked with the lower caste ladies. She came and took my ear forcibly and I stood. Then she pulled me to the bathroom and made me sit down. She opened the tap and filled one bucket with water. My mother then came out from the bathroom and took a jar of Ganges water and poured it into the same bucket. So mother has given me a bath with that Ganges water, I was purified from those ladies. After that, my mother was cleaning the place where the ladies had sat using cow-dung. After that, my mother called me to sit for lunch. I was hungry but I was not ready to take her food. She asked why you are not eating. I was angry. Then I told my mother, from this moment, I am going to remove all casteism from the world. My mother said, you know, so many great men came on this earth. But none has been capable to remove the casteism, so how can you remove it? I told her, Okay, no great man has removed it yet. But your *Prabhat* will remove the casteism from the world. When I started *Ananda Marga*, *Pranay Kumar Chatterje* became an *Ananda Margii*, he was my General Secretary. But *Pranaya Kumar Chatterjes* father was my father's priest. Therefore, according to tradition this *Pranaya Kumar Chatterje* should have been my priest. This being so, when *Pranaya Kumar* would come anytime to my house, he would always be thinking, How will I take the leftover food of my master. One day, he came and again he was thinking how to get the leftover food from me. So I just ate something and I gave the entire remaining portion to *Pranaya Kumar*. So I told my mother, See your Pandit is eating my leftover food. So there is no more casteism. Your priest is eating from my plate. My mother realized that day and said it is true. Now I see everywhere that casteism is going to be removed.

When Baba was student in *Vidyasagar* College of Calcutta University, He had a job to teach music and dance. According to His history, Baba had no training or experience in classical dance, even so He was teaching. When my Master was in the class nine, His father *Laxminarayan* passed away, but mother, Abharani Sarkar was a very courageous woman. She decided to send Him for higher education to her brother in Calcutta.

### An ideal husband

At the time of Baba's marriage in 1959, the ceremony took place in *Chuchura* Court. After completing the marriage, Baba came to the house of His wife. Baba's mother-in-law wanted the marriage to be performed according to the local system. Baba refused. "When I have already married with *Uma* in the court, it is not required to do as per the local system." Then mother-in-law said to my master's cousin, what type of man your brother is? My master's cousin replied my brother has some principle. If your daughter will follow the same principle she will enjoy her whole life.

When my master's wife, *Uma Sarkar*, came to Jamalpur, one devotee asked Baba, What will we call your wife? Baba replied, she is my wife, so she becomes your mother. From that time, she became the mother of the *Ananda Marga* mission and she received the same respect as my master got from the devotees.

Baba's life was very busy. He did not take her to restaurants, cinemas or parks, but when He went for spiritual functions, He took her and gave her the same status as He had. Before giving general discourses she always came to the dais and also gave speech among the devotees, in this way, devotees had the same respect for her.

One day I was in Malaysia. One Didi, her name was *Uma*, asked question to me. "Dada ji, lord Shiva had a wife, Lord Krishna had a wife, but why did Baba leave His wife?" I simply replied Baba did not leave His wife; *Marga Mata* (the mother of the organization) left my master. "Then I explained the whole story".

There was DMC in 1970 in Calcutta. There was a big gathering. At this time, Baba transferred His P.A., from the post of P.A. to Madras Press Manager. *Ananda Marga* is a missionary organization. There is a system anybody at anytime can be transferred anywhere (from any post to any other post) in any place. First, you follow the system, then if you have any difficulties you can say your problems. There is no such system that one person will hold a particular post for all the time. According to *Caryacarya*, only the post of *Purodha Pramukh* is not changeable, it is for life long or he desires.

Baba's P.A. (personal assistant) requested Baba, 'Don't transfer me from the P.A. post. But Baba strongly objected his request and said, "In this organization, whatever transfer I made you will just follow accordingly." But he didn't follow it. The P.A. then requested to Mother, to appeal Baba on his behalf. At that time *Uma Sarkar* was in the house of her mother (*Bandel*). She came from her mother's house having received all news from the P.A. and she rushed to Baba's room. She had become very upset about Baba's sudden decision. *Uma Sarkar* requested Baba not to transfer *Vishokhananda* from the P.A. post. Baba replied, "There are two things. You are a mother and you have the right to love the children. You have the right to feed and take care of the boys and girls. I have no objection. Often you call the boys and girls from the field, I never asked why? You know I made the organization and I am working for my organization. So in my organizational activities you should not interfere. You must work in your jurisdiction. *Uma Sarkar* was upset and she did not want to listen anything from Baba. She told forcefully, "If you do not take my advice I will destroy your organization." Baba said 'alright'. I want to see how you are going to destroy my organization. As she left the room Baba called her back and

said, "You are my wife. It is my duty to bring you back. What you are setting up, you can do. There is no objection."

When my master came from Calcutta to *Ranchi*, many *dadas* said to Baba, Baba you have married her so you must keep her with you. So at the request of Dadas, Baba sent one dada who was very close to *Uma Dutta*, to Calcutta to bring her *Ranchi*. When the Dada came to her, she refused. (Baba did not leave His wife, but she left my master). "There is not a single mistake in the part of my Master. My master always paid respect to the women. I heard, while talking to the women He always used the term Ma (mother). He never addressed by name or any other terms.

One day when Baba was in the Nursing home of Calcutta. His nurse (*Manju*) suddenly asked Baba, I heard that you do not talk to the women. Baba replied, "Who told you that? I am talking with you. Is it not proof that I do talk with everyone? Baba said her; all the women of the universe are my daughters, so I have to talk to them. Baba has given equal respect to the women in our organization. According to the Indian religion, women of lower caste shouldn't listen the Veda mantras. If any women do so then she has to go under punishment. In Christianity also, the man will work as a priest but not the woman because there is dogma that Christ came as a man so only men can hold the post of priests and not the women. In the Muslim religion they say that five ladies are equivalent to one male. But in Ananda Marga, there is an equal right for the women. Baba has initiated the men (man and woman both). Even the Kapalika Sadhana was given to the both man and the woman. Women can also give initiation. Baba has given equal social responsibilities to the ladies and gents. According to Ananda marga there should not be any discrimination in this sphere.

Baba has given many articles related to the women and their rights. When Baba was on His European tour, a question arose, many sisters wanted to have P.C. (personal Contact) with Baba but Baba refused. Baba said, 'When I make a rule, I do follow it first. I have made a rule that workers will not meet a lady in a lonely place. So I am also to follow. Therefore, I am not going to give personal contact to any women individually. But I will give P.C. collectively; my blessing is equal for both male and female". All women became happy. Baba gave personal contacts collectively and everybody received His blessings as it was their own.

In the time of *Dharma Samiiksa*, there was collective *Dharma Samiiksa* also for the women. Baba use to punish and remove the *samskaras* of the *Sadhaka* individually or in a group.

Once, when Baba was scolding a women worker, someone said, "How can Baba scold a lady like that?" Baba replied, "When anyone comes in front of me, I am looking at his or her qualities. I don't look whether it is a girl or a boy".

### Strict Administrator, His Government Job

In the year 1941, Baba joined the service in *Jamalpur* Railway workshop. While working in the accounts department, He started helping others in very miraculous way. People came to Him in large number to have their palms read and to discover their future.

My Master used a special mirror for telling fortunes; the people named it 'The magic mirror". But doing so, after utilizing the spiritual power He use to fall sick. Mother does not like the super human activities. One day, His aunt came inside the room. She had a problem regarding her son. Baba sows her in the mirror; her son was seen standing on a ship. The aunt was surprised and happy at the same time. But my master felt seriously ill. After this incident mother *Abha Ranii* was so annoyed that she broke the mirror into small pieces.

During His lunch hour, Baba was explaining many things

amongst colleagues:

a. I love you as well as I like you. He explained, you should love all but you may not like all since liking is your personal affair.

b. Bhavisaca—*Bha* stands for Bhagavan (God); before starting any work you should take His name. *Vi* stands for Vishvash (Faith); have faith in Him. *Sa* stands for Samyam (Self-control); you should have Samyam in every walk of life. *Ca* stands for Caritra (Character); it must be flawless, that of the highest order.

c. Baba gave advice to government servants: Be fearless, don't be talkative, give firm decisions, don't keep unnecessary things, and don't indulge in unfair means. What money has to come will certainly come. So adopting unfair means is not an ideal act.

According to the duties allotted to Him, He had to audit the stores and accounts section of the Eastern Railway offices under Jamalpur. His sense of responsibility was unparalleled, be it in His personal life, organizational affairs, or office duties. Only after discharging His due responsibilities, He used to accomplish other duties. He was giving special importance to *Samyak Karmanta* of Lord Buddha. It is needless to mention that in every field of Baba's life, this *Samyak Karmanta* was put into practice.

This is an incident concerning Baba's auditing tour. As per the instructions concerning His supervisory officer, Baba had to inspect some stores of the railway workshop. Baba wanted to inform the storekeeper-in-charge in advance. Accordingly, He telephoned him. A junior official answered the phone. Baba said, "I am *P. R. Sarkar*, speaking from H.Q. I am coming to inspect your store tomorrow. So let me talk to the in-charge of the store."

This worked as a bolt from the blue for the person on the telephone. He cried out, "Baba, *P. R. Sarkar* is coming for inspection?" Baba could easily understand the fear in the mind of the person. Keeping the receiver on the table, the gentleman went to call the in-charge. The in-charge came and revealed his identity on the phone. Baba asked him with a bit of resentment, "Well, how is it that your assistant was about to have a heart attack hearing about my visit for inspection? Why should he yell, 'Baba, *P. R. Sarkar* is coming for inspection'? Well, am I a tiger to be so much afraid of?"

The in-charge expressed regret for that and gave his due consent for inspection. Really, it was not without reason for the person to be so much scared about the inspection by *P. R. Sarkar*. Baba said, "When I go for inspection, I usually ask to show such things where there are problems and discrepancies. I had to look in the heaps of records to locate the problems. They could not escape me even though they were showing extra things; giving logic cannot divert my mind. They were never successful in their efforts, nor was it possible to please me by feeding rich dishes or by other unfair means as is done in the case of other officials. During one inspection, I did not accept even a cup of tea or cold drink or anything, lest accepting their offer obliges me. Furthermore, I avoided the same because I did not like to give them this privilege. At last, all the discrepancies were caught red-handed and they had nothing to say. Finding no alternative, they had to beg excuses and to plead for undue favours with tears in their eyes. They said, 'Sarkar Babu, I was compelled to do all these due to pressures of economic problems. Please do not get me discharged from my service; I have a large family to maintain, all the members of my family will starve to death.'"

Baba said that at that time He was in a fix. He was neither able to supply any relief showing undue favor to the defaulter, nor was He able to discharge the person from service. In His entire service life, there were rare examples of discharging anyone from a job. When wealth enters the home, at the same time

sin also enters from another door, in this way earning of more wealth is the cause of the downfall of a person. The sin sings, "Give me food", and you will feed him. If you stop feeding sin will eat you up then."

Once Baba told an incident that, "I was working in Railway office at *Jamalpur* and received the assignment to investigate the accounts of other employees who had been suspected of some illegitimate dealings. The man learned of my investigation and knowing that he would lose his job if his activities came to light, he approached me and asked me not to expose him as he had a family to feed and could not afford to lose his job. Baba told him that He could not consider his request as it would be duty. Nonetheless, when Baba handed His report the man lost his job, Baba helped him with some money from His own pocket to support his family for a week and during that time Baba arranged another job for him."

Many people were getting railway passes from rail department to travel from one place to another. The T.C. (Ticket Checker) not punch the pass, it can be used another time. Baba said that it was not good when once you travel on the pass whether T.C. punched the pass or not, that ticket should not be used anymore. If anybody does this then he/she is cheating.

My master never compromises with injustice in office life because His work was updated; He never left pending work behind.

Baba gave to juniors the same status as of a younger brother. But He wanted them to work in the office honestly. He did not like idleness, he was very strict. He always told junior subordinates that everyone should come in time and go in time, because no authority can change anyone He should do work honestly. If any difficulty arises tell me frankly, I will help you. If anybody wants to go out, please inform me. My master was always careful of subordinate workers. My master worked twenty-six years in the railway workshop.

### A Responsible Brother/Guardian

When my master was in the ninth class, His father passed away. He went to Calcutta for two years and completed intermediate. His mother wanted Him to have higher education, but because of deep sense of family responsibility, He left education earlier and return to *Jamalpur*. Because He was eldest in the family, no one was then earning member in the family. He came and joined the same department where His father was working in the railway workshop. At that time, mother was the guardian of the family, therefore, whatever salary He got, He gave it to His mother, as she was maintaining the family expenditures.

When my master was 14 years old, once He went to His sister's house and with all seriousness, made a promise, "I will also give you gold ornament". Everybody laughed at such a promise by a young boy. His sister also took it lightly, but my master never forgets His promise. In 1989, my master sent a box of ornaments reminding her of His promise.

My master was busy in ideological, organizational activities, even though He was extremely busy in His life yet had kept the place for the worldly relatives. It whatever is very difficult for common people to uphold.

One day He said, "I'm not talking and not giving any advice when I'm taking a bath and putting water over my head, because if I speak, the water will come in my mouth. So this time only I'm not working". Rest of the time He was utilizing each and every moment of His life, even while eating. This is very small thing, but very educative. Baba would never throw away anything, not even a small piece of soap. Whenever a new soap was given to Him He would shape the small piece into the new and then use it as one piece. Such proper application was in every aspect of His life.

*Shri Manas Ranjan Sarkar*, His younger brother had

some experiences concerning His guardianship:

He was very regular in following His routine, never such occasion came in the life of us brothers when we either clashed or maintained any difference of opinion. Baba never scolded me, hence slight indifference on His part would hurt me immensely. Once, I wanted to purchase a lottery ticket and He forbade me, the reason being that it will create greediness. At the time of mother's sickness, He attended her needs personally and more often massaged her legs. While playing in the neighborhood, the children often beat me, Baba scolded me, "If you continue retreating like this in the beginning, later you will not find any place to fall back further." Baba always taught us to be gentle, to stand against all sorts of injustice. To become a good citizen, we must overcome all sorts of weakness and should never yield to any temptation of worldly life. We found in "Baba" an ideal guardian, an ideal brother and an ideal son.

### A Disciplined Soldier

When my master served in the accounts department of the railways, second World War was in full swing. At that time, India was under British rule. The British Indian Government issued a notice that every government servant must join the Territorial Army training who is in between the ages of 18 to 25. That was compulsory for everyone; He joined and for training went to *Assam*, *Bandal* (Bengal).

One day, when He was taking a field walk, He told one story: Once, while camping in the jungle, Baba was with two other companions in the same tent, named Mohan and Mukherji. Mukherjii was greedy and static in nature, Mohan was covetous and mutative, and Baba alone was sentient there. Baba said, whatever food we will manage to procure, we should divide amongst ourselves, but I think Mukherjii will never do so.

During the Holi (offering of the color) festival, Mukherjii went home and brought a box full of sweets back with him. He kept it under his cot and whenever Baba and Mohan gathered to chat, Mukherjii would eat the sweet in front of them. One day, *Mohan* conspired with Baba. He said, 'Prabhat, you are a genius, can you do something to get the sweets? Baba instructed, "Go to dinner as usual at 8.00 p.m. and I will come half an hour later. You return to the tent, entering from the rear. When I come, I will signal you by coughing. Immediately, you must create a rattling sound. When I give another cough, leave the tent through the rear and return only after half an hour. Mohan did as Baba said. That night Baba came with Mukherjii to the tent after dinner. He coughed and suddenly, there were some noises coming from the tent. Then he told Mukherjii, "There is a wild animal in our tent attracted by your sweets! Mukherjii asked worriedly, "Prabhat, what shall we do now?" Baba told him to get a bamboo stick. When he returned, Baba again signaled Mohan. Mukherjii came with the bamboo stick, giving it to Baba. Baba swung the bamboo around inside the tent and said, "The animal has gone now." When Baba entered with Mukherjii, the place was a mess. Some sweets were scattered around. Mukherjii asked, "What to do with the sweets, shall I keep them?" Baba warned him that he feared that the animal had infected the sweets. Then Mukherjii asked, "Then shall I throw them away?" Baba replied, "No, Instead you give all the sweets to *Mohan* even if he refuses." *Mukherjii* acted accordingly and in his absence, Baba and Mohan had eaten the sweets. Baba remarked that, "If one deprives an exploiter or greedy person of their belongings by hook or crook and uses them in a benevolent way for the society's welfare, then it is not a crime". This is the policy of PROUT.

### Maximum Utilization

Time:

In His student life, from the early days His life style was unique. He always utilized time in a maximum and proper way.

When He had completed His studies, taken rest and then gives classes in music. He also uses to give guidance in different subjects to the students of upper and lower classes. Any money He got from music classes, He utilized it to give aid to the poor students in the form of books and other necessary things.

During leave time, He always wrote articles for *Amrit Bazar Pratika*. He also wrote many articles for different magazines.

During government service, on holy days, He utilized the time for *Dharma Pracar*. During His lunch time of half an hour, He was among the office staff, discussing different subjects on devotion and philosophy of life. During office hours, He paid particular attention to His work so that there was never any pending work on His table.

During organizational life, His life was more systematic and methodical.

Example—Once He said when I put water on my head, I do not talk except this, I am always giving advice while attending to any personal duties. Mostly, my master was doing two or three duties at the same time; while doing personal duties, He would be giving dictation.

During field walk in the garden, He does four or five duties at a time, 1. field work, 2. dictation, 3. listening *Prabhat Samgiit* or correcting its mistake, 4. giving *darshan* to the devotees.

During lunch, He gave time for the worldly relatives. Sometimes during the meal, He would tell us many things about the meal itself; how to cook and the different types of food.

During jail life, He taught the workers maximum utilization of their time. Even during jail life, He maintained proper routine.

During the period at nursing home, doctors advised Him not to talk with the workers, to take reports and giving *Prabhat Sangiit*. My master declared it was impossible to stop all these things; He would not take reports but He would continue to give some advice. "To give *Prabhat Sangiit* is not a problem for me as I don't feel any trouble."

Baba went to the nursing home twice but never stop giving *Prabhat Sangiita*, some time He gave 65 *Prabhat Sangiita* in a day.

When He was moving from one place to another, He was utilizing the time in teaching about the particulars of the land of that locality, He also explains about the environment, food, habits, language, economic and political history, and geography of that particular area etc.

Once He visited *Ananda Nagar*, *Deoghar*, *Patna*, *Betia*, *Gorakhpur*, *Kanpur*, *Agra*, *Delhi*, *Jammu*, *Bilaspur*, *Jaipur*, *Gwalior*, *Allahabad*, *Varanasi*, *Daltainganj*, *Ranchi*, *Tatanagar* and *Calcutta*. During these journeys, He introduced *Prabhat Sangiit*, delivered spiritual and social discourses. He also said about the impact and importance of historical, socio-economic and culture of the area. He gave more than 500 *Prabhat Sangiit* during this tour.

### Maximum utilization of the mundane things:

Slippers—After His great departure, the organization decided to recover all-important or non-important things from the different parts of the world related to Him. Things like stories, photos, missing articles so many things were collected. Someone also found a slipper that had become very old and many nails had been used to repair it. This slipper was found in *Raipur* from the house of *Acarya Kailaschand Bhalla jii*.

In 1968, when Baba had gone to *Raipur*, *Dada Kailaschand* had asked Baba for His old slippers and has given new slippers. Baba at first did not agree to the change, but when the devoted *Acaryas* became more persuasive, Baba agreed to change. One day while we were sitting in *Jamalpur ashram*, Baba said to *Dada Dasarath*, "have you seen my past life, what was I doing seven thousand years ago?" *Dada Dasarath* told Baba, "Lord *Sada Shiva* is sitting in the mountain." Then Baba

said, "Tell us about 3500 years ago; what are you seeing?" Dada Dasaruth replied Lord Krishnn sitting on a branch of a Kadant tree on the bank of the *Yamuna* River. Baba then asked Dasarath what is His dress. Dasarath replied that He was dressed as a Prince. Baba then told Dasarath, "Open your eyes." Then Baba drew our attention to His own shirt, which was torn and said, "You have seen that how I was in past and now I am using old clothes with stitching." He show us how He was very simple.

Nothing was meaningless or unimportant to Him—When soap becomes very small, generally people throw it out; but Baba used to attach it with a new soap and continued using.

His working style was very scientific and methodical—One day, during reporting session, He told us that even if there was a blackout, even absolutely no light, He would still find everything in His room. He knew exactly where He kept His pen, keys, money, handkerchief, etc., because He was keeping everything in a proper way. He used some words for remembrance. Once He told P.A., "When I go for bath, I will not ask for *Lungi*, Towel and Soap, one by one. Rather I will say only LUTASO, it means LU=*Lungi*, TA=Towel, So=Soap.

### Monk Life and the Missionary Life of My Master

(If there is even small hole in the dam, the whole dam will destroyed)—While in jail, Baba told a story regarding the sacrifices of one's life. He told about a small hole in the base of a dam that threatened to burst at any time; causing its complete destruction; similarly, any weakness in a missionary's character threatens the sanctity and livelihood as a servant of God. Baba emphatically said that a missionary must uphold the virtue and spirit of their character to be a true devotee. Baba explained that once upon a time, a king went to a forest to relax. One day, the king noticed smoke streaming towards the sky and ordered the minister to find out where the fire is? So, the minister and two soldiers journeyed to find the smoke and while approaching, came upon a monk meditating inside a ring of fire that engulfed his surroundings. The young Sadhu's body glistened with sweat and was glowing with inner light; his face was very bright and a powerful vibration pervaded their area. He was undergoing severe penance and an arduous life in the quest for the supreme. The minister was awestruck at the supernatural sight and reported to the king. Upon hearing the news, the king became outraged and ordered that monk to come bow before him as he claimed the Sadhu was verily a subject of the kingdom. The minister knew that this was impossibility as he knew the monk was solely bowed to the supreme. The king was an egotist and retold his wish to the minister. So, the minister devised a way to bring about the downfall of the supreme character of the monk. In an attempt to allure the monk out of deep meditation, the minister hired a beautiful prostitute. Everyday, she would bring delicious food to the forest and call out the monk's name. One day his eyes remained closed as he was absorbed in the supreme and would not reply to the mistress call. However, after sometime, the sweetness of the woman's voice began to cause an internal struggle in the monk's mind and he desired to open his eyes, but he knew intuitively that the woman had inimical intentions. The temptation finally overcame the monk as he desired delicious food, which he had not taken for a long time. The monk devoured and thoroughly enjoyed the delicious food and began to take food daily. After some time, the woman convinced the monk to come to her place to eat as it was too arduous for her to continue to bring the food to the jungle. Soon the monk began to go to the lady's home and eventually fallen from the path. In due course of time he had three children. Thus, he now had the duties of a family man and had no choice but to provide for his household responsibilities. However, as a monk he had never worked laboriously before and could not even comprehend a strenuous life. The wife became angered and he

could no longer neglect his family duty. So, he thought to go before the king in hopes that the king, out of kindness and pity, would help him. So, one day, he took his children in his arms and bowed before the king. Because many years had passed, the king had forgotten his desire to cause the downfall of the monk. The monk looked like he had become aged seemingly like eons, king did not recognize him the minister then explained he is the same monk who was lost in the thought of the supreme and neglected the king one day.

Baba explained that many temptations arise that make it difficult to maintain the character and dignity of a Sanaynsi. The yogi must develop single pointedness of mind to overcome these natural desires and transfer these crude longings towards the supreme. Baba concluded the one who devotes their existence for the missionary path must always uphold the virtue of a true sanyasi in all the aspects of life.

### The life of a Missionary:

During one reporting session in the early 1970's in *Ranchi*, Baba described the essential character of a missionary snnayasi. He told a story about a small town in the eastern part of India called *Mokama*. It is situated on the banks of the Ganges river, which during the rainy season would flood the town. One year the Ganges parted and formed a small island near *Mokama*. A monk stayed on this island with his disciples. His closest devotees would go to *Mokama* by boat to bring food as alms daily.
