One day, three Englishmen wished to follow the monk's back to the island and attempted to cross the shore to reach the boat because of deep mud on the banks of the river the monks humbly decided that they would lie down and the man would cross the mud. One by one, they reached the boat, but as the last man crossed over the youngest monk, a nail in the man's shoes caused him to scream "ouch". As the boat began to traverse the water, a strong wind capsized the boat and all three men were drowned. After the monk came back the Guru began to beat severely the young monk who had cried out in pain. Baba further said that He was present there and had witnessed the beating and He inquired that why he is beating? The guru replied that as a monk he had expressed his pain, Mother Nature was summoned and had created a strong wind to punish them so three man had lost their lives.

Baba explained that missionaries cannot express pain, grief or sad emotions, when they are at service to the humanity because it lower them into a limited plane of thought which creates these states through a reactive mind. Rather one should keep oneself absorbed in the bliss and will of the supreme; verily are these sufferings a blessing as they make the *sannyasi* strong and adapt to a hard life. Undoubtedly, Baba Himself felt pain and tortures given by others but never spoke about His pain. He always remained loving and forgiving, thus exemplifying the true spirit of a missionary who alleviates the suffering of others while ignoring one's own.

### Maintaining Missionary Life:

During Baba's world tour in 1979, one monk asked the secret to remain focused and successful in the organization. Baba gave four points to be followed that would ensure perseverance in the *Marga*:

(1) Keep enough money so that you can maintain existence without struggle.
(2) Don't keep too much money as it would lead to unnecessary luxury and misuse. Whatever comes, use it as soon as possible in good work?
(3) Keep your mind aloof from the opposite sex.
(4) Follow the organizational structure so that development may be efficient.

Through these codes, Baba said that one could work with maximum efficiency towards the propagation of the spiritual ideology while maintaining physical existence without unnecessary strife. By creating a balanced existence, one could

be a true *Sadavipra*.

### An Organizer

There are several types of organizers in the society.

1. The ordinary organizer (an ordinary organizer is one who organizes a small group of employees in some factories or companies, etc, and launches a movement to get their demands fulfilled. For example, trade unionists.
2. The great Organizer– The great organizer is one who takes greater responsibility and organizes for the fulfillment of a particular objective of a country.
3. The universal organizer– The universal organizer is he who

organizes the world in a particular aspect of life and gives a particular direction to the entire world.
4. Master organizer– Master organizer is only Mahasadvipra, who teaches all the aspects of individual and collective lives of the entire human society, that is, spiritual, social, economic, educational, cultural and political according to the standard of collective psychology. He brings revolutionary changes in all the branches of social life, this master organizer comes only when the society falls down to the state of degeneration, where it goes beyond the capacity of ordinary, great and universal organizer.

When people lose even a ray of hope for their survival due to rampant exploitation in all branches of social life, when they pray Paramapurusa in the wilderness for their shelter and security up from degeneration to disruption, to derangement to its original state and then gives acceleration to the social movement their We find Lord Sada Shiva, Krishna and Mahasadvipra my master (*P.R. Sarkar*) a great organizers. The basic qualities of an organizer:

1. An organizer bears burdens and remains ever cheerful– When Baba was in Patna, the cell of Patna jail was a type of cave. There was no window, no ventilation and Patna is not the suitable place for summer. Baba faced all these inconvenience there, but when anybody went to jail to see Baba. He was always in a cheerful mood. Once when I met Him in Patna jail, when I saw the physical condition of Baba, I started weeping. Baba told me, why you are weeping my child? I'm in the jail happily accepting all the difficulties: I saw His whole life; He faced lot of struggle but was always cheerful.
2. He made cadres and followers to feel that He belongs to them and them alone.

 He had relationship between Him and cadres, either of sons and daughters or fathers or Guru or disciple. He educated every cadre (boys and girls) as sons and daughters. During reporting, He made to feel all that are their brothers and sisters. There has been an incident in 1963, when organization was very small. A worker was posted in Andhra Pradesh (India). There was a system; that every worker was getting pocket allowance of 60 Rupees from the center. That worker couldn't get money from the center in time. The money was sent by money order, but he was starving as he didn't get it. One day, baba suddenly stopped eating. People asked; "Baba, why are you not eating?" Baba said, "You know my worker is not getting food in the field. What right do I have to eat food myself?" After a day, when that worker got money, Baba started eating again.
3. Organizer's behavior is a most changing one– When He was in jail; His work was progressing as usual. Due to His most charming behavior, the eight Ananda margiis missionary immolated them to protest against immorality. Dada Divyananda at Patna, India, Dineshvarananda at Delhi, India, Akulananda at Bakipur, Uma, *Lokesh*, in Germany, *Gagan* Deo in Dallas, Texas, Didi Asitima, Manila, Philippines. All these brothers and sisters immolated themselves.

 Once, when my master was on the tour of Europe, the Denmark government refused His entry into the country. He was; at; the airport together with six other workers and more than 600 other disciples were waiting outside to receive Him. When they realized that Baba is refused for entry, they started to demonstrate outside the airport in Copenhagen. They started Kiirtan and performed Kaoshiki and Tandava (in full swing). The airport authorities then gave Him the chance to give general Darshan (meeting) to His devotees. But he was not ready to do that. The Margiis continued their demonstration and once again the authorities requested Shrii Shrii Anandamurti to give general Darshan and finally He agreed. Baba was to be allowed

to give general *Darshan* in the V.I.P. lounge, and also the police were to keep away from the lounge for at least one hour. The authorities agreed and allowed the margiis to enter the V.I.P. lounge where the lecture went on smoothly for 45 minutes.

When the margiis entered the V.I.P. lounge, everybody used to do *Sastanga Pranam* and then sit in front of Baba. Just a minute before, they were all shouting, but now in front of Baba, everybody was very silent. Then one airport authority said to P.A., "Your master has magic power!" Due to His charming power, everybody is silent. Even the airport authorities were hypnotized by His personality.

His love and compassion – When our headquarters was in *Ranchi*, work was in full swing due to the seminar years. One of the senior workers complained about one lazy worker. Baba listened to the worker and told him two things. "Do you know I am always calling come, come! But never told anybody go back home." Then Baba inquired, "Is he going to market, is he shopping or not? He is moving around the city in his uniform or not?" The senior worker replied that yes Baba he does; then Baba smiled and said that was his way of doing organizational duty, at least people are recognizing he is a monk of *Ananda Marga*. When the workers heard the kindness and love of Baba towards him, tears roll down over his cheeks. (There are several stories about His love and kindness.)

Master as an organizer – He possesses infinite tolerance, forgiveness, and magnanimity of mind. Baba's body was very delicate. In the Patna jail, in cell number 16 there was no window, no ventilation. Patna is the hottest city of Bihar. He faced the entire furor. He forgot to take any revenge against them and He forgave all those who committed wrong with Baba. All that he had done was due to the magnanimity of his mind.

He respected others' sentiments – Once He said, "If you are criticizing any religion or any person, you are criticizing me." Once, He was on tour in the north part of India. At the request of many margiis there, He went to one old temple. Before entering the temple, He removed His shoes, then He entered the temple.

He bears every one's shortcomings – During the state emergency in India, *Ananda Marga* was banned, my master was in jail, and so were most of the workers and important margiis; others were underground. During this time, the government was trying to start a case against Baba. They thought, it is a better opportunity to crush Ananda Marga when Baba is inside jail.

My master, Shrii Shrii Anandamurti, appealed to the court saying that His workers are in jail and others had gone underground. The mission had all its possessions seized. Also there was improper communication with His lawyer and no finances to handle His case. Because of these things, lift the ban on His mission, and then He would have no problem in handling the case. The court did not agree and refused my master's appeal.

Then my master appealed to the Indian Parliament. They too refused. Belongings and car were sold to fight the case with the government.

During the emergency regime the court declared many things concerning my master and He spent many years in prison. In this way He had to face all types of struggle and difficulties in His life.

An organizer must feel the pulse of His cadres and public – From 4th July 1974 to 22nd March 1977, the history of Ananda Marga is filled with so many ups and downs. Though the Indian Government had banned Ananda Marga, arrested all their important workers and more than fifty percent of members who were out of jail went underground, yet the rest of the members continued to work strongly. They were collecting news, publishing papers and circulating among the margiis and sympathizers. We were getting news from Baba and other margiis in jail. Baba was also sending messages to us, time to

time. At the same time, one worker wanted to go to Manila. He needed permission from Baba, and sent a letter to Him in jail. After some time, the reply came on the same notepaper, "I want to see that this worker reaches there as soon as possible." The worker went to Manila and worked in Philippines during the emergency period. My master knows the spirit of the workers. He inspired them and they worked accordingly.

**An organizer is always fair, sympathetic and humanistic to His cadre and public—** Early in the 1960's there was a DMC. One night, which was very cold, two workers had no blankets. They were both in Baba's place and did not wish to disturb Him with their requirement. They instead covered themselves with a very thin cotton sheet, and their bodies were drawn up from the chill. Suddenly, Baba got up, came out from His bedroom and saw the two workers sleeping with only the thin cotton sheet. Baba got His own blanket and covered them. They became warm and slept nicely. When they awoke in the morning, they were surprised to find the blanket covering them and they asked Baba who had given the blanket. Baba replied, "How can I sleep when my sons have no blanket? Rightfully, it is my first duty to arrange blankets for my sons."

Baba won the heart of His workers. Both were ever happy and in full of joy. My master was always sympathetic and humanistic to His cadre and public.

**Service and sacrifice are the qualities of an organizer—** When my master was a student of high school, there was an earthquake in Monghyr, (Bihar Province, India), six kilometers from Jamalpur. My master formed a relief team and worked with His father in Monghyr. His whole organization was started only to do service. It is an example in His case that He left railway government service saying He did not need to do service in railway department further. When some friends asked Him regarding His service of Jamalpur, He replied no service I left for the suffering humanity. He worked day and night for the mission.

Once, in 1965, I was with Him in the car, coming from Jamalpur to Bhagalpur, as we passed Nath Nagar, I saw two tin sheds. One had been built by the Rotary Club and another by the Lions Club. The sheds provide shelter for people during bad weather. Baba told the history of the sheds; He said that the Rotary Club built the first shed; then the Lions built the other. When there are such competitions in social service the people are benefited enormously. He said, "There should be similar competition in doing social services amongst my sons and daughters."

**An organizer accommodates multidimensional minds—** This world has so many different religion, race, color, caste, creed and complexes. So we need such leader who can unite all. Requires strong mind that can control all the minds.

My master started this mission in 1955 with the help of family devotees. In 1962, He introduced monk (full time workers) system. In 1962, He started international Pracar. From that very moment in our mission, people from many different religious group joined *Ananda Marga*. From many different caste and color people came to join. All the different cultures had come to meet in *Ananda Marga*. How was this possible, because thousands of different minds were working behind Him? He knew the psychology, weaknesses and language of a person. My master touched the pulse and solved the problems of every one. Margii became His heart and limbs.

Due to His love and affections, many workers immolated during His incarceration.

Due to His thousands of different minds, He brought man, women, students, teachers, farmers, intellectuals, non-intellectuals and business people all into one platform.

**An organizer should have confidence, strong conviction, indomitable optimism and firm determination— During the imprisonment of Baba at *Buxar Central Jail, (Bihar)*,**

MR. *Hengarani*, the CBI (Central Bureau of Investigation) officer, tried to persuade him to abandon the idea of implementing PROUT. The officer pointed out that Baba's wife and several workers had left Him and many more were losing faith. The CBI chief added that the government was willing to provide Baba, with a luxurious house and ashram in the mountains, if He agreed to retire and confine Him only to spiritual topics.

My master sharply and with firm determination, replied, "When I'm in physical form, I cannot leave PROUT. If my wife left me, it is my personal problem. For the workers who left, I'd simply like to thank them for the work they have done in my organisation!"

Then He said boldly, "*Mr. Hengarani*, you look into your case. When I am acquitted and released, my organization will grow ten times bigger than at present." This is only one example I am putting here. Every step in His life is a firm determination.

An organizer must have an iron hand, an elastic mind, and a loving heart— It is difficult to find an iron hand, elastic mind and loving heart altogether in any single person. In my master's case He had all these qualities. During a reporting session, Baba said, "If your ideology is gigantic, the problems encountered will also be gigantic. You must keep your mind like a wood apple, and not like a mango fruit. Do you know the mango skin, it is very soft and any bird can eat, but in the case of the wood apple, no one can destroy. If your goal is extensive, your journey will take time. You must be like a wood apple".

During *Jamalpur*, one day one worker went to Baba's quarters to show a copy of a newly printed book. Baba looked at it and found a printing error. He rebuked the worker immediately. When the worker had gone, Baba said to one devotee, "Sometimes people think I am very severe, but in reality I am severe only outwardly, not internally. I am like a wood apple (a kind of fruit in India which is covered with a hard shell and is very good for the stomach) you see, if I am not hard from outside, then I would not be able to get the work done in the proper way. The wood apple is so sweet that if nature does not give it a hard shell, then the crow will cut it right off the tree."

In 1968, when our organization's headquarter was in *Ranchi*, Baba went to *Netarhat (Bihar)* for rest. After His evening walk, He entered His room and immediately came out again. He quoted a line from a *Hindi* film song '*Jalane wala jala kare, kismat mere sath hai*' (Those who are jealous of me, I don't care at all, my luck is with me)

**The best organizer is ever vigilant—** The 70's were some difficult times for my master. There were struggles with both central and state government. My master left *Ranchi* in May, 1971 and went to Patna. On the 4th June, 1971, the police raided the office and jagrti. They were searching for bombs but found nothing. They again raided the same place on the 21st June and this time they declared that some things were found. On these ground they arrested Baba in *Patna* on June 26, 1971. The C.B.I., the political police of India, brought Baba to *Ranchi*. The *Ranchi* court at midnight on the 27th was ready to get evidence on my master, but the *Ranchi* police had no such documents or charge sheets against Him. The court then freed my master. Once again the police wanted to arrest Him, but this time Baba was under treatment in a cottage in *Patna* hospital and left before the police arrived.

Baba said, "The central and state governments have failed to destroy *Ananda Marga* and *Ananda Margiis*. Now this time, all powers of India and K.G.B. want to arrest me personally, so keep my suitcase always ready." Baba then gave instructions so that the organization can run in His absence. In this way my master was always vigilant.

When He was in jail, a month before He sent a message to all the *margiis*, "I see that what is coming is not good, so

everybody must set aside one year's ration for their family." Nobody understood what was going on but after six months an emergency was declared and *Ananda Marga* was banned. Many *margiis* were either in jail or underground. Because of the prior warning by Baba, many families had accumulated rations and were able to cope though the main family member was in jail. This was very vigilant of my master.

Once Baba said, "You know if you people were not vigilant, this government could have crucified me like Christ."

He directly or indirectly kept His cadres and followers in His touch. In the very beginning the wholetimer system was not introduced, the reporting system was also not given. Baba always kept the family Acarya with Him. In 1962, when the wholetimer system begun, Baba started reporting system. Baba had given RDS (Review, Defect and Solution) and IRSS (Inspection, Review, Solidarity and Structure). Reporting was given for both workers and followers. Through reporting, Baba checked the 16 points. He taught many philosophical things and gave proper ideas of the field. He taught the workers to be a leader in the field. If any worker would not come, Baba inquires why he does not come, what is the difficulty with him? He was dealing in this way and in other side report of the work was taken. That system is still going on in our mission. Our Master was always linked with the common *Sadhakas* and the workers.

An organizer always keeps His cadres and followers active and dynamic with newer constructive programs and healthy sentience. My master used to change programs within a period of six months in a very dynamic way. When our headquarters was in *Ranchi*, He announced Sadhana year (demonstration year), next was Seminar years. This process would go up to the village level. The Katha Kiirtan year (Utilization year), As well as He also gave new terminology in organization, like RDS, SSCK (Structural Seminar Cadre *Kiirtan* in 1967 to 1971), UHH (Universal humanity heart), RCP (right time, continuous effort, proper finishing), LC (local cadre), LFT (Local Full Timer), LPT (Local Part Timer), PKB (*Purna Kalik Bandhu*), EC (Elected Cadre), BP (*Bhukti Pradhan*), UBP (*Upa Bhukti Pradhan*), GMP (*Gram Pramukh*) etc.

If an organizer has the above mentioned qualities, He will be successful in His mission. The master organizer, Shrii *P.R. Sarkar* had all the above qualities and many more. To describe all of them is beyond the power of my pen.

My master came on this planet, when the society was in a state of degeneration. He was a preceptor, philosopher, and a master organizer as well. He had a colorful and mysterious personality.

Shrii *P.R. Sarkar* came with a revolutionary philosophy or ideology, discarding all the narrow and dogmatic concepts. To Him, human society is one and indivisible. He proclaims, "*Paramapurusa* is our father, *Parama Prakrti* is our mother and we are the members of this cosmic family." Therefore, to establish one human society, there is need to build a great world. There should be the world government. For the formation of world government, collective psychology is needed. This is the historical interpretation of human society and not the class struggle indeed.

My master is not only an organizer but for a happy socio-economic life, He gave a perfect economical theory on the basis of rational distribution and maximum utilization of the entire potentialities of the universe. The theory is known as PROUT, which is a theoretical panacea of the present problems of the world. (See chapter 9).

He is the organizer of the entire human society. Under the banner of His beautiful ideology, we are fulfilling His mission. Humanity will be not in tears and misery; He wanted to see radiant smiles on the faces of not only human beings, but each and every creature (Flora and Fauna) of this world. He left His

mortal frame directing each and everything in black and white. My master was a loving father, divine Guru, reliable friend, strict guardian and a master organizer. His colorful personality, multilateral actions and unipurpose love was for the entire creation. (See also chapter 5.)

### A Loving Father

1981, when *dharma Samiksha* was in progress, in the headquarters of Ananda Marga (511, *Jodhpur* park Calcutta), one whole timer came from Europe with a large number of plants. He came out of the airport and was about to enter a taxi when an airport official stopped him. He enquired if the boy was a member of *Ananda Marga*. The dada replied, "Yes, and I just came from Europe." The official asked if he could ask a few questions about Shrii Shrii *Anandamurti*. Dada told him "No problem, you can ask any question you like about my beloved Guru." Then the official asked, "Is Shrii Shrii *Anandamurti* uncultured?" Caught by surprise, the dada said, "Why do you ask such embarrassing question?" The airport official said, "Because two days before one foreigner was leaving the immigration (*Dumdum* airport). I was at the counter. When he gave his passport to me, I noticed he had just entered two days before and now he was leaving. I asked him why did you spent so much money to come here only for two days? That gentleman was overjoyed. His face was shining with joy. The man replied to me, 'I came to see my spiritual master, Shrii Shrii *Anandamurti*. I met him and He removed all my *samskaras* (results of previous actions). Now I am pure. I sow the mark of caning on His back. Since then a question plagued me if shrii *shrii Anandamurti* is cultured or so cruel that cane His devotees so severely? The Dada replied, "How many children do you have?" The officer said "two". *Dada* asked, "Can you give your full attention to them?" The officer replied, "No, I have so much work, I cannot give proper attention to my children." Then the *Dada* said, "But my master gives proper attention to all His devotees, and accordingly, He gives love and affection to His sons and daughters. One son came from Europe and my master gave proper treatment to him he left *Calcutta* with full of joy. This goes daily in case of hundreds of devotees." The officer was mum and silent.

My master was the creator of a comprehensive philosophy, the constitutional head of a global organization and guru of millions. He never forgot the responsibilities of a loving father in His day to day life. He considered the requirements of each and every one of this vast universe. We accepted Him as a universal father. Generally, the Indian ladies wear veil and cover their head with the part of sari. But in the case when they stay in their father's house, they don't care about it, since they feel that it is their own house. But when they go out of their father's house or the house of father-in-law, they use veil covering their head. One day during DMC at *Ananda Nagar*, Baba declared that this *Ananda Nagar* is father's house; therefore woman needn't to cover their head with sari. Every woman felt freedom in this regard. This is the work of a father.

In the earlier days devotees use to be very emotional with my master. Once a devotee asked Baba, "People saying, 'you are now discriminating between worldly sons and us.' 'So how long we can bear this?" Baba replied, "My son, I have no worldly relation, my all relations are spiritual. Someone desires to be my worldly son, become as such, but it is better to be my spiritual son. In that case, I shall reprimand you; punish you so that you always move on the path of righteousness."

Once a devotee asked Baba who is your relatives and friends? Baba replied that His nearest and dearest ones are those who follow the sixteen points.

Once there was DMC in Patna. There was an *Acarya* meeting. All family *Acaryas* and monks were sitting there. Baba came in the meeting and suddenly asked, "Is there anyone here

present from *Nikammapur* (Lazy city). I saw two gentlemen stood up in front of Baba with folded hands. Baba asked, "You have finished your daughter's marriage?" The gentlemen replied affirmatively. Baba said, "But I did not receive any invitation card". The gentlemen replied that the marriage was done according to local ritual system and not according to *Ananda Marga Carya Carya*. Baba said "You are an Acarya, if you are not following the *Ananda Marga Caryacarya*, how will your *Diksa' Bhra'ta'* follow it?

There is a story in the great epic *Mahabharat*. There was a king *Bharat*. He had nine sons, but he chose his successor from the public. When his mother, *Shakuntala* asked him, "Oh my son, you have nine sons, why have you chosen heir from your subjects, not from your sons, king *Bharat* replied "O mother, I am a King. My entire subjects are my sons and daughters. I can choose the most capable." In the case of my master, He is the father of everyone, but for worldly maintenance, He adopted Sri *Kinsuka* or *Gautam* as his worldly son, but *Gautam* didn't follow the sixteen points so He left Baba. Those who are following the sixteen points are His Spiritual son. There is a lesson for every *Ananda Margii* that none should compromise with those who are going against the sixteen points. Therefore, He adopted Sri *Kinsuk* and parted *Gautam Sarkar*.

June/July 1981, was a memorable year for *Ananda Margiis*. It will surely take place in history as we were blessed with *dharma samiiksa*. My master reviewed one by one the past and present actions of a horde of disciples and devotees. *Dharma Samiiksa* was like an x-ray of the past and present actions of the devotees. He gave solutions of physical and psychic problems. He also taught spiritual upliftment through asanas and spiritual lessons and also advised regarding the progress of the organization. In the case of anyone, man or woman, have made a mistake, he/she must take punishment. During the *dharma Samiiksa*, one gentle lady who came from the north part of Bihar and was a noble *Sadhiika* was also doing organizational work sincerely, had made some mistakes and Baba beat her with stick. He smashed one of her right hand bangles. Baba told her lovingly that He was beating her to remove her bad *Samskara* and mistakes. Her eyes filled with tears. After that she went home. I visited her to hear her experience and reaction. As I asked her, she again began crying and the tears rolled down her cheeks. She told me that Baba is not only Guru but He is our gracious parent also. His roles are always balanced. Then again she said that her parents only loved her, they never punished. But Baba's love contains both kindness and punishment.

When our headquarters was in *Ranchi*, Baba had told about love and punishment. If you love only, then you are the enemy of that person. If you are only punishing them, you're also an enemy. There should be balances between love and punishment and the punishment should not exceed to your love. If you give love more than the punishment, it is welfare. When you give more punishment than love, it is cruelty. So 'Love-punishment = Rule (Shashana) & Punishment - Love = cruelty' (We feel the love of our affectionate father Baba is deeper than the ocean. His heart is larger than the sky).

### A Strict Father

What is difference between the rule of a mother and a father? According to my personal experience mother are the incarnation of love. Why does the mother love the child so much? Because the child is part of the mother's body. When the baby was in the mother's womb, at that time child was the part of mother's body but when the child is born the baby becomes different part of the mother but yet it is a part of the mother. Therefore, the mother usually shows more love to the baby than the father. If a child does not receive proper care and love from its parents, the child will become cruel in his life.

According to my master, love and punishment must be

balanced otherwise child becomes undisciplined. Therefore, my master always maintained the organizational rule even in family life. My master had only one son whose name was Gautam. When Gautam was twenty years old, he married. He came to Baba's house in Lake Garden (Calcutta) to ask Baba's blessing. When *Gautam* met Baba, He asked Gautam, 'have you asked permission from your mother?' *Gautam* replied, 'No Baba' Again Baba asked, "Have you taken permission from your Uncle? Answer came 'No'. "Have you informed me of your marriage?" Gautam replied no. Then Baba asked, "When everybody of your family is died then who you will give you blessing?" Baba again asked, "Have you married according to the *Ananda Marga* system?" Gautam replied 'No'. Then Baba said "Look Gautam, I have other work to do other than giving everyone blessings. You must follow the Caryacarya. If I will bless you it means anyone can come for blessing and if I do not give it then no one can put your example and can utter I blessed *Gautam* though He is not married according to the *Ananda Marga* system."

Gautam left Baba's quarters without His blessing and Baba adopted Kinsuk *Sarkar*. The moral teaching for the world is that you do not support even your own family if they are at wrong. So, one must follow the path of Yama and Niyama in every cases.

### A Divine Guru

16th October, 1962, there was a DMC in Raipur. During the General Darshan, Baba said "Tum diivan, tum diivan, tum diivan *mero, Mai* gulam mai gulam mai gulam tero".

It means, "You are my master and I am your slave". Baba added, "I'm like the washer man washing all your *Samskaras*." According to the meaning of Tantric Guru, He who removes our internal darkness and guides for spiritual development is GURU. It is not necessary to guide merely physically even He guides us in astral form, or any physical form He may guide us. He is a guru, the divine guru. If you do not follow He will punish you. Once He told that His two eyes are always watching you. "I'm your shadow." A new worker was posted in Western India. He had come for reporting. Most of the workers have come from the schools, colleges, or universities. They had not much experiences regarding cleanliness. As in this period of their educational life they depend upon their parents for every thing. So a worker came to Lake Garden, (Calcutta). Baba was living there when *Tiljala* was not built up. During the reporting, every department head, area level workers of all trade, all circle level workers of all trades and all DS of ERAWS section were present there. Baba also calls them in front of him by name. A worker came in front of Baba. I want to say something. Baba use to teach the junior workers questioning to the seniors.

Baba asked you're following the sixteen points properly?
Worker: Yes Baba! I'm following properly.
Baba: Are you fasting regularly without water?
Worker: Yes Baba!

Then Baba named a particular month and date which was Purnima. In Ananda Marga the workers (Monks and Nuns) fasts for four days in a month. The workers said that he was fasting without water, Baba asked him is there a river nearby his school and the worker agreed. Baba further asked him if he takes bath in the river every evening. He agreed and said he baths there everyday in fasting day.

Baba: On that particular day of Purnima, you had taken a bath in water, you started drinking.

Baba told him a quote - Sinking sinking water drinking, thinking that Baba is not looking.

The worker accepted his mistake. Then Baba said, "I want to inspect your school can you give me permission." Baba was saying in light mood, with smiling face.

The worker said yes Baba, you can inspect my school.

Baba was sitting in *Calcutta* physically and started an inspection of the school of Maharashtra. Baba said, "Let us enter his kitchen. There is a cooking pot which is very dirty and black. Before he came he cooked food, ate but he did not clean it properly. Then Baba told let us enter his bedroom. People are cleaning and removing all dust from the room but this gentleman is collecting all garbage in one corner of the room. Baba jokingly told us the principal must be guided by some principle. Then he will become a perfect principal.

Baba further told, let us enter the classroom. There is a nice chart on the wall but not clean, the table and chair requires more cleanliness."

One devotee was a smoker. One day Baba remarked, "Somebody was asking me whether smoking is an immorality? I replied that it is not immorality but a bad habit one should quit it. This question was in the mind of the devotee and Baba replied him indirectly. But he continued to smoke finding it difficult to quit. One day, a helper of the Jagrti, who used to take every care, the devotees comfort during their stay at the Jagrti, became severely ill. For this bad habit, he could feel that Baba was scolding him through that jagrti helper. The next day, he sat in meditation at the jagrti and prayed to Baba to grant him the will power to stop smoking. Since then he had stopped smoking. It was a miracle to him.

During his stay, he saw Baba giving various demonstrations with Ac' Dashrath as the medium. He would explain the philosophical aspects, sometimes taking, human skull in hand, and explaining the point of the part of mind pointing the skull. He would touch Ac' Dashrath's point of conscious mind and ask him to speak what he was seeing. Dashrath would speak out, the scene of Mahabharata. Baba asked, "How is lord Krishna's moustache, or was he clean shaven?

He replied, "Lord *Krishna* has moustache." Baba said to him that we should correct the notion that Lord Krishna was clean-shaved, as depicted in modern pictures."

One day when I (Krpananda) was in Indonesia, due to a certain reason I was very much disturbed. A sound came from outside. It was Baba's voice: "Don't worry, I'm always with you." I became happy. He is our divine guru. He is guiding us directly, indirectly physically, mentally in luminous form or through sound and astral form.

A Dada was posted in Peru, South America. He was arrested on the charge of murder. Since Peru was under military rule, Dada was unable to plead innocent, and his associates did not know about his arrest. Then helplessly, Dada went under untold suffering inside the jail. He refused to eat Tamasik food. Day by day, Dada became thinner and thinner. He could not recognize himself in the mirror. Ultimately, he gave up his hope to live and did Kiirtan in the last moment, as it was his death cell.

Suddenly, a boy appeared at the door attracted by the Kiirtan. He asked if the prisoner was an *Ananda* Margii. He said there are Margiis in his hometown and they sing "Baba *Nam* Kevalam" also. The boy asked Dada if he wanted any message to be delivered to the *margiis*. Dada sent message to get him released. That night, a group of officers came to torture him. Dada felt that he would be happy to die soon so that at least his mind would escape from jail.

Early the next morning, the warden received a telephone call giving orders to release Dada. At the same time, a Margii received Dada's message from the boy and went to the prison. Dada met the Margii and both of them went searching for the boy to thank him. The boy was no where to be seen. After sometime, Dada went to India for reporting. Baba asked Dada who was the boy that helped you and whom was the mysterious telephone call? Just after hearing this, Dada wept openly.

The first Dada posted to the Berlin sector had no place to

live. He had no money. Return ticket to India was to expire within a few days. Mentally he prayed Baba for help and had planned to return to India if nothing could be done. He sat for meditation in a park. After sometime, he did guru puja. He thought it would be his last day in Europe.

Suddenly an old man approached him and asked him if he was an Indian monk. He handed over an envelope to Dada saying that it is a love letter for him and left. Dada was surprised to find a large sum of money in the envelop that was enough to purchase the first Berlin sector *jagrti*.

A very devoted Indian Dada was posted outside India. He was very disturbed, in thought of leaving Baba. Baba called him in His room and said, "There are so many things around me, my bed, slippers, books, etc. They are not able to get salvation. I love those who are far and those who are near equally."

### A Reliable Friend

According to Samskrta, there are several words for friend, but all words of Samskrta have different meanings. Bandhu—A friend from birth to death. Only Supreme Father can be called *Bandhu*, those who are living with us always. *Mitram* means sun and mitra means friend. The person is of same status, two leader, two businessmen, teacher, etc. when they become one they are friends. Sakha—It means two bodies sharing one mind, we can use bosom friends. In the case of my master He had reliability with every inanimate and animate.

Here is the story of one devotee in his own world. It is an event of 21st October 1991, when I returned home from my office at about 6 p.m. My wife handed over to me a short note (letter) written in Bengali. Upon my inquiring who gave her that letter, she replied that when she was weaning rice in the outer verandah at about 11 a.m., all at once she noticed an old man, stranger, in a loin cloth (dhoti), Punjabi Shirt, shoes and spectacles. She had seen him for the first time in life. The old man asked my wife in a very courteous voice, "Is *Lokamitra* in the house?" and extending His right hand He requested my wife, "Would you kindly give this letter to Lokamitraji? When she inquire as to where He came from and who had given that letter to him, He responded to all these queries simply by saying what would she do by knowing these and walked away. Actually because she was upset at my youngest son *Prashanta* for not returning home from school, she had rushed into the room with the letter and put it under the bed. She was so engaged that she forgot even to mention to me anything about the letter when I came home for lunch. That time she was lost in scolding Prashanta to her satisfaction. And, when I returned home in the evening, she showed me the letter.

I tried my level best, but could not make it to read the letter because it was written in Bengali. Then I started contemplating whom the old man might be that knows me by the name, *Lokmitraji* Finally, both of us decided to go to a Margii brother Nirmalendu Chatterjii. Seeing him, I requested him to read the letter for me. After listening to what was written in the letter both of us, me and my wife were fully convinced that it must have been sent by Baba? Even we realized that He Himself must have visited us that day in disguise.

The English translation of the letter could be renders like as follows:-

> *Lokmitraji,*
>
> You had promised that your physical body was meant for service to Baba. I reside in your house in form of Prashanta. Then why not you take interest and help in promoting the education of mine (Prashanta). When Prasanta will pass scoring good marks, then only Marga or I will be served adequately
>
> –Friendly yours, Baba

It would be desirable to mention here that none other than Baba, Cosmic father and Dada ji *Ac Shraddhananda* Avadhut, know me by this name. Hence who else other than him could

write me this letter? The first sentence of the letter relates to my personal contact with Baba when He, in the month of January 1971, had asked me during my P.C. at *Ranchi*, "Whom this body belongs to?" I replied, "It is yours, Baba." "Then should you not utilize this body for the works of *Paramapurusa*?" He asked that I had said, "Yes, Baba." This conversation has not been disclosed to anyone. So no one had knowledge of it. Hence no one else than He could make a mention of that in the letter.

Whatever written about *Prasanta* is cent percent true. He is the youngest son, naughty by nature. He has not been taking interest in study for the last three to four years. Last year he was even beaten up—yet no reforms or improvements. Neither has he studied at home nor at school. If I confess, I have not done anything for him except scolding and beating. Honestly, I did nothing to improve his studies. Other than Baba, who else can understand my intentions, a friendly advice, which proves the fact that I had ignored my family responsibilities by not assisting *Prasanta* in his growth and development as a manifestation of *Paramapurusa*? The cosmic father has made me conscious of my responsibilities towards my family.

True, how great is my lord, my cosmic Father, Baba, who visited at my door and opened my eyes by giving such a priceless advice. I ever pray to Him to reside in my heart and mind and accompany me in life and death.

One question came in my mind, why He wrote the letter in Bengali. Perhaps had He written in Hindi, my wife would have read it instantly and He would not have managed to escape? Indeed, His Liila is beyond imaginations. (Korba, M.P. India)

---

# III

## MY MASTER: HIS FIFTEEN SHIILAS

> "Morality is the base, *sa'dhana'* (spiritual practice) is the means, and Life Divine is the goal."

> [Illustration: A potted plant with patterned leaves.]

# PANCADASA SHIILA
### (Fifteen Rules of Behavior)

During a field walk, once my master told one devotee, "It is your duty to propagate the philosophy and ideology of *Ananda Marga*, and not me."

The devotee then told Baba, "But Baba for anyone to do that, they must understand that your each expression is an ideology." My master was mum. The philosophy and ideology of *Ananda Marga* is too difficult for the common people to understand, but His life is very easy for anyone to understand. Therefore, I am to say, He is an ideology.

Human life has two sides - personal life and social life. My master *Shrii Shrii Anandamurtijii*, whom we endearingly call BABA, has given us fifteen shiilas for regulating our personal life, whereas the sixteen points given by my master are both for personal and social life. My master gave fifteen *shiilas* in the month of May, 1964 on the occasion of the annual D.M.C. at *Patna (Bihar*, India).

Needless to say, my master, who gave these fifteen *shiilas* and about whom I am writing this article, followed these *shiilas* meticulously in His life. There are many stories in His life but I am giving some instances in the paragraph that follows.

1. **Forgiveness** (This is the first *shiila*) - When Baba came out from *Bankipur jail*, *Patna*, our headquarters was at *Patna*. One day after Baba's release from jail, *Ac. Keshavananda Avt* went to *Darbhanga (Bihar)*, for organizational work. While there, He saw *Dr. Rahamtulla* and inquired about his health. It may be mentioned in this context that *Dr. Rahamtulla* while

posted as medical officer, Bankipur central jail, Patna, at the bequest of C.B.I. gave poison to Baba. On the historic night of the 12th April, poison was given on 12th February 1973, causing serious complications in Baba's health and giving rise to five types of reactions—

Serious weakness all over the body, Nervous debility all over the body, Excessive pouring of water from both the eyes, Severe headache and Feeling of mental inertness.

When *Ac Keshavananda Avt* met the doctor at Darbhanga, the doctor told him that he was then in fine health. He also told him that Baba has forgiven him for the blunder committed by him in administering poison to Baba in *Bankipur* jail at the request of C.B.I. He further told him that when one day he went to Baba's cell to express His sorrow for the blunder, Baba pardoned him. Taking this advantage, he informed Baba that he was having serious stomach problems and requested Baba for its remedy. Baba told him that he should get His transferred from *Patna* to *Darbhanga*. The doctor further said that according to Baba's advice, he got his transfer from Patna to *Darbhanga* and he got cured of stomach trouble after sometime. After returning from *Darbhanga*, *Ac. Keshavananda* Avt told the story of the doctor to Baba. Baba then said, "Do you know *Keshavananda*, why the doctor got cured of his stomach trouble. The doctor was born at *Darbhanga*, he grew up there. His body and liver were adjusted with the water there so when he returned there the water of *Darbhanga* readjusted his body. This is a very simple science." This is only one example of His forgiveness. He not only forgave the flawed doctor but also showed him the way to recover from his stomach illness.

2. **Magnanimity of mind** - In 1973, when my master was in *Bankipur* jail, Patna, in connection with some false and concocted case, the then Prime Minister of India, Shrimati *Indira Gandhi*, made some derogatory statements about my master from the public platform in *Mumbai*. When this was brought to my master's attention, He did not in return make any disparaging remarks against the Prime Minister, rather He said, "I have not spoken anything against this noble lady, but out of envy this gentle lady is speaking against me." In this way, Baba gave more respect to the *Indira Gandhi* than her conduct deserved.

3. **Perpetual restraint on behavior and temper** - For the cause of His mission and to make Himself available at all times for the growth of the organization that He built up; my master shifted His headquarters to *Ananda Nagar* (West *Bengal*, India). Being afraid of the growing popularity of *Ananda Marga* due to its social and welfare activities, the communist regime of West Bengal Government grew apprehensive and hatched up a genocidal conspiracy to nip the organization in the land. In furtherance of this evil design, the communist regime started making false propaganda against the organization and instigated the local simple-minded people to make a murderous attacks on the inmates of *Ananda Nagar*, thereby causing the death of five Sannyasis on the 5th March, 1967. This however, could not restrain the popularity or the activities of *Ananda Marga*. Later on, when the headquarters of the organization were shifted to *Tiljala*, *Calcutta*, the Marxist Communist regime of Calcutta hired goondas to make murderous attacks on the Sannyasis (Monks) and *Sannyasini* (Nuns) of *Ananda Marga* and brutally killed seventeen of them, in broad daylight, on the streets of Calcutta near *Bijana Setu* on the 30th April, 1982.

On both these occasions, my master maintained exemplary endurance and restraint in retaliation. More harm could be caused but my master demonstrated extreme patience to teach others that nothing should be done in the heat of anger. Even the head of the communist party made a remark about how my master was working very cold mindedly.

4. Readiness to sacrifice everything of individual life for the sake of ideology—My master established the Ananda Marga organization in 1955. The objective of Ananda Marga is "*Atma Moksartham Jagata Hitayaca*", salvation of self for the well-being of the universe. He started the organization at *Jamalpur* (Bihar) which was His place of work. He was a railway employee and the railway had allotted Him lodging; it was used as a Jagriti or the meditation center where devotees used to meet, and He lived in a rented house. This was one of the first instances of his sacrifice for the ideology. Later on, He left His home, His service, His family and personal comforts for the sake of the ideology. Until the last moment of His life, He worked for the cause of the ideology. By His own ideology and by His own example, He taught us the lessons—"Fight for your ideology, Live for your ideology, Die for your ideology and be one with your ideology".

5. All round self-restraint—The six *'Ripus'* (*Kama*, *Krodha*, *Lobha*, *Moha*, *Mada*, *Matsarya*) and the eight *'Pashas'* (*Lajja*, *Ghrna*, *Bhaya*, *Shaunka*, *Jugupsa*, *Kula*, *Shiila*, *Mana*) are the propensities of the mind which adversely affect the personality due to their undue influence. A man who has no control over these becomes their slave. My master has taught us a process of *Sadhana* by which a Sadhaka (Practitioner) can have control over these propensities and exercise self-restraint. My master had full control over the propensities. In the course of various demonstrations, He has shown the behaviour of mind under the influence of one or the other propensities and when it is free from the influence of it.

6. Sweet and smiling behaviour—My master is an embodiment of eternal bliss and happiness. That is why He is Lord *Anandamurtiji*. We have never seen Him morose. We have never marked any sign of anxiety or distress on His face. Not only was He always in a pleasant mood, He always wanted everybody to be in a pleasant and smiling mood too. I am reminded of His repeatedly commenting at the workers meeting—"He who does not smile has the face of an urn shaped Pot." When Baba was in Bankipur central jail, I had gone there for His Darshan. Seeing His physical condition, I started weeping. He told me in a sweet voice, "You see, I am in jail and in some way in the physical bondages of the Government; but you are free of those bondages, so why are you weeping?" Saying those words my master started smiling. In time of sorrow also, He maintained unusual calm and quiet behaviour.

7. Moral courage—In every action, small or big, in every situation, simple or complex, my master has shown exemplary moral courage. In this connection, I may cite an instance; on December 29th, 1971 at 6 am the C.B.I took Baba into custody on a false and fabricated criminal charge and took Him to their office at Patna. The C.B.I. officials surrounded Him and started interrogating. One officer asked Baba, "If you are God, can you change the morning to evening?" Hearing this foolish question, Baba loudly shouted at them, and in no time all the C.B.I officials instantly left the Baba's room. Only after one hour, they came to Baba's room, but nobody had any courage to ask any further questions to Baba. I may mention another incident in this context when Baba was in Buxar jail, the Director of C.B.I Mr. Hingorani met Him there and proposed, "Mr. *Sarkar*, if you abandon preaching 'Prout' philosophy, we can withdraw all cases against you and we will provide you with a bungalow, a car, a monthly allowance to maintain your family." Baba replied, "In this life, I cannot leave Prout philosophy." The C.B.I. Director then told Him, "Your workers are leaving the organization. Your wife has left your house." Baba replied, "If my wife has left my house, it is my personal matter. So far the workers are concerned, they always worked hard. When I shall come out of jail, I shall make my organization ten times stronger than now." This bold reply of my master discouraged the C.B.I official and he left the jail.

disappointedly.

8. Setting an example by individual conduct before asking anyone to do the same - My master set many examples through words and actions. Even as a student he was very close to His teachers and popular among His friends due to His amicable nature. He would not, however, tolerate any injustice and even in His early childhood, He had to fight against evil forces and mischief mongers. Once it so happened that some saffron colored so called Sadhus took shelter near His house at *Jamalpur*. They had cooked meat and while they were at the point of eating it, my master as a small boy started hurling stones at them single-hand so that they had to flee, leaving the cooked meat to be enjoyed by the street dogs. For His love of different subjects at that small age, the teachers of the school named Him "living encyclopedia". As the eldest male member of the family, He had to sacrifice His desire for higher education and to take a job in the railway immediately after completing His intermediate science examination in which He ranked first amongst the ten from Calcutta University. He sacrificed all His personal comforts for His family for His widow mother and three younger brothers. As an extremely obedient son of His mother, He would never go outside without her permission and would deliver His entire salary to her, and she used to return some pocket money to Him who also remained unspent to be utilized for some charity or to help some really deserving persons. While in the services, He was very particular in His work and had a wide circle of admirers not only for His efficiency but also for His versatile knowledge in all subjects including palmistry and astrology. As a spiritual preceptor, He established the socio-spiritual organization with the motto of salvation of self for the well being of the universe. His philosophy and ideology spread far and wide. For His mission He left the little home and made this universe His real home. He left His small family and made this universe His big family. He left His service in the railways for service of the entire creation. These sacrifices He made asking His disciples to do so. He even went to jail and as protest against not complying with His demand for a judicial inquiry against administration of poison to Him while in jail, he remained without any meal for more than five years. When His disciples used to go to jail to meet Him, He used to say, Except for my long fasting, you should follow all my actions and if necessary you should not hesitate to go to jail for the cause of ideology". My master always used to say, go on doing work while you are alive and even when you are at the point of death keep continue your work".

9. Keeping oneself all of from criticizing others or condemning others or mudslinging and all sorts of groupism - My master would never indulge in looking on the dark side of anything. He would not even listen any negative information. My master has given an ideology that is extremely rational and logical. It was therefore natural that while upholding these philosophies and practices, those who are unscientific, irrational, and illogical, raised their voice against him and even conspired to nip Him and His organization in the bud. But my master never lost His calm, He knew those irrational philosophies were doing immense harm to humanity. Yet He would never speak ill of any of the propounder or followers of those irrational philosophies or indulge in mudslinging. To Him, human society is one and indivisible. To Him, the *"Dharma"* of human beings is one and the same. Therefore, there is no question of any group or groupism in the human society. He used to repeat. "Let everybody be happy. Let everybody be free from all suffering. Let nobody be made to suffer under the pressure of any circumstances.

In 1966 at *Jamalpur*, during a field walk, Baba asked a question, what is the sweetest thing in the world? Many brothers and dada's gave the names of different sweets but Baba

was not in agreement. Finally, Baba smiled and said, "Criticism of others is sweetest thing." No one understood why? Then Baba explained that mankind spends many hours listening as they love to hear criticisms of others. "It is better not to criticize anyone. When you throw a stone into the mud, the mud spatters you not others. Don't misuse your valuable time criticizing anyone. Don't create enemies by criticizing others."

10. Strict adherence to Yama and Niyama—A man had to discipline himself in relation to his external environment as well as against his own extroversial propensities. His efforts to discipline himself in relation to his external environment are known as Yama and his efforts to discipline himself to his extroversial propensities are known as Niyama. Yama has five parts, Namely—Ahimsa, Satya, Asteya, Brahmacarya, and Aparigraha. Niyama consists of five parts, Namely—Shaoca, Santosa, *Tapah*, Svadhayaya and Ishavara Pranidhana. My master was established in "Yama" and "Niyama". He has clarified the real content of the different parts of Yama and Niyama. To Him Ahimsa is not an aversion to use of force as much as the fight against immoral forces the application of force may be essential. The main spirit of Ahimsa is not to hurt anybody without sufficient cause, by thought, words or deeds. Of the many instances, one cannot forget that in spite of medical advice to use insulin to control His diabetes, my master did not use any animal product. Only when synthetic insulin was available then He uses for Himself. What can be a better example of Ahimsa other than this?

Satya, according to my master, is not "rta" as it is commonly understood. Satya is "*para hitartham*, vacnmanaso, yatharthatvam, *satyam*". It means that with the spirit of the good of others, whatever we express is Satya. My master was established in Satya that whatever He has talked in life, it was in the spirit of doing well to others. Asteya means not to steal. This can be physical as well as mental. My master always felt that man does not own anything, so man should not quarrel over worldly objects or deprive others of their rightful claim. The principle of Asteya is very much pronounced in His Socio-Economic theory known as Prout. His personal life is a glaring example of the principle of Asteya. He used to say, if you have to travel by train and for some unavoidable reason you have not been able to purchase your ticket, after reaching your destination you should purchase a ticket for that distance and tear it off. Once a devotee reported to Him that Mr. X, a government servant, was taking bribes but he was also very charitable. His reply was "One cannot write white letters with black ink".

When the land of Ananda Nagar was donated by the gracious wife of Shrii Raghunandan Singh, ex king of *Garjaipur* (West *Bengal*), the officials of Purulia sub-registrar's office demanded a heavy bribe for the registration of the land. Since offering or accepting bribes is against "Yama and Niyama", the matter was reported to my master for His direction. My master firmly said, "We cannot agree with this. However, if the greater cause of humanity which we propose to do at Ananda nagar gets frustrated for non registration of land for not paying bribe, under pressure of circumstances you may have to do it but never pardon a bribe taker unless he corrects himself." In the respect of "Yama and Niyama", there are hundreds of instances in the life of my master. His living was simple, His food was simple, His dress was simple, and every moment He was practicing Aparigraha. Once it happened that He was wearing a torn Dhoti. One day, He had come to Jamalpur jagrti on His usual visit when He pointed out the torn portion of the Dhoti to a senior Margii, Ac. Dasharathaji, saying "Look Dasharath, I am wearing a torn dhoti, In my previous life long long ago, I was a prince and I used to be dressed in gorgeous garments and I was not required to wear a torn dhoti, but it makes no difference to me". This

only goes to suggest that my master was none else than Lord *Krsna* in His previous advent. My master considered every object of the universe—animate or inanimate—as a manifestation of *Brahma* and loved every object alike. His Neo-humanistic philosophy is based on this idea. What can be a better example of *Brahmacarya* than this?

First part of *Niyama Sadhana* is shaoca or cleanliness both internal and external. My master was so particular about cleanliness that His every action is imitable and educative. His food was sentient; His dress was plain, clean and spotless. He always used to think of the welfare of the entire universe. Even in extreme cold He would take not less than two baths in a day. He would never use the same cloth twice without having it washed. I remember Him saying time and again to us, if you have so much time that you can either take your bath or your food, you should prefer to take bath, maintain internal cleanliness is more difficult than to maintain external cleanliness. For this, we should have proper control over "*Indriyas*". My master used to quote the following lines from "*Dhammapada*" as a guide to have control over "*Indriyas*",

> *Cakkhuna samvaro sadhu, Sadhu shrotena samvaro,*
> *Ghranena samvaro sadhu Sadhu jihvaya samvaro,*
> *Kayena sambaro sadhu Sadhu vacaya sambaro,*
> *Manasa sambaro sadhu, Sadhu sabbattha samvaro"*

I mean "you must not look at such things which will cause your degradation. You must not hear such words that will bring about your mental degradation. You must withdraw your nose from those objects, the smell of which may bring degradation. You must not taste such things that will take your mind to degradation. You must withdraw your body from that work the performance of which brings degradation. You must not use your vocal chords for using such words as may cause your degradation. You must not take such thoughts in your mind as may cause degradation. You must keep yourself under perfect control."

I cite one instance to show how much my master was particular about cleanliness. Once a worker, posted at *Assam* had come for *Dharma Samiiksa*. That worker was very particular in sixteen points. When he entered Baba's room for *Dharma Samiiksa*, Baba mentioned a particular date and asked that worker whether due to inclement weather, did not take bath and did puja just after *Vyapaka* Shaoca and did not take bath for the whole day? The worker lowered his head in shame and was overwhelmed in gratitude for my master for pointing out his mistake. The second part of *Niyama Sadhana* is "*Santosa*". *Santosa* means thorough contentment. In my master's life, we have found thorough contentment. He used to say that if you make anything other than *Paramapurusa* goal of your life, you will never get Santosa because human mind is always aspiring for something intimate and his aspiration is fulfilled only if he realizes *Paramapurusa*. Contentment and greediness cannot go together. To be contented, one has to have control over his allurement. One day, Shrii *Manasa Ranjan*, youngest brother of my master, sought permission to buy a lottery ticket. Baba told him, "Lottery is the cause of greediness. You should be contented with your earnings and maintain yourself accordingly." He used to quote a verse.

> "*Sa tu bhavati daridrah Yasya asha visala, Manasi ca paritusti ko arthavanah Ko daridrah*".

Whose hopes are many are poor indeed, a man whose mind is contented is neither rich nor poor.

During a field walk, Baba once said, regarding *Santosa*, You know, satisfaction is very important in life; *Sadhakas* should practice *Santosa* very strictly. If *Santosa* is not in their life, it will be very difficult for them to maintain the spiritual life. For collective social life, people will demand their minimum necessities but in individual personal life, they have to practice *Santosa* all the time. The third organ of *Niyama* is

"*Tapah*". *Tapah* means to suffer pain for providing happiness to others. A man may be charitable yet he may suffer for nothing. But with '*Tapah*', suffering is natural. Throughout His whole life, my master was dedicated to eradicating the suffering of others not only in the physical field but also in the psychic and spiritual field. For amelioration of physical sufferings, He has established a global relief organization known as Ananda Marga Universal Relief Team (AMURT) to take care of the suffering humanity during natural calamities like flood, fire, earthquake, cyclone, drought, epidemic and the like. He has set up a network of hospitals. He has established another global organization known as Education Relief and Welfare Section (ERAWS) for taking care of relief, education and welfare during normal periods.

For doing work in the psychic field, He has set up a network of educational institutions, a 'Gurukula' university and another global organization known as Renaissance Universal. In the spiritual field, He has propounded a spiritual ideology and spiritual cult for the spiritual well being of all human beings. For the cause of this ideology, He has undergone immense sufferings, including a jail term for about seven years and a life without food for five years.

*Tapah* is of four types - Viprocita (Intellectual), Ksatriocita (warrior like), Vaishyocita (Service by money and material), and Shudrocita (Service by physical labour). The life of my master has remained a symbol of a happy blending of all four types of *Tapah* and it has inspired all to follow the path of service and blessedness. In His *Tapah* Sadhana, everyday throughout His life He used to remain engaged 18-20 hours.

The next organ of Niyama is Svadhyaya. It means reading something and understanding its perfect meaning. His advice is that one should read such books that are educative and free from dogmas. One should read such books, which elevate the mind and create feelings of universalism. Those who read scriptures for the purpose of Svadhyaya, they should read the clearly recognized Dharma Shastra or Darshan Shastra. Those who cannot read should do Satsaunga (company of virtuous people). My master used to often quote the following verse.

> "*Satsaungena bhavenrnuktih Asatsaungesu bandhanam*
> *Asatsaunga mudranam ya sa mudra parikirtitah*".

"Good company leads to salvation and bad company leads to bondages. To leave bad company is called *Mudra* Sadhana in Tantra. My master always offered good company to everyone who he came into His contact. He would discuss and give discourse on such subjects which elevates the mind. His writings on spirituality and lyrics (5018) can be read for Svadhyaya. He was so particular about Svadhyaya that He made it an essential part of everyday Sadhana. In His book, "Neo humanism Liberation of the Intellect" He has said that if one has to get rid of dogma, he has to do Svadhyaya.

The last but not least organ of Niyama is Ishvara Pranidhana means to move to the causal factor. So that process that leads one to the causal factor of this universe is known as Ishvara Pranidhana. This is the practical sadhana, which every human has to practice, and through which a living being has get human body. My master was so particular about His own Sadhana that He would not miss it even for a day under any circumstances. That was His instruction to us also. He used to say, "Be great by your Sadhana, by your service and by your sacrifice."

Whenever He went out, His programme was so mapped out that His daily schedule of puja in the morning and the evening was not disturbed and while on a journey He must stop someplace for at least two hours so that He can perform puja (meditation). At times we used to ask Him, 'Baba, we worship you in Guru Puja for salvation, you are an entity free from all bondages; whom do you worship in your puja?" He would smile and say, "In my puja, I worship you all." So particular

was He about *Sadhana* that He taught us to follow it meticulously, "No *Sadhana*, no meal". If any *sadhaka* who has either neglected his *Sadhana* or has become irregular in *sadhana*, my master would point out that on such occasion, the *Sadhaka* did not do his *Sadhana* properly or that he was irregular in his *Sadhana*. Such steps from my master worked as a great input for the *sadhaka* as He would think my master is always watching my every activity, big or small.

11. If, due to carelessness, any mistake is committed unknowingly or unconsciously, one must **admit it** immediately and ask for punishment—My master was always keen to see that if any of us committed any mistake, we should have the moral courage to admit the mistake and to take punishment as atonement. It is not enough that one should only say, "Pardon me!"; rather one should take punishment of his own volition. I am reminded of an instance. During the 1987, DMC at *Ananda Nagar*, Ac Vijayananda Avt was reading the English rendering of a *Prabhata Sangiita* in which there was a mistake. My master corrected the mistake from the dais and said "It is my mistake that due to lack of time I could not give the English rendering of the *Prabhat Sangiita* myself; otherwise, such mistakes would not have cropped up." We knew for certain cause Baba was so pressed of time that it was not physically possible for Him to do so, but the way in which He had corrected a senior *Acarya* was so unique. The message carried by these words of my master is far reaching.

12. Even while dealing with a person of inimical nature, one must keep oneself free from hatred, anger and **vanity**—My master always cautioned us not to make our mind polluted by any improper thought, deed or expression. In His life, we have seen Him extremely calm and quiet even when many have tried to malign Him and His organization. The C.B.I. officials, the jail authorities, the communist regime of West *Bengal* and even the Union Government of India conspired against Him and besides initiating false cases against Him, indulged in mudslinging. But my master never expressed hatred, anger or vanity against them. He often quoted—"Akodhena jine kodham Asadhuna sadhunam Jine, jine kodariyam danena saeccena aliika vadinam".

13. One should not be talkative—It is commonly said and perhaps correctly that one who talks more, tells more lies. My master always used to caution us that we should have perfect control of our vocal cords. Talking takes away a lot of energy. This energy can be utilized for a better cause. We have seen our master never wasting His energy in useless talk. When one of us committed a mistake my master used to award the punishment of *Mauna* (not to talk to anybody) for a specified period. In *Sadhana Shivira*, to observe *Mauna* for at least an hour during the busy hours of the day is an essential of the program. The practice of observing *Mauna* and not to talk irrelevant things helps one to become truthful.

14. **Obedience to structural code of discipline**—My master always insisted on maintaining discipline in all walks of life. When it is a question of organizational or structural discipline, the interest of the individual as well as the collective body is involved. When there is a clash between the individual interest and the collective interest, the demand of discipline is that the individual interest is to be surrendered at the altar of the collective interest. My master has sacrificed all His personal comforts for the cause of ideology. By His own example, He has motivated us to be disciplined. He used to tell us, "Like a soldier, you have to do or die for ideology."

15. **Sense of responsibility**—My master was always insisted that everybody should have a keen sense of responsibility. By His own conduct, He taught us how to develop the sense of responsibility. In this connection, an incident, which occurred in *Calcutta*, is worth mentioning. One day, Baba suddenly came to the central office about 11.00 p.m.

This was quite unusual because Baba never came too late in the night. Nobody was prepared for this. A new worker had come for *Acarya* training, but since Baba had not signed his application, the training had not started. This worker had been waiting for training to be started. Baba immediately called the training-in-charge and signed the application and said, "The training was held up only for me; that is why I have come. The training can start right now, why should we waste even one night?" My master gave so much importance to time that He would not allow even a minute to be wasted. That is why He could build up a global organization within such a short span of time.

# IV

## MY MASTER AS AN AUTHOR

> “They are “educated” who have learned much, remembered much and made use of their knowledge in everyday life, their virtues I will call education. Real education is that which leads to liberation.
>
> We should remember that morality, spirituality and humanity, and a happy blending of occidental extroversal science and oriental introversal philosophy is the very foundation of our education.”

Pratika - Representation of Ideology.

### His Different Books

I mentioned in the introduction that my master's life had two parts. One part is of *Karmii Guru* and the other part is of *Jianani Guru*. In this chapter, I am showing that my master is a great scholar of this world. You can know about all this through His books. This book is attempting to bring you nearer to the Supreme.

According to my knowledge, Baba did not write any books; He dictated or gave discourses and these dictations, or discourses were placed in book form.

In the early 1980's, Baba always used to go for an evening walk; from His residence to the tiger graveyard is three kilometers. His devotees carried candles, table lamps, matchbox, paper, and pen. When Baba gave dictation, the person relating to the article gave it form of the book. Regarding *Shabda Cayanika*, *Varna Vigyana* and *Varna Vicitra*, Baba gave discourses, and the group of devotees compiled these discourses in letter form and afterward it was transcribed into the book form.

**Ananda Sutram**—This book under review is a philosophical treatise of *Ananda Marga*. This is a rare book containing 85 aphorisms inside the book and then a brief compact commentary was written according to the ancient tradition of sutras literature. The author has thrown light on metaphysics, epistemology, social thought, etc. This book is divided into five chapters, each chapter is resplendent with the glow of intuition, full of originality, clear and constant thought, and based on unmistakable truth. Those who are keen

to know Marga philosophy in brief and in the language of intellectuals will find this book highly rewarding.

**Idea and Ideology**—The purpose behind the writing of the book *Idea and Ideology* is the same as the purpose behind *Ananda Sutram*. In order to present the elementary philosophy of Ananda Marga to the English readers, this book was written. This book deals with such important topics as the cycle of creation, Saincara and Pratisaincara (involution and evolution), the origin and development of life and mind, five fundamental factors, five inferences, ten sensing and motor organs, mind, Pranendriya and propensities, the Kosas or five layers of mind, *atman*, *Paramatma* and Sadhana, life, death and *Samskara*, psycho-spiritual parallelism, etc. The book will be an immense support in understanding the *Ananda Marga* philosophy.

**A Guide to Human Conduct**—The starting point of spiritual life is morality and the culminating point is the attainment of nirbiija Samadhi. Morality is the foundation of spiritual practice; it is not the ultimate goal. Hence to be established in morality is not the supreme goal of life of a spiritual aspirant.

The mental equilibrium, which *Sadhaka* requires at the starting phase of spiritual practice, is termed as "morality." *A Guide to Human Conduct* provides a simple scientific explanation of morality. Spiritual morality has two stages: Yama and Niyama. By the way, it may be mentioned here that Yama and Niyama are the first two parts of Patanjala Yoga (Yama, Niyama, Asana, Pranayama, Pratyahara, Dharana, and Dhyana). *Yama* has five aspects: Ahimsa or non-violence, Satya or truth, Asteya or non-stealing, Brahmacarya or ascription of *Brahmahood* and Aparigraha or non-acceptance of non-essential objects. Niyama has five aspects too: *Shaoca*—cleanliness, Santosa—contentment, *Tapah*—penance, Svadhyaya—study of spiritual books, and *Ishvara Pranidhana*—imbibing of cosmic ideation. The author has explained the ten items of moral code scientifically and psychologically in a very lucid way. Hence, *A Guide to Human Conduct* is a very valuable book as it contains some basic guidelines, which are essential for the spiritual aspirant in their quest for spiritual elevation in the initial stage.

**Ananda Marga**—Higher than morality is spirituality. To be firmly established in spirituality, one needs a clear spiritual outlook. For this, one should be conversant with the basic ideas of spirituality from the very beginning. That is why, immediately after the organization was set up, the Marga *Guru* wrote the elementary philosophy book, *Ananda Marga*. The topics discussed in this book are: Dharma, macrocosm who am I, The world of macrocosm, how human beings live in the world, the goal of human life, and the importance of spiritual cult etc. The author was guided by the sole intention of acquainting the readers with the primary ideas of spirituality and developing a healthy scientific spiritual outlook in them.

**Subhasita Samgraha (30 parts)**—The Marga Guru, immediately after starting the organization, introduced two new things: Dharmacakra and Dharma Mahacakra. Dharmacakra is the weekly collective meditation of the Sadhaka at a fixed place; Dharma Mahacakra is an occasion for spiritual congregation of hundreds and thousands of sadhaka coming from all over the countries of the world. The Reverend Marga Guru would remain present in those Dharma Mahacakras and deliver lengthy spiritual discourses scintillating with penetrating intellect and profound wisdom. Those discourses were carefully recorded and later, edited and published serially in the name of *Subhasita Samgraha*.

Needless to say, those discourses were rare gems of philosophy and science of spirituality. They were 1. Intuitional science in Vedas, 2. Intuitional science in Tantra, 3. Shreya and Preya. 4. *Pravrti* and Nivrti 5. The chariot and the charioteer (Based on Svetasvataropanisad). 6. Matter and consciousness. 7. This world and the world beyond. 8. Microcosm and macrocosm. 9. The wealth of expanded mind and spirit. 10 Towards the supreme adorable one. 11. He shining everything shines. 12. The singular stance of Devotion. 13.
