It is noticed that the blind, disabled and lame people out of severe physical clashes develop a tremendous ability and efficiency in music, craftsmanship etc.

As for the orphans, Baba said He would ask the entire householder *Margiis* and sympathizers to adopt an orphan if circumstances would allow. Childless parents would also find a good opportunity to select a son or daughter. These adopted children would then be sent to *Ananda Marga* Schools. After attaining adulthood, they will become good citizens in the universal brotherhood.

Hearing this plan, G.S. became convinced and he sent the letter. When no reply came after fifteen days, Baba told him to

write another letter. G.S. wrote several times but the government never replied. Perhaps due to the false pride of the government, Baba's valuable solution was disregarded. Today, millions of beggars are roaming all over India in the most deplorable conditions.

4. My master gave PROUT philosophy in 1958. Based on the socio-economic philosophy, the leaders of the society are both moralists and spiritual leaders and they form the political parties. At this time the Indian political parties are philosophically bankrupt and they want only to win elections, and to do so they give catchy slogans to capture the votes of the peoples.

Due to its nature and philosophy, the new PROUT organization became very popular among the common people and therefore the politicians became anti *Ananda Marga*.

When the CBI arrested my master, the head of CBI office said to my master at Patna jail, if you leave the Prout philosophy, we can withdraw the case against you and government will provide you with a car and a bungalow. My master replied that He would not leave Prout in this life. The government therefore became even more antagonistic towards *Ananda Marga* and did their best to ruin the movement, but *Ananda Marga* eventually won each case one by one.

Why the Communists were against Ananda Marga?

A. PROUT has exposed the philosophical lapses and weaknesses of Marxism and proved its own superiority. Nowhere in the world have the communists ever had an encounter with a strong ideology. They know that they will survive physical clashes but will not escape annihilation in case of an ideological clash. This is why they oppose PROUT and so *Ananda Marga*.

B. At the rapid progress of Ananda Marga and her increasing influence, the communists have been active against *Ananda Marga*.

C. Self-styled progressive, as the communists are, they are brainwashed not to consider anybody else as progressive. They are as dogmatic as clergyman, a mullah or priest, because they do not want to know anything beyond Marxism. This has made them extremely intolerant and subsequently inimical to Ananda Marga.

D. There is a secret instruction to the Communists "Do not look at any dying force, but keep a careful watch on arising force and as soon as the new force will try to lift up its head, you crush it down at any cost." It goes without saying that *Ananda Marga* is an indomitable force that is rising in response to the call of history. By opposing Ananda Marga the communists are paying tribute to their secret instruction.

E. Communism is directly proportional to poverty and exploitation. In other words, communism has charm for those people who live in abject poverty. *Ananda Marga* is getting response not only from the so-called intellectuals but also from the toiling people—peasants, laborers, students, teachers, and clerks who are highly oppressed and who, but for Ananda Marga, would have fallen prey to the communists. Besides, many intellectuals among the communists have embraced the high spirit of PROUT. This has made them rise against *Ananda Marga*.

F. Ananda Marga preaches spirituality and morality and stresses the building of character. All these things are extraneous to the philosophy of the communist and that is why they oppose Ananda Marga.

G. The spiritual science as set forth by Ananda Marga makes one socio-politically conscious and this leads the communists to deduce that Ananda Marga is a political organization. They take to this deliberate propaganda, because by that they will be able to befool the public that when they attack Ananda Marga, they attack a political organization and not a spiritual organization.

### Why His Personal Assistant (PA) went against Anandamurtijii?

*Ananda Marga* started on the 9th January, 1954, at *Jamalpur*, Bihar, India. From 1954 to 1963, there was no permanent PA to my master, sometimes a particular person became PA and sometimes several persons acted as P.A. Around the middle of 1964, Ac. *Abhedananda* became Baba's PA. He worked as PA to Baba up to the 5th of March 1967, when he was murdered with five other workers at *Ananda Nagar*. Baba then left *Ananda Nagar* and set up office at *Ranchi*; Ranchi is now capital of *Jharkhand* State. After the death of Ac *Abhedananda* Avt, Baba appointed *Vishokhananda* as P.A. He was then PA from March 15th, 1967 to October 1st 1971. 1968 was declared as a seminar year and *Vishokhananda* was preparing seminar notes, directing other workers and acting as bridge between Baba and the organization. He soon developed a big ego. He started thinking that he was the one controlling the organization as almost all the central workers were working under His guidance. Human ego and idleness is the biggest enemy of the human being. *Vishokhananda* was fully swimming in ego, he forgot that *Anandamurtijii* was the man who formed and ran the organization. *Anandamurtijii* was our spirituel Guru. He knows where and what was going on. In our organization, the post has no value, at any time anybody can be transfered from any given post, even the general secretary can be posted in below diocese level. Due to the system, no worker stay in any one place indefinitely, this way, workers can learn many different cultures and languages, and also no worker should develop any inferiority or superiority complexes. Since you are a volunteer, you work as a volunteer in any post in any place. This system was started from 1962, when the organization started whole timers system.

On October 1st, 1971, there was DMC at *Binami Dharmashala* in *Calcutta*. At this time, Baba decided to transfer His PA, Vishokhananda as press manager of *Chennai*. When Vishokhananda heard this news he become very upset, he had been utilizing and enjoying the power of the president of Ananda marga, he requested Baba to keep him on as PA. Baba, however, was determined to transfer him from the PA post. Then Vishokhananda sought the help of Uma Sarkar, the wife of my master. She also requested Baba to keep *Visokhananda* as PA. Baba however said that He had so many workers in the organization that duties were to be reassigned and Vishokhananda must now perform as press manager in Chennai. For workers, every post has same importance but *Vishokhananda* felt as though he had fallen from heaven to hell and he therefore left the organization, taking eight other workers with him and he then became antagonistic towards the organization and my master.

### Why His wife Mrs. Uma Sarkar went against the organization and my Master?

Miss Uma *Dutta* got married to my master in 1959 at Chinchura Court in Bengal. She was from an ordinary middle class family and not very educated. When she came into my master's house, he instructed her in the ways of a Margii and as Marga Mata (the mother of the organization), and she became the mother of the organization. When my master was in *Jamalpur*, He was working in the office from 10 am to 5 pm. The rest of the time He gave to the organization, they were utilizing time for pracar on every holy day. My master never went to cinema, clubs, restaurants or parties. Mrs. Uma Sarkar wanted all these things because she was an ordinary lady, but my master's thoughts and planning were very high. It became very difficult for Mrs. Uma Sarkar to live her life with this type of man.

My master was very busy and was regularly away from home establishing a worldwide organization. In His life, there were many difficulties, He faced local customs, religion and dogma and propaganda against the mission. Slowly, the

provincial and central government became antagonistic towards my master, especially after He gave Prout philosophy and anti-corruption discourses. Also at this time, the international agencies also became antagonists.

All these things Mrs. Uma Sarkar witnessed, she had seen the starvation and had also witnessed, with her naked eyes, the butchering of the five monks at Ananda Nagar. She was also in Ranchi when Baba was arrested. All these struggles she saw and feared in her mind.

Baba had introduced His wife as the mother of the mission and He gave her the chance to speak to the devotees before He came on stage. In 1968, during once DMC at Muzzaffarpur, she made another dais parallel to Baba's dais and gave a lecture from there. When Baba came on stage, He inquired about the other dais and when told, He realized that something was going on in her mind. It appeared she wanted equal rank to Baba. How could this be possible for her?

Finally, on October 1, 1971, during the DMC at Calcutta, my master had transferred His PA. Mrs. Uma Sarkar requested Baba to change his mind, but Baba told her, you are the mother of the organization, you feed the children and love them but you must not poke your nose into my organizational activities. She became furious and warned Baba that she would break His mission. Baba replied, I want to know how you would destroy my organization, go and try. She went to leave the room and my master said that He could not order her to go, as it was His moral duty to bring her home. However, He said, she was free to do as she liked. At this, she left.

Liila or Krda - When I was in Ipoh (Malaysia), one Didi asked me to explain why Mrs. Sarkar left the organization and Baba. I told all the above, but this did not seem to satisfy her. Then I told her in the Sanskrit language, there are two words for play- Liila' and Krd'a.' The game everyone can see and easily understand is called krd'a'. The game, which is not easily understood, can only be understood by high meditation or by His grace, that game is *liila'*. My master's game was liila'. "Life is a game, play it".

Lastly, I told this didi, at that time Ananda Marga was moving with great speed. The government wanted to break the rhythm, but they had no way of retarding it. In any match, it both parties are equal, the match will be good and the audience and players both can enjoy. Baba gave nine persons from the organization to the government for their fight with Ananda Marga. My master was the hero of the mission side on which He had almost the whole organization with him. On the other side were the nine defectors and the Indian government, using the public money in their fight. During the period October 1, 1971, to August 2, 1979, there were several peaceful demonstrations all over the world, processions, eight people self immolated, my master was poisoned, a non government inquiry was formed, many political, social and religious leaders met with my master, Patna session court administered life imprisonment to my master, Ananda Marga was banned and thousands of Margi were arrested, while thousands of others went underground. Most of this game my master played from His cell number 13 of Patna jail. When workers told him that we had won the case in high court, He remarked simply, the first chapter of Kurukshetra (battlefield) is over. Now Didi was satisfied on this point.

### Why Madhavananda became antagonistic towards Master

In 1968, there was a DMC at Koochbihar in West Bengal. The Communist Party Marxist (CPM) was the political power. Due to ideological differences there was a clash with Ananda Marga. Ananda Marga is based on a spiritual philosophy and CPM is based on a materialistic philosophy. The CPM was always fearful of the strong pracar and scientific philosophy of Ananda Marga. When DMC was started at Coochbihar, they wanted to disturb our function. One of our

LFT's, Ravi Sarkar, was killed by the CPM activists. Ravi Sarkar was my master's bodyguard. Madhavananda was in charge of security, but at this time he took a security jeep, left Coochbehar, and gone to Calcutta. My master was then arrested in Coochbehar.

When all this became known in Calcutta, those in the office there became very angry with Madhavananda and Krishnananda. After four days, Baba came from Coochbehar. He forgave them both but they were banned from his residence. From this moment, they both developed inferiority complexes and they sought to take revenge on my master. On June 20th, 1971, my master moved the headquarters from Ranchi to Patna. During that period, the police arrested Madhavananda. He was in jail when Mrs. Uma Sarkar and Vishokhananda left the organization October 1st, 1971. They both made contact with Madhavananda and he too became against my master.

Unholy Alliance—After their defection at Calcutta at DMC in 1971, Mrs. Uma Sarkar and Vishokhananda convinced Madhavananda to give a statement against P.R. Sarkar and so started an unholy alliance with the political police, the CBI. The CBI became more active in their search for information to form a case against my master and finally they were coming to a conclusion in this quest.

Murder Plan—At first it was planned to kill my master by an accident or any other means possible, but then they thought that Ananda Marga, being a new organization working throughout the world, would grow within days if Anandamurtijii was murdered, as He would then become like Christ. His mission had become very popular throughout the world. It was therefore decided that it was better to defame His character. If the government could prove that P.R. Sarkar was of bad character, and then He would lose His followers, no one would then wish to follow the path of Ananda Marga, and the organization would then die. On these grounds they then did not murder my master.

The charges against my master:

1. Anti-nationalism—My master was seeking to capture political power by violence.
2. Murder—What was this concocted murder charge? The organization of Ananda Marga is based on voluntary service since 1962; there is no paying system in the organization. Also, there is a system that any worker leaving central office or sectorial office would first have a tour program, and the worker would act according to this program; if, for some reason they fail to maintain this program then they would contact the central or sectorial office by phone. Our organization is very big and when there is no link with a worker, we understand it means that that worker has left the organization. From 1962 on thousands of workers left the mission and no record was kept of those who left. The CBI collected wards, dead bodies and Madhavananda became the prover with Mrs. Uma Sarkar and Vishokhananda as witnesses for the case. In this manner the CBI started the case against my master.

Cross examination of Vishokhananda by learned counselor Shri Nageshvar Prasad (Sri Nageshvar Prasad was Baba's learned counselor in the Patna court case) Here is a brief account of this cross-examination.

Counselor: When did you become P.A. of Shri Anandamurtijii?

Vishokhananda: I became P.A. on March 15, 1967.

Counselor: You were P.A. of Shri Anandamurtijii from 1967 to October 1971, so you knew all things within and without the organization better than anyone else?

Vishokhananda: Yes I know all matters of the organization and yes I knew better than the others.

Counselor: You knew that Mr. Sarkar wanted to capture political power by revolution.

Vishokhananda: Yes they wanted to capture political power.

in India by revolution.

Counselor: Do you know of any type of gun?
Vishokhananda: No
Counselor: Do you know of any type of rifle?
*Vishokhananda:* No
Counselor: Do you know how to make a bomb?
Vishokhananda: No
Counselor: Do you know how to fight or to kill others?
Vishokhananda: No

Counselor: (to justice) Sir, this gentleman has told us that he knew everything in and outside of the organization. According to his statement Mr. Sarkar wants to capture political power by revolution, but this head of the organization knows nothing about revolution. In fact, there are numerous omissions, contradictions, and discrepancies in his evidence and that of the others. One has to concede to the capacity of the learned senior advocates for the defense, who has submitted that they can write volumes over these omissions and discrepancies. Indeed, I find that on every material point, there is some defect or other in the evidence of Madhavananda. Their statements may create doubt about the truthfulness of the whole prosecution in the final trial.

### My Master at Court

My master was arrested on December 29th, 1971 at Patna. He came to court 5 times during His jail life of seven years. He gave His trial statement in court, twice making His statement under section 392 CrPC before the special magistrate, Rajendra Prasad Srivastava, who was conducting the commitment inquiry into the conspiracy and murder case. My master, Shrii Shrii Anandamurti and four of His followers including the Marga General Secretary were facing the false charges.

The special magistrate recorded my master's statement on camera, lawyers of both parties were present. My master was brought in an ambulance, because He was weak from His long fasting and was sick, from the Bankipur jail at 11 am. He was taken to the court room on a stretcher and placed on a small cot at a short distance from the special magistrate's chair. My master was wearing a silken shirt and dhoti. From the stretcher, He made His statement lasting over 90 minutes. Earlier, the court had fixed September 26th for the appearance of my master but it recalled its order to view a report by a team of doctors, including the civil surgeon of *Patna* who had previously examined him in Bankipur jail on September 15th, 1972. This was His second appearance in court—His first was for identification. Except for these two occasions, my master, Anandamurtii, was exempted from appearing in the court on grounds of His health according to section 540(a).

Proceedings of the Case—Protracted committal proceedings were drawn up at the court of *Shri R.P. Shrivastava*, a special magistrate of Patna. While, deposing before the court, Madhavananda, alias Gour *Majumadar* and *Vishokhananda*, alias Guru Prasad, made different allegations including conspiracy of murder, against Shrii Shrii Anandamurti.

On the 16th of September, 1972, Shrii Anandamurti appeared before the court and refuted the charges brought against him. He told the magistrate that the charges brought against him were concocted, false and motivated.

During His ninety-minute deposition, no one except His personal assistant and lawyers of either side allowed inside the court. But a huge mob waited outside to have His Darshan. Shrii Shrii *Anandamurti* arrived there by ambulance. Armed police cordoned off the court, but in their eagerness to have His Darshan the people often disregarded it. Full-throated shouts of Anandamurti ki jai reverberated through the entire area.

When my master was comfortably settled on the cot, the magistrate asked him in what language He should record His statement. Baba replied I know more than two hundred languages of the world; you can record in any language of the

world. The special magistrate was mum for about a few minutes, the room was so silent one could hear a pin drop. Then my master, Shri Anandamurti told the special magistrate "I shall give my statement in English but in the Cambridge pronunciation." He added, in this world, there are three different pronunciations of English; there is Oxford, Cambridge, and mixed pronunciations. Generally, people speak mixed pronunciation.

Magistrate: It is said that you claim and the followers of your organization believe that you are *Taraka Brahma* and God incarnate in the line of Lord Shiva and Krishna.

Baba: Ananda Marga philosophy does not support incarnation (*Avatarvad*). If I am the incarnation of God, every atom of the universe is the incarnation of *Brahma* (God). Those who say so, do not know anything of Ananda Marga philosophy.

Magistrate: All followers of the Marga obey all your commands blissfully. Do you want to say anything in this connection?

Baba: It is not a fact that my followers obey me blindly. Ours is a rational philosophy in that there is no scope to force anyone to do anything.

Magistrate: You are the founding president Ananda Marga and you control and manage it through its five branches namely: Seva Dharma Mission, Progressive Federation of India, Proutist Block of India, *Vishva* Shanti Seva and ERAWS (Education, Relief, and Welfare Section). Do you want to say anything in this connection?

Baba: I am founder president of Ananda Marga Pracaraka Samgha. Ananda Marga is a cult. I do not know Seva Dharma Mission, PFI, PBI, or VSS. They are neither branches of Ananda Marga Pracaraka Samgha nor affiliated to it. Only ERAWS is a department of the *Samgha* (mission).

Magistrate: It is true that there was Dharma *Maha* Cakra held at Jamalpur on the night of July 28th, 1970?

Baba: Yes that is true.

Magistrate: It is said that before you left Jamalpur for *Ranchi* on July 29th, 1970, you ordered, within the jurisdiction of *Singhbhum* district, near to Tatanagar, and with the assistance of Akhocori *Himachal Prasad*, *Acarya* of *Ananda Marga* and then posted there as police superintendent, the murder of *Tapeshwarananda* and *Sudhananda*, *Shashimitrananda*, and *Mrtunjayananda*. What do you have to say about this?

Baba: Some interested parties have concocted these facts.

Magistrate: It is said that you scolded *Madhavananda* for not completing the work. What do you have to say in this regard?

Baba: For the past three years I have not spoken to Madhavananda. I stopped speaking to him after the *Coochbehar* incident. All the facts are false.

Magistrate: It is said that after completing six murders, Madhavananda had seen you on August 1st, 1970 and you had expressed satisfaction with his work and had blessed him for further success. Do you want to say anything in the regard?

Baba: Madhavananda was not allowed to come to my residence after the Coochbehar incident.

Magistrate: Do you want to say anything more?

My master went on to tell the court that He had discharged His private secretary, *Vishokhananda*, in the last week of September, 1971. He said that Vishokhananda repeatedly requested him to reinstate him even only as His domestic secretary. He said that His wife had also requested him to retain Vishokhananda but He had rejected her request also. Due to this cause, Mrs. Uma Sarkar and *Vishokhananda* had gone against him.

After this court session, when my master had left the court, the magistrate, Mr. Prasad, said to our learned counselor, Nageshavar Prasad, that this was the first time in his life he had

met a man like Mr. P. R. *Sarkar*. He pointed out that there was a ceiling fan overhead but in spite of that his shirt was entirely wet. In the course of taking the statement when my master said that He knew more than two hundred languages, I believe this shocked the judge as he shocked and became mum. Also, when my master said that He would give His statement in English but Cambridge pronunciation I understand that this pronunciation was difficult for the judge. Another point, after the statement had been given, the magistrate read it back. On one sentence my master asked the magistrate, "Where have you put the comma and the full stop?" The magistrate explained and my master said, "According to your placement, the meaning of the sentence is different for what I said." The magistrate then changed the sentence according to my master. With such things, it was no wonder the magistrate shivered while dealing with the Master.

NOTE: Master gave two statements to the court. One was for Ninety minutes and the other three days. The above statement was for the ninety minute. On the other occasion, my master gave the same statement, but this time He was so weak due to long fasting that He could not speak and He gave the statement by putting His finger on the alphabet and His lawyer made sentences which the judge recorded unofficially.

REPORT— Claude-Armand Sheppard:

On the 9th of August 1976, the International of Jurists in Geneva tabled its report to its representative, Mr. Claude-Armand Sheppard, on P. R. *Sarkar's* alleged political ambitions were not even remotely relevant to the charges, opens the proceedings to the general suspicion of their being used for political purposes, and to discredit the (*Ananda Marga*) movement. He noted that the government had confiscated all AM property and had obstructed the funding of defense lawyers. He proposed that proper funding of defense lawyers should be allowed, and added, there is really no justification for the present situation, which smacks of bad faith. His report summarized the conditions of the trial.

The political connotations of this trial are inescapable. They are apparent in the testimony of some witnesses, whose evidence appears to be designed more to discredit *Ananda Marga* than to implicate the accused in the commission of a criminal offense. They are also evident in the manner in which the Indian press and official comments about *Ananda Marga*, as well as listening to some of the witnesses called by the prosecution, one cannot avoid the conclusion that a government witch hunt has been instituted against anyone associated with *Ananda Marga*. Nothing favorable to *Ananda Marga* seems to be permitted to appear in the press. In this connection, it is highly significant that on January 29, 1976 the State Censor issued a confidential order addressed to all accredited correspondents representing Indian news organizations and foreign agencies, newspapers, television and radio ordering that "all news comments (including editorial comment), rumor, or other reports relating to the court proceedings against Shri *P. R. Sarkar* alias *Anandamurtijii* and all other Ananda Margiis.... Shall be submitted for scrutiny and shall not be published without permission in writing." The trial was thus taking place in a judicial vacuum, its fairness threatened by government denunciations of *Ananda Marga*, by the exercise of untrammeled arbitrary powers under the Emergency Rules, and by the enforced silence of the press and of public opinion.

For in the authoritarian climate of India today it is virtually impossible to find witnesses willing to brave the authorities by testifying on behalf of the accused. Indeed, many *margiis* are either in detention, or in hiding. Even if they could be found, such witnesses are said to be utterly afraid to come forward. Their testimony would expose them almost certainly to arrest. In other words, the accused have not only arrayed against them the entire power of the Indian police establishment, but even if they had all the funds necessary to prepare an adequate defense,

it is highly unlikely that they could find, or if they found them could produce, witnesses willing to testify on their behalf. Fair trials in a dictatorial framework are difficult to conceive and probably impossible to achieve.

Giving evidence before a USA congressional committee, Sheppard said: "Far more than trying to decide or rather to obtain the conviction of five individuals on the ground that they may have perpetrated a murder, participated in a murder, the prosecution and the government are obviously intent on using the judicial process for purely political motives of discrediting and destroying Ananda Marga. The trial is not the trial of the accused; it is the demolition of *Ananda Marga*."

### REPORT – William Wells, Q.C. London:

British Lawyer William Wells QC had been asked to investigate the case in depth and visited India and attended the court proceedings in 1974 and late 1976. After a lengthy appraisal of the background to the cases in 1974, he concluded: "In these circumstances, it may be difficult to believe that the authorities, whether in Delhi or Patna, are treating the allegations against Mr. Sarkar seriously, except in the sense that they are a useful stick with which to beat a difficult and embarrassing movement."

### The Last Argument of Nageshvar Prasad

The chance of justice from the courts was minimal. Fear crippled the judges. Like others, they were not free from the poisonous atmosphere. The position of radio and television was the same. In this grim atmosphere of fear, the judicial proceedings for Baba's sentencing started. The Ananda Marga workers, most of whom were in jail, and a large section of the public of India anxiously awaited the verdict of the important case.

A silence as of death overcast the court immediately before the commencement of proceedings. Nobody uttered a word. Had the circumstances been different, the courtroom would have been packed for such a sensational case, but due to the Emergency, there were only a few officers of the CBI and a few advocates. All of a sudden, attracting the attention of all, the senior-most advocate of Patna High Court, octogenarian Nageshvar Prasad, rose up to plead our case. Everybody gave their full attention to his arguments.

Looking to the judge once and then to the audience, Nageshvar Prasad started: "Your Honor, with high fever all over my body, I have come to appear on behalf of the main accused, spiritual preceptor Shri Anandamurti. Ignoring the temperature of my body, I have come in view of the importance of today's trial and particularly of this great man, Shri Anandamurti, who is involved in today's trial.

"Sir, I have already submitted that the main accused in this case is innocent. All charges against him are fabricated and false, but unfortunately the charges have perhaps been found to be established. I know that for this, either death or imprisonment for life will be awarded to him. On earlier occasions in all such cases, I have prayed for imprisonment for life, but today I am requesting you to award the death sentence to Shri Anandamurtiji. For propagating a lofty ideology, Jesus Christ was crucified by the immoral forces. This torture made Jesus immortal in history. The immoral forces got Socrates to drink poison and thereby made a place for him in history. There are many examples in history of torture and oppression having been meted out to propagators of spirituality, social reformers, and scientists by the immoral forces. Similarly, hanging will be the best reward for Anandamurtiji, who has come for the materialization of a great ideology. It is my firm belief that this extreme punishment, which is the outcome of the conspiracy and collusion of the immoral forces, will make him immortal in human history. Therefore, Your Honor, I am praying for the death penalty for the spiritual preceptor, Shri Shri Anandamurtiji, who is the main accused in this historical case."

A pin-drop silence prevailed in that courtroom. One could

perhaps have heard the beating of one's heart, if one had listened for it. All present were thinking, "Had Nageshvar Prasad gone mad that day?"

The judgment of Patna High Court—The two judges, The Honorable justice Shivesh Prasad Sinha and the Honorable Justice Choudhary Shiva Saran Sinha had given their statements with full summary of the case (section; 120; 13/302/109/302 of the Indian penal code). Present were councilors for the state magistrate B.D. Lal, Mr. P.K. Choubey, Mr. Mahadeo Prasad Sinha and Fanish Singh. Councilors for Ananda Marga: Mr. A.K. Sen, Mr. Banerjee, and Mr. Ram Tanuk, Mr. Sachidan, and Choudhary Mr. Subodh Kumar Sinha, Mr. Nirankar, and Ms. Kamlesh Jain.

The statement comprised 197 pages and 153 paragraphs. Below is a very brief part of the judgment as to write it all will give a whole book.

117\. *Madhavananda* was arrested in connection with Ranchi bomb case and he was in jail in connection with that case since the 21st June, 1976.

143\. So as far as pw3 (*Madhavananda*) is concerned, I have already dealt with his testimony in detail and his testimony has been found to set of unreliable character.

Comment about Mrs. *Uma Sarkar*.

100\. After DMC of Calcutta, even when the investigating officer took Uma *Sarkar's* statement, she did not know that anybody was being prosecuted for the murder of the Avadhutas. She did not even know at that time that some dead bodies had been recovered from the forest. She did not know that the faces of some of the dead bodies had been found burnt.

Comments by the Honorable Justice about *Vishokhananda*:

According to pw15 (*Vishokhananda*), this witness did not try to inform the police or other authorities even under an assumed identity nor by writing an anonymous letter. ... But still, he did not inform anyone, much less any police officer, about any of these murders until after the period 1st of October, 1971, when he left *Ananda Marga*.

Acquittal of Appellants

1\. According to said appellants, (*Anandamurtiji*) had stopped talking to pw3 (*Madhavananda*) since the Cooch Behar incident. This incident took place in 1969; since the appellant (*P.R. Sarkar*) had stopped all communication with pw3; the question relating to giving directions to pw3 in any matter did not arise.

153\. In view of the discussions made above, the appeals are allowed; the appellants in both appeals are acquitted, their conviction and sentence are set aside.—Signed, *S.P. Sinha*, (Justice High Court, Patna)

Statement by Honorable Justice Choudhary Siva Saran **Sinha**—I had the benefit of reading the judgment of my learned brother. I agree that the two appeals be allowed the appellants are acquitted of all the charges framed against them and that they be set at liberty if not required to be judicial in connection with some other case.

I, however, wish to add some of my own views on some of the topics that the defense did not challenge before this court.

25\. For the reasons stated above, and also those discussed in the judgment of my learned brother, it must also be held that the evidence of pw3 (*Madhavananda*), even at its face value, lacks corroboration in material particularly by other independent and unimpeachable evidence or circumstances as to clearly warrant the conviction of any of the appellants in any of the charges framed against them. It appears to be a case where the benefit of doubt should go to the appellants, which is accordingly allowed to all of them.

Signed, *Choudhary* Siva Saran *Sinha*, (Justice Patna High Court, July 4th, 1979)

The Coochbehar Judgment—The sub-divisional Judicial Magistrate Shri L.N. Ghosh observes:

I. O. prays for time, allowed to date. Prayer for time is rejected as enough time has been allowed and S.P., CBR was informed by order No. 20 dated 18.6.70 for expeditious disposal of investigation. But nothing has been done by the prosecution; hence, accused persons are discharged u/s 63 Cr. P.C.

Prayer for time is rejected as enough time has been allowed and S.P., CBR was informed by order No. 11 dated 18.6.71 for expeditious investigation of the case. But, the prosecution has done nothing. Hence, accused persons are discharged u/s 63 Cr. P.C.

In the court of S.D.J.M., Shri L.N. Ghosh, one more conspiracy against Ananda *Marga* was frustrated. Truth and Justice prevailed over untruth. It was once again established that the forces of dark must recoil themselves from the domain of Light.

Vide G.R. No 419 of 1969 in the court of SDJM Sadar, Coochbehar and Vide G.R. No 418 of 1969 in the court of SDJM *Sadar*, Coochbehar.

# XI

## MY MASTER: HIS JAIL LIFE

> "Struggle is the essence of life. Yours should be a pauseless struggle against corruption, hypocrisy and animality."

> [Photograph of a rose.]

### The Tales of Torture

Cell No. 13 at Bankipur Jail, (Patna is the hottest place in Bihar province in India). No sunrays can enter the cell because there are no windows. No air can pass because there is no ventilation. Baba spent 7 years there. 5 years, 6 months, and 2 days He was without food. For 4 years and three months, He did not touch the earth because when one is on a long fast one cannot touch the earth. He was always lying on His cot, running the organization, and giving proper guidance. He was poisoned on the 12th February, 1972.

This is a brief story of His struggle and life in jail; life is a struggle; He faced it, and won over it.

Why My Master went to Jail? According to the great Indian epic, *"Ramayana,"* Rama was exiled to the jungle for 14 years according to His parent's orders. During this time, my devotees and Sadhakas enjoyed His Darshan; *Rama* helped poor devotees, and killed the bad man, Ravana. Baba went to jail to give *Darshan* to the devotees (inside jail). Secondly, Baba went to jail so as to see how His workers would work in His physical absence.

In the year 1971, there was a violent attack on *Ananda Marga* at *Ranchi*. Baba moved to Patna from *Ranchi*. In *Patna*, the police wanted to arrest and harass Baba. They also wanted to do the same with other workers. Baba was sick. He was admitted to a cottage (special room) of the Patna medical college hospital. A big protest march was organized. For all this, the police during the protest march arrested many margiis.

In the evening, one devotee went to see Baba in the hospital. A devotee was telling Him about the march. One Margii who was taking care of Baba's food said, "Baba your food is ready, it will

be good if you eat now; otherwise it will get cold." The devotee thought, "I have to leave now." The devotee did *Sastaunga Pranam* to Baba. Baba said, "Stay a few minutes more. I will tell you a story; then you can go."

Baba started, "One day, two people went to a forest to hunt. They made a platform on top of a tree and waited there with guns. A policeman came and asked them in a very angry voice to come down immediately. They did not respond. By seeing their lack of response, the police started to climb up the platform. When he reached close to the top, one of the hunters jumped down and started running. The policeman also jumped after him and started chasing him and caught him. The policeman abused the hunter, and then the hunter asked him why he was holding and abusing him. The policeman answered, "Don't you know, keeping an unlicensed gun is a crime? You will understand the consequences soon." The hunter said, "Wait", he put his hand into his pockets and took out his gun's license. The policeman became dumbfounded and said, "Then why did you run so much?" He told the policeman that his colleague had no license. The policeman, on hearing this, started back to look for the other hunter. He was tired, he could not run fast. The hunter then told him that the other hunter could not be waiting for him (policeman). After finishing the story, Baba asked this devotee how much he liked the plan and the devotee said it was unique. This story showed the risk he took upon himself.

### The History of the Arrest of my Master

Before being sent to Buxar jail, the CBI officer took Him to the office of CBI at Patna. There were more than two dozens officers waiting. Baba, on reaching there sat before the officers. Suddenly, one of the officers asked Baba if He was an incarnation of God and if so, to change the direction of the sun from east to west. Baba became annoyed and shouted! Then all the officers left the room. After half an hour, some officers came and took Him to the magistrate's house. The magistrate was a lady. Without any inquiry, she ordered that Baba be put in jail.

Baba was arrested three times. The first time was in Coochbehar, West Bengal. At that time, Baba spent three days in jail. After three days, when He returned at 9:00 p.m., He came out near the door of the jail, turned back and did Namaskar. Baba told "Ami jacchi" (I am going). After this sentence, almost all officers, jail authorities, and police, were weeping as well as the devotees who had gone for the release of Baba.

On the second occasion, Baba was arrested in the *Ranchi* bomb case at Patna and He was released the same day, due to the inefficiency of the Indian Police. The Police had to bring Baba from Patna to *Ranchi* for the hearing. He arrived in *Ranchi* at 12:00 midnight. In spite of that, the Judge was waiting in his own house for the case. However, the police had not prepared the charge sheet against Baba. The furious judge hurled abuse at the police officers and immediately released Baba from custody. Thereafter, He spent that night at a *Margi's* home and returned to Patna the following day.

On the third occasion, Baba was arrested just before the DMC scheduled in Varanasi. As Baba was to leave Patna for Varanasi, the CBI officer came at 6:00 a.m. on the 29th of December, 1971, without warrant. Baba asked the CBI officer for the warrant. He replied: "I (he) am the warrant." That day was just after Ekadashi. The Police would not let Him take water. The CBI officers took Him to Buxar Jail. But the road was so bad that Baba's delicate body endured much; He was transferred after three months to Patna central jail, about 75 kms away from Patna.

### Baba gave a dream to a devotee

The wife of the *Buxar* central jail Superintendent had a dream on that night that a great man is coming to the jail; and He is on a long fast and so proper food would have to be provided to him early in the morning. She told her husband about her dream. He confirmed that somebody was scheduled to come and permitted her to serve Baba food and

drink. In this way, Baba graced her by letting her be the one to serve Him and break His fast on its third day).

Baba's cell was nicely cleaned and some preparations made. As soon as the news reached the CBI officials that the Superintendent was extending courtesy to Baba, they objected to the provision of such facilities and started creating trouble for him. Only what the jail manual prescribed should be given. So the organization moved court on this ground and Baba was transferred to *Patna* Medical College Hospital. After some time, He was shifted back to *Bankipur* Central Jail, Patna.

Poisoning—At the insistence of the government of India, in connivance with the CBI, Baba was given poison by a jail doctor on February 12, 1973. That morning, P.A. saw Him; His eyes were watering profusely and becoming very red. That day He said; "I wish that my people know that I have been poisoned or else the government will manipulate the fact and give a different version." From that day on, PA was the only person authorized by the CBI and jail authorities to see Him. Visitors were not allowed to talk to Him. Baba told PA, "I have been poisoned but I can survive it, I cannot talk to you so long so let this message is conveyed to the people of the world." To protect against this poisoning and to demand a false inquiry into this incident, Baba started fasting from April of that year. This unequalled fast lasted for about five years and four months. Shortly after starting the fasts, Baba wrote a letter to the governor of *Bihar* State.

The historical letter of my master to the Government:

"Sir,

With due respect and humble submission, I would like to bring to your kind notice the following facts along with tales of harassment, tortures, maltreatment, poisoning, and other inhuman activities done by agencies of the government.

The CBI (Central Bureau of Investigation, of the Indian government) arrested me on December 29th, 1971 at 6 o'clock in the morning on some charges concocted by the CBI with certain malicious and political motives. The 28th of December, 1971 being Ekadashi (11th day of the moon) was my fasting day. On the 29th morning, after I was arrested by the CBI, they did not allow me to do my morning duties throughout the day. They did not allow me to take even a single drop of water and they produced me before SDO Patna in the afternoon. My remand order to jail was kept ready in the SDO office before I was produced in SDO's chamber (not court). This shows that the SDO has already decided to send me to jail (SDO was a lady) even before she saw the arrested person. She did not ask me a single question and so I did not get the scope to narrate the tales of torture. I was brought to Buxar jail (it is in another district) at about 11 o'clock in the night. I was to remain without food for about 60 hours at a stretch.

Due to unhygienic conditions prevailing in the jail, I became sick, and on the 25th February, 1972, I was sent to PMCH (Patna Medical College Hospital) for treatment. There, the CBI and state police kept me under inhuman tortures for all the 24 hours up to the 3rd of April, 1972. There I was kept without food continuously for three days from the 25th of February, 1972. The motive was clear and simple. They wanted to kill me as per the secret instructions of CBI in the PMCH room no. 17.

I was forced to remain within the four walls of the room for the entire period up to the 3rd of April. I was not allowed to peep through the window or to raise the curtain of the door to see the sky. They did not allow me to read newspapers coming from my house. They did not allow me to take food coming from my house and instead forced me to take unhygienic food prepared by men of their choice. They did not allow any of my friends and relatives to see me even from a long distance. They created a disturbance even in my spiritual practice.

I was sent to Bankipore Central Jail in the first week of April, 1972. Just a few days after my coming here, the authorities stopped my interviews with my relatives and friends. Even now,

nobody can see me without a written order from the court of law.

In the first week of June, 1972, my mother died. I am her first son. As per usage of India, the first son is to do spiritual observance of the parents. But the cruel, inhumane authorities of the government did neither allow me to see her in her deathbed, nor did they allow me to do her last rites.

In Bankipore Central Jail also, the authorities stopped me food continuously for fifty hours with the intention of killing me as per verbal instructions of the CBI.

I am an aged man of above 50, but because of a regulated routine life, I used to maintain good health, but due to continuous torture by the government authorities in general and the jail department in particular, my health has been ruined. Four persons who used to look after me became the eyesores of the jail authorities. Two of them were transferred to Daltonganj jail and two to Buxar jail, although none of them has got any court case in Daltonganj and Buxar. The only intention to transfer them is to keep me alone and kill me conveniently.

In the first portion of 1973, they had posted several Sipahiis (police) just on the gate of my cell to prevent others from seeing me.

It is clear that they wanted to kill me secretly and shift my body silently with the help of those Sipahiis.

On the 12th of February, 1973 I felt very much unwell in the afternoon. The jail doctor came and prescribed certain medicines for diarrhea and dysentery. On the same day, at 9 o'clock in the night, again I felt unwell. The doctor was informed and came at about 10:30. He said that he would be informing the civil surgeon in Patna. I requested him to please inform my men and also gave him my residential address and phone number. The doctor came again at 11 o'clock and told me that he had informed the jailer to send news to the address of my men. Afterwards, I came to know that the jailer did not convey the news to my men and suppressed the facts for reasons known only to him.

At about 11 o'clock in the night of February 12th, 1973, the jail doctor said, "The civil doctor is not coming to see you, but he prescribed a medicine for you." He administered that so-called medicine, which was actually poison. Just after taking the medicine, I became senseless. I came back to sense at 7 o'clock on the morning of February 13th, 1973. At that time, I was undergoing the five reactions of the poison—Extreme weakness throughout the body, Nervous reaction throughout the body, Profuse watering from both the eyes, Extreme pain in the brain, Inertness of brain.

On the morning of the 14th I felt a sixth reaction and that is, I was unable to see anything clearly even from a short distance. I became almost blind. A few days after that, an eye specialist came and said I was suffering from conjunctivitis. On 15th night, two more specialists came. At that time my blood pressure was 180/100. On the 16th morning, my urine was examined, the sugar was +++ (3+). I have got no doubt that I was poisoned on the 12th night and all these diseases are reactions of that poison. On 19th February, 1973, a first class Magistrate was deputed by district magistrate to record my statement. In the statement, I have stated that in case of medical treatment, the faith of the patient plays a very important role and as I have lost faith in these government doctors and as a strong suspicion has been created in my mind that they have poisoned me as per direction of CBI, I would like to be treated by my private doctor. On the night of the 21st February, I requested the jail authorities verbally and on the 27th February, 1973, in writing, to request the district magistrate, Patna to deputize a first class Magistrate to record my statement demanding a judicial probe. I further told them that I want that in that judicial commission there may be judge of the Supreme Court of any other High Court (because Patna is the place for occurrence). But as ill luck would have it, I have not yet been favored with the presence of a first-class magistrate to record my statement. In view of the foregoing, I would request you to please

favor me by appointing a judicial commission to go through all above mentioned complaints and order relevant matters at an early date. I have got a strong suspicion that certain black hands are working against me from behind the curtain and some of them are important persons. I want to disclose the names of those important persons before the judicial commission.

Yours Faithfully,
S/D *Prabhat Ranjan Sarkar*,
Bankipore Central Jail, *Patna*.
Copy to President of India for information and necessary action
Copy to Secretary General, UNO for information

Serious attempt of murder—After the poisoning attempt, Baba's kidney and teeth were badly affected, requiring constant medical attention until He chose to enter *Mahasamadhi* (A Yogi's conscious final exit from his physical body).

There was another serious attempt to end Baba's life in jail that was later corroborated by a revelation of one high CBI official that was sympathetic to the *Marga*. He revealed that the CBI was committed to finish Baba in jail. All their best efforts had failed, up to the following last attempt which finally convinced them that *Anandamurtijii* is not an ordinary man and had decided to stop further useless plans to kill Him. At that time, I heard about their plans to hire three professional killers from the district jails of *Bihar* to kill away Baba, and to later declare that Baba was dead due to some other reason.

The first man was sent at midnight and easily entered Baba's cell. He saw Baba walking inside but strangely when he reached one corner, He kept on walking, He kept walking up the wall, across the roof and down the wall again. Aghast at the unexpected scene, he was completely shaken. He turned back and told the prison officers that the person in the cell is most holy. After relaying what he had just witnessed, it didn't matter if he was not paid, but he could not kill him. The second assassin entered Baba's cell next midnight but was bewildered to find no one in the room. The empty cell made him afraid and he also turned back and told the officials his experience. The third killer (tougher than the first two as the CBI disclosed later) was sent the following midnight. But when he was about to reach Baba's cell, he heard a terrifying sound: Hooo! Hooo! The criminal was terribly frightened by the sound and he ran away immediately in horror and became mad. That morning, the whole jail heard about these incidents. Baba's PA went to the office where the CBI officials were sitting and started telling them to their faces that you're out to kill Baba. They told PA that Baba was doing Mantra or Tantra in jail. He said that you don't worry about that. It is known that you people have brought killers from different district jails to kill Baba and that He came to know that fact from the person to whom you assigned this dirty work. Baba's PA exposed their heinous acts in front of them in the jail office and watched their faces turn black.

He always remains the Lord of all—The CBI circulated the rumor that Margiis from different parts of the world would come to rescue Baba by plane or helicopter. The government took the hilarious precaution of covering the whole jail with an iron grill, which looked like a mosquito net, spending lakhs of rupees uselessly. Baba said "They kept me in prison but my ideas and thoughts will spread to all the countries. They are always restraining my organization but my philosophy has already spread throughout the world." The government knew that *Ananda Marga* had followers in different government departments, such as military, police, etc. They feared that such persons would cooperate with foreign *margiis* in getting Baba out of jail. They posted several police and military around the jail—such guards become sympathetic to Baba so they suspected that these personnel were also the followers of *Anandamurti*. They had to go to the extent of transferring and changing their own sentinels abruptly, without reason.

They kept Him prison, but He always remains the Lord of

all. Baba went to prison to prove that great men have been the objects of persecution. He taught how to observe morality and to develop oneself, that society is full of immoralists so one Margii challenge on Lakh people. He demonstrated this in prison when several government ministers, secretaries, inspector generals of the jail and their high jail officials come to jail to have His *Darshan* so that their sins could be washed away by Baba's grace.

The first Superintendent of jail intimated to Baba the identity and elevated status of the visiting party, which included heads and prominent officials in the government. After listening to the superintendent in a courteous way, Baba sat on His cot but in a fiery manner declared "These people have no right to inquire about my health because they have no role in poisoning me and they have not restricted the finger regarding my letters to protest and persecution, so I do not want to see their faces. I would be happy if they leave this place immediately. I feel much suffocated by them."

He showed how spiritual forces and righteousness work. That He Himself went to jail to show that He is not an exception to it. He broke centuries old dogma and tradition. When He was inside jail, the CBI revealed that Baba was going to be hanged. This cell no 13, they said, is escaped execution. But Baba by example has smashed this centuries old dogma and tradition. They scoffed that if He is really God, then He will come out to save Himself.

Room no. 13 have no windows, no ventilation, no water, facilities, etc. As a precaution that condemned prisoners will not attempt to commit suicide, no facilities are provided. So you can imagine how Baba suffered in this abnormal condition. He was used to remaining in an air conditioning and here He was locked for years and years, with no fan, no windows, no bed, etc. So we struggled so much and we moved to the court to ameliorate His condition.

Brother R. Prasad, a Margii from Delhi, saw the condition where Baba was locked. It was so hot that when He seat for meditation, His clothes become wet down to his Lungi. During summer an ordinary man could not even live in that unhygienic place

Once there was a jail strike and the jail authority did not post any sweeper. Baba in spite of His failing health, Himself cleaned the room. Such pitiable condition and mind racking ordeals, the government of India brought to bear on our Lord cannot be forgiven in our whole life or in the hundred generations to come.

### My Master gave power to see and talk with uncle to Bhagavan in Jail:

When Baba was in Patna jail, a simple, young villager named Bhagavan cleaned His cell.

Bhagavan used to watch how so many VIP's officials and devotees would come and queue with people from all around the world to see Him.

One day a week was for Indians, one day for foreigners and one day for Parashakti, a devotee from the U.S.

*Bhagavan* saw how the people used to clamor and fight to see Him and though he was not initiated, he used to think how great Baba was. But, it kept coming in his mind that he was unfortunate because no one was coming to see him.

One day, Baba secretly smiled and asked Bhagavan, "Is no one coming to see you?"

The emotion immediately welled up inside him and wiping the tears from his eyes, he replied "No Baba, I am from the poorest family of my village. My family hasn't got money for the journey to come and see me.

Baba asked him, "Tell me what you want. Either or one member of your family can come to see you or can go home and see any of them."

Bhagavan become as happy as a man given a gift, he said that he wanted to go home and see his uncle.

Baba instructed him to return by 9.00 am the next day after

his morning bath.

Accordingly, Bhagavan came to Baba's cell and performed Sastaunga Pranam to Him. Then Baba instructed him to sit in *Padmasana* and close his eyes. Although not a sadhaka, Bhagavan had no trouble to sit in the Asana.

Baba leant forwards and touched his Ajina Cakra. Bhagavan felt his mind immediately withdrawing from the world and concentrating at that point. He went to a trance and found himself in his family house. His uncle was sitting on a cot smoking a bidi. Bhagavan talked to his uncle in this state and then returned to his body.

While he sat before Baba, Baba said to him "You will be released from here very soon." Bhagavan was astonished. Being a poor man, he had expected to remain in jail all his life.

He told Baba, "I have no money to fight my case. I can't live any longer or pay my bribe. I didn't expect to be released at all."

After a few days, the police submitted a charge sheet to the magistrate relating to Bhagavan's case but Bhagavan's name was not written there. Bail was set and was paid by AMPS. Later, Bhagavan was released and took initiation.

My master said that in Ananda Marga there is no excuse but punishment.

By the end of 1973, Baba had been fasting in jail for six months. He has to use stick to walk and only lay in His cot. Even so, the Mission was progressing well outside the jail. They made the CBI wonder how AMPS was advancing even without the direct guidance of Anandamurtijii.

They suspected that He had a radio transmitter hidden inside His room by which He was controlling the organization. In pursuing the idea, the CBI made two mistakes. One, a jail authority, stole Baba's gold ring on the pretext that he thought it was a radio transmitter. Due to this, our respected brother Ac. *Atulananda* Avt. Committed self immolation in cell no. 16. Late, when Baba was sleeping, a jailer entered His cell and searched it for any possible R.T (radio transmitter) (A jailer is the officer in charge of a jail division). He found Baba's shaving machine. This particular machine was made outside of India and had the special feature that as soon as one blade was finished another would automatically come to replace it. While inside Baba's cell, the jailer took the machine apart and unfortunately moved it.

Just at that time, Sri Suresh, the advocate who was taking care of Baba came into the cell. "What do you want with that?" Suresh asked.

The jailer replied that he was seizing the machine and took it to his office.

The next morning, Baba asked Suresh for His shaver, as per usual.

Suresh told Baba that the jailer had taken it.

Baba asked if the man had at least taken Suresh's permission.

When He found that the jailer had not, He said, "Ok, I will file a case against the man."

Suresh suggested to Baba that he would simply go the jailer's office and request the return of the machine.

Suresh went there and was politely received by the officer. The man's face was yellow, he had called almost every major shop in India by phone, but could not find a similar machine anywhere in the country. Then Suresh informed him that if the machine was not promptly replaced, then Baba would file a case against him.

The jailer became very afraid. He explained, "Do you know, I have to dairies. One is for 1500 prisoners and the other for *P.R. Sarkar*. I send a report on Him very two hours to the Home Minister of India.

He asked if Suresh could convince Baba to wait one day or more. However, when Suresh returned to the cell, Baba asked him "Where is my shaving machine. Hearing the jailer's request Baba said, "No, I will take it today. You watch how I do it." Baba

was first class prisoner in the jail. All first class prisoners must return to their respective cells by 9 pm. But this night at 9 p.m., Baba asked Suresh to take His chair outside the door so He could sit outside the cell.

When the warden came to lock the cell doors, he was surprised to see Baba sitting outside His cell, but he did not want to confront him, and moved on to complete his round.

In this way, the time passed, First thirty minutes, then an hour, finally, he came back and asks Baba to enter His cell, Baba said "I know the routine, but I want my machine".

The warden explained that it was the officer's mistake not his. Baba insisted on seeing the jailer to ask for the machine. Then the warden went to a telephone and called the jailer at his home. The jailer was taking dinner, but when he heard the name of *P.R. Sarkar* he left his meal and came to the jail. He came to with folded hands and said to Him.

"Baba, due to my mistake, the shaving machine is broken and I'm afraid that I can't find a replacement".

Baba replied, "Mr. Jailer, in *Ananda Marga* there is no excuse, only punishment. The jailer accepted His words and submitted to him.

My Master never Surrender:

When Baba was fasting in jail. He said "you see, I am not eating any food. You people will publish a local language newspaper and that will be my food. So from all around the world, the newspaper came. There was a government instruction to the jail that no letters were to be given to *P.R. Sarkar* without having been scrutinized first by the jail authorities. But of course, many of them were not in English. These articles were sent to the home ministry in Delhi, who then sent them to the different embassies for translation. The translation was then sent back to the home ministry who sent them back to *Patna* jail for Baba.

One day, during His evening walk, Baba asked a worker, why has a wire mesh been fixed above our compound?"

Dada explained "The government is afraid that a helicopter may come across from Nepal and fly you away".

Then Baba replied, "I came from the front door, I will go from the front door too".

When Baba was first in jail, He was imprisoned with four other workers. One day, one of them, my brother Barun quarreled with a warden. Baba came and intervened, scolding the warden, the man stopped arguing and left for the office. When he had gone Dada *Satyananda* asked Baba why he had scolded the warden when it was Barun who was at fault. Baba said, "It is my policy that I save my worker first then if the worker has made a mistake I will give punishment.

Ideological Commitment- After Baba had fast for one year, five members of parliament (*i.e.* Mr Atal Behari Bajpeyi, *Abdullah* Bukhari and others) from the opposition parties went to visit Him in prison. They request him to break His fast. He replied, "I am not a political leader, I'm a Dharma Guru. I cannot break my fast until my demands are met." They said, Baba, if you die, your organization will die too, and there will be nobody to inspire your followers." Baba replied, it is not the person, but the ideology, that inspires. My body will perish, but my ideology will remain immortal. It is not known what the reactions were of those MPs, some of whom later became ministers, but it is definite that they never met such a powerful and dignified person, an embodiment of His ideology, in their life. Baba's magnetic personality and amicable behavior inspired any person who came in contact with Him. Once, in the presence of the Jailor Mr. Bharatbhusan, jail superintendent Mr. Sengupta and some others, Baba was explaining the scientific criteria of death. Then looking towards Mr. Bharatbhusan He said, "Do you know that the change of body is similar to that of changing a shirt? If you lie down here I can show you a demonstration of it. Do not worry; life will be restored to you again." Bharatbhusan had no intention of agreeing with it and slipped away from the room. Baba looked

towards Sengupta whether he was ready to become the medium of this demonstration. Others in the room could not suppress their laughter.

He became the center of attention of friends and foes alike. The central government was restless as how to silence Him or to dispose of Him. Followers and sympathizers were worried about His safety. Common people were observing with great interest the fight between *P. R. Sarkar* and the might of the establishment. Only Baba Himself appeared to be calm and unperturbed, as if visualizing the defeat of His captors with amusement. He used to walk up and down in front of His cell and would recite "Vishva Jadi chale jay kandite, kandite, ami eka base rabo samkalpa sadhite", 'even if the whole world passes away weeping, I shall wait alone and fulfill my determination.' Remaining inside the prison, He relentlessly encouraged His disciples with vigor and confidence. He was imprisoned on the 29th of December 1971. On the eve of 1st January, 1972, He sent this message:

> "Clouds cannot overcast the sun for a long time. The creatures of darkness never want the expansive exhalation of human society. Even then, humanity shall march ahead. No one can arrest the speed of its progress. You be the harbingers, you be the prisoners of this glorious march. See not a single individual lags behind."

He was poisoned in prison on the 12th of February, 1973. On the occasion of His birthday, on the full moon in the month of May, 1973, He sent this message:

> "Cimmerian darkness, however dense it may be, the crimson dawn must follow. The friends of hell may burst out in laughter, but all their efforts must fade in the world with the sunrise."

# XII

## MY MASTER AND THE PRESS

> "Fight for your Ideology. Be one with your Ideology. Live for your Ideology. Die for your ideology. Life represents Ideology. Life should be sacrificed for the Ideology."

> [Illustration of a rose]

### The Interview

Once, during my interview with one Editor-in-Chief in the northern part of India, he said "we are free to write anything in the news paper, but must follow the rule of the Managing Director of the publisher", I smiled and answered, "The animal whose neck is tied with rope cannot eat the grass beyond the length of the rope. My dear editor, you are not free to write anything in your newspaper, you are like the animal, you cannot move beyond the length of the rope." Finally the editor agreed. The Indian paper was mostly guided by capitalist people, who do not want the exposure of PROUT philosophy and do not want the public to know the good news of *Ananda Marga*, but they also could not hide the personality of my master.

My master never called a press conference or gave a TV interview. Some leading newspapers and magazines tried to contact Him but He never agreed to any interviews, but some paper sent their questions to my master, and He gave a reply written by His P.A. or Margii. Finally, these were printed in the news. (October 16, 1976.)

Shrii Dilip Ganguli, a special correspondent at Patna from *Amrita Bazar Patrika*, a daily paper published from Calcutta, interviews Baba in very extraordinary circumstances.

**Q.** Are your followers engaged in violent activities to get you freed from the prison?

**A.** I completely reject incidents of violence. If any group of youths having no knowledge or faith in the ideology of the Marga were engaged in such acts, then I would not like to be freed in this manner.

**Q.** Why does the Janata Government blame you?

**A.** We have good relations with the Janata government. As a result, the CBI and a particular foreign government have become discouraged. And the recent acts of violence within the country and outside are a direct result of their joint conspiracy.

**Q.** Why is the CBI interested in spoiling the good relations of *Ananda Marga* and the Janata Government?

**A.** The bureaucracy in the CBI believes that if the Janata government, due to its inimical relations with *Ananda Marga*, instructs a thorough inquiry into the activities of the CBI against *Ananda Marga* during the erstwhile congress rule; several top brass officers will be exposed. Hence, they are very busy trying their best to see that our relations are spoiled at any cost.

Mr. *Ganguli's* article further stated, "It seems that the jail administration in *Bihar* state is still linked with the British rule. My requests for personal interviews were turned down. When I wrote an application to the jail superintendent, he said, 'We cannot allow you to interview Shrii Sarkar, as He is a prisoner here.' But upon being reminded of the fact that he had allowed an Australian correspondent to interview Shri Sarkar last July, he altered himself and said, 'See, we certainly have to give some extra concessions to the foreigners.' Then I met a Margii devotee who was to meet Shrii Sarkar today (Oct 16) requesting him to bring replies from *Anandamurtijii* to my questions. In the meantime, I received His acceptance for this sort of special interview, through another Margii follower. Fortunately, indeed, Shrii Sarkar had just broken His vow of silence after a gap of three years the entire Margii follower could listen to him replying to my questions.

Shrii *Sarkar* has been kept in the Bankipur Central jail situated in the midst of *Patna*. He has become very weak due to prolonged fasting. At the time of the arrest, He weighed 67 kg, which has reduced to 46 kg now. Yet the most astounding surprise is that even such painful hardships have not been able to weaken His thoughts, even at this age of 56. Only physically He has grown too weak.

Referring to my questions related to the incidents of violence, Shrii Sarkar answered that the fundamental ideals of the Marga are aimed at restricting humans involved in violence. It encourages and promotes proper utilization of human energy through service oriented activities. That is why the Marga is established and operating schools, hospitals and rehabilitation centers for orphan children and the aged. 'Our mission's philosophy is self-realization and service to humanity. There is not even the slightest scope for violence.' How can there be self-contradiction between the ideals and activities of the mission? Speaking about the well known *Purulia* incident of 1967, the propounder of Ananda Marga said, 'Five of our *Avadhutas* were brutally killed in the attack on the mission headquarters, but none of the attackers were injured. Had violence been our belief, the other side should also have suffered some injuries.'

Regarding the recent news of violence in Australia, He said that these are said to be carried out by an institution called by the name, Universal Proutists Revolutionary Federation. Shri Sarkar claimed that *Ananda Marga* has no links with this so called UPRF.

As proof to His claim, Shrii Sarkar further added that at the time of proclamation of emergency rule, Ananda Marga and all its allied organizations had been banned. UPRF did not appear in the list. 'When the government herself does not consider UPRF to be an accessory to Ananda Marga, then it is not proper for her to accuse Ananda Marga for the activities of UPRF,' He said.

Shri Sarkar thinks He was not given an impartial trial. He says, the trial was conducted during the emergency period when the judiciary was restricted. Moreover, the entire case stands on the confession of only one single witness, *Madhavananda*, who has turned an approver. Shri Sarkar advised that it would do no damage or harm if a new inquiry was conducted and the case was tried again after that. Concluding, He said, 'It would increase the honor and prestige of the Janata Government.'

Shrii Sarkar founded *Ananda Marga* in 1955. In India alone, the Marga has an approximate four million followers. A spokesman said there were two thousand whole timer workers. The Marga followers are in the more than eighty countries of the world."

Interview with India Today (India Today is the leading weekly magazine published from New Delhi).

Singing and dancing to the tune of 'Baba Nam Kevalam' groups of saffron-clad *Sannyasis* marched through the streets of Patna on July 4, symbolizing that they are back on the road of bliss. Millions of *Ananda Margiis* the world over, at the same time, were celebrating the day as the victory of Dharma. The Patna High Court has acquitted Him of all charges of criminal conspiracy. Acquitted with Him were four of His trusted followers:

*Acarya Sarveshvarananda* Avt, the general secretary of Marga, *Acarya Satyananda Avadhuta*, the senior monk (*Avadhut*), *Barun Kumar Mukharji* and *Pavitra Kumar Roy*. The verdict of the court, setting at liberty *Anandamurtiji*, after He had spent seven years in jail, has come exactly on the day when the entire organization, with all its front organizations, was outlawed by the government in 1975. The court was satisfied that the prosecution (CBI) had failed to prove the charges against *Anandamurtii* and others. Prabhat Rainjan Sarkar alias *Anandamurtii*, 57, the spiritual head of *Ananda Marga* (the eternal path of bliss) – has always been a controversial figure. An ex-employee of the *Jamalpur* railway workshop, *Anandamurtii*, has been a mystery ever since He founded His socio-spiritual organization. While His critics maintain that *Ananda Marga* is nothing but a "pseudo-spiritual organization", His followers claim that it is a mission with twin objectives of *Atma Mokshartham* (liberation of self) and *Jagata Hitayaca* (service to humanity). To attain this liberation, the founder preceptor promulgated a scientific spiritual ideology of Tantra Yoga. And to achieve the ideals of spiritual realization *Anandamurtii* innovated and taught a psychological cult comprising a new system of the *Astaunga* path (eightfold path). The system makes the person elevate himself from physical consciousness to spiritual bliss. Hence, it is named *Ananda Marga*, says an ardent follower. After the organization was founded in 1954–55, it widened its horizons and now it has followers in more than 80 countries of the world.

*India Today* correspondent relentlessly pursued *Anandamurtiijii* for an interview, but His continued detention made it impossible.

Ultimately, *Anandamurtiijii's* private assistant Ac. *Ramananda* ji, who is a regular visitor to the jail, presented a set of questions to Anandamurtii. *Ramananda* ji reported back, "Baba never agrees to an interview but in your case, your questionnaire, though most critical, was unusually welcomed by Him." The following is *Anandamurtiijii's* reply to the questionnaire.

**Q.** How do you feel about the verdict of the High Court?
While millions of your followers the world over celebrated the days as "Victory of Dharma" you did not express any reaction and one of your followers explained that since you are Nirbhikar (passionless) you received this news coolly. Is it a fact you never react to anything, and if so why?

**A.** Victory of *Dharma* is a natural phenomenon. Everything natural should be accepted with normal coolness.

**Q.** Your followers allege that you and your organization have been victims of a calculated malicious propaganda by a certain agency aimed at destroying your organization. Much damage has been caused to your organization during the past seven years, during which you were in jail. Now, if you are acquitted of all charges, how do you plan to remove this misunderstanding from the public mind to re-establish your organization?

**A.** We will be doing our humanitarian duties with a subjective approach and objective adjustment.

**Q.** Much confusion was created in the past because of lack of communication between you and the general public. Do you now think it is proper to have public contact so that the confusion does not persist? Why have you lived a secluded life in the past?

**A.** There is no necessity of my coming into public contact because the actual social service is rendered by the workers of the organization. I simply assist the workers.

**Q.** The speed with which *Ananda Marga* has spread in the west and attracted a large number of people has created misgivings that it is being supported or sponsored by some foreign agencies.

Moreover, your attack on the KGB and CBI in the past without mentioning the CIA has added to the confusion. What have you to say about this allegation?

**A.** I do not know if the CIA did any harm to *Ananda Marga*. If the same is brought to my notice with sufficient proof, I will certainly condemn them. I have got no weakness for any organization. Good people should always be supported and bad condemned in unambiguous language.

**Q.** It was alleged that you wanted to capture political power and establish *Sadvipra Raj* in the world. No doubt this was refuted in the court but at the same time the books referred to in the court during the trial confirmed this view in which you have said, "Violence is the essence of life." How do you explain the fact?

**A.** What is said in the court is final. The word I use is "Sadvipra *Samaj* and not Sadvipra *Raj*". "*Raj*" is a microscopic fraction of *Samaj*. I have already clarified my view regarding violence. It is not always *Himsa*. My interpretations of violence can co-exist *Ahimsa*.

**Q.** Do you have any political ambition? Do you feel that unless corruption was removed, society would not be of your liking? And how do you want to establish that kind of society? Unless you take active part in politics, is it possible?

**A.** I have got no political ambition. I have propounded a theory of PROUT for the welfare of human society. Those who need it will materialize this theory.

**Q.** What is your opinion about democracy?

**A.** Democracy can only be useful where the following essential factors are present at least amongst 51 percent of the voters. Morality, literacy, and socio-political economic consciousness. Otherwise, it is an instrument before the public.

### Interview with Hindi news agency

A special correspondence of the leading Hindi News Agency, *Samachar Bharati*, too sent written questions to Shrii Sarkar. His replies reflects the clear views on various contemporary and continual problems. The questionnaire was sent to Shrii Sarkar on the 4th August 1978. His replies are put forth in the closer English rendering here under:

**Q.** Perhaps only two days are left when humanity will observe peace day as a mark of protest against the human genocide caused by the explosion of the first atom bomb on Japan. Would you kindly give a message in our national language for that occasion through "*Bharati*"? So far as I have understood, you believe that even after explosion of the atom bombs, the elements of humanity and those of nature's creation will continue to exist. In this context, your opinion can again be expressed through the medium of "*Bharati*".

**A.** Humanity is more powerful compared to the atom or nuclear bombs. Hence humanity need not fear these bombs. Humanity faces no danger from bombs.

**Q.** Even today, elements of crime violence are present in people's mentality. What you elucidate some ways to eradicate them?

**A.** I wish to awaken a mind of universal affinity in the consciousness of the human beings of the universe.

**Q.** We see a nationwide ongoing debate on the issue of bringing about "radical changes" and other required ratification in the education policy. Don't you, as an exalted "spiritual personality", feel it essential to introduce and include 'grand integration of all religious spirituality as well as universal humanistic morality", in Education?

**A.** I have already explained my education policy.

**Q.** Most of the time you deliver most of your 'Discourses' in Hindi, the national language. These days, once again the language issue is taking prominence. If you find it desirable, you may give your amicable advice in order to establish Hindi as a universally acceptable "link language' through our own 'news agency of Indian language' please!

**A.** I consider each single language as a language of God. Hence each one would receive equal regards from me.

### Interview with *Dinaman* Magazine

#### "WE HAVE NO ENEMIES"

*Ananda marga's* founder, *Anandamurtii* (whose real name is Prabhat *Rainjan Sarkar*), is quoted to have said in Calcutta, when asked to comment about the recent attacks on *Ananda margiis* on April 30 when 26 *Ananda Margiis* were killed (government statistics record the death toll at 17) that "I don't consider either the communist party of India - Marxist CPI (M), or congress to be my enemy". A correspondent had handed *Anandamurtii* a written set of questions at the latter's residence in southern Calcutta, *Lake Garden* area, to which written answers were provided.

*Anandamurtii* was asked whether He was holding the CPI (M) responsible for the recent attack on Ananda *Margiis* which had resulted in the deaths. Additionally, Congress (I) had been accused in the past as being anti-*Ananda* Marga. Why did this attack take place, who perpetrated it and what really is the reason for people being against *Ananda* Marga?

In reply to the above, *Anandamurtii* said, "I have not been to *Tiljala* (which is where *Ananda* Marga headquarters are, and near which the death incident took place) for the past one year, and additionally, I have been unwell from August 4. As a result I believe the workers residing at the property there - those that were physically present at the scene, can only provide the correct answer to the question in regard to the specific incident.

**Q.** However, why the opposition to *Ananda* Marga and why the attacks?

**A.** *Ananda Marga Dharma*, philosophy, meditation, humanitarian sentiments, society consciousness, respect and determination are all directed towards progress. As a result, certain jealous and greedy people are regarding *Ananda* Marga organization as an enemy - it is most unfortunate. Ananda Marga does not consider anybody as an enemy. Despite a number of obstacles being put in its way, it continues to be of service to humanity the world over and will continue to do so in the future as well.

*The End*
